# Train — Feature Documentation

> The strength + conditioning area (plan, execute, log, analyze). **Status: ✅ done (FE mock + FE real + Spring Boot backend)** — the only gaps are clearly-flagged Phase-3 AI/analysis features. Lives under the **`Edzés`** tab — one of Design 2.0's five first-class tabs — whose route `/train` is now a **Mozaik hub page** (`pages/EdzesHubPage.tsx`): one hero + six tiles, each tile navigating to a **full-page sibling** on its own stable route. The nine-tab `SubNavDropdown` shell and its `tabs.ts` are gone ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md), [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)).

---

## 1. Summary

Train is the largest mezo domain: the area for planning and executing strength work and conditioning. It covers periodized **mesocycles** (`Mesociklus`), the active week's **gym split**, full-screen **workout execution** with per-set logging, an **exercise catalog + per-exercise records** explorer, **sport** (`Röplabda` — volleyball, cross, and TRX, 0..n scheduled slots per day, `mezo-05v6`) scheduling/logging, and structured **interval running** (`Futás`) plans. A `Mai` page surfaces all three rhythms (gym + sport + running) for **one selected day at a time** via a `DayStrip` navigator (default: today; `mezo-9bbc`), with the detailed Mon–Sun agenda on the `Heti` page.

**Mozaik 2.0 is the Nth render layer over the same unchanged model.** The Design 2.0 F2 wave (`mezo-d20.3.1`…`.3.9`) dissolved the Train section shell and re-faced most of these pages onto the tile-mosaic vocabulary — but it was *recompose, not reinvent*: **every data hook, mutation, contract and honest-state rule below survived it verbatim**. `useTrain`/`useRunning`, the workout-session model (`logic/workoutState.ts`), the recommendation/progression engines, the medal engine, the meso template/run split and the whole backend slice are exactly what they were before the redesign. What changed is the surface: the nine sub-tabs became one hub + six tiles, the sub-tabs became full-page siblings, emoji became clay SVG sprites, and the entrance choreography became a one-shot rise-stagger.

**Status per layer — all ✅ done:**

- **FE mock** (Phase 1): synchronous static fixtures, every tab renders without a backend.
- **FE real** (Phase 2): each read/write swapped to `/api/train/*` behind the same hooks; no signature change.
- **Backend** (Phase 2): full `feature/train/**` Spring Boot slice over Postgres — mesocycles, workout execution, sport, exercise catalog/records, running.

**Phase-3-planned (🟣, flagged throughout, do NOT assume live):** `niggleWarning`, the live **cross-load → volume recompute engine** (Sport AND Running cross-load are still static; since `mezo-ly27` the **static `sportMuscleLoad.ts` heuristic is its FE interim** on Heti's `MuscleWeekSheet` — this is a DIFFERENT thing from the weekly performance ramp below, which IS live), `shoulderLoadTrend`, sport `jumpCount`/`intensity`/`team`/`season` capture, gym-schedule **`duration`** (the gym-time **schedule is now live** — see §4 `GymScheduleSlot`; only per-day duration stays out of scope), copy-week, and the active-workout **Voice** tool (still no UI at all, Phase 3). **Progressive Overload Plan 2 SHIPPED** (design spec `2026-07-25-progressive-overload-design.md`, above; PR #80 + the Phase B arc surface, `mezo-hi9m`): the performance-driven **weekly volume ramp** is a real engine now — `VolumeProgressionService.rolloverIfDue` writes `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity.currentSets` + a `volumeRecompute` audit live, per calendar week (toward MRV only for a muscle explicitly marked **Emphasize**; **MAV** for the default **Grow** tier since `mezo-3m5m` — §4 `#### Volume progression`) — and the read-only **`Mezociklus áttekintő`** volume-arc surface + the Mai entry card + Heti's `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip are live too, §2/§4. **Progressive Overload Plan 3 SHIPPED too** (`mezo-gj42`) — completing the progressive-overload checklist alongside Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`, the RIR-aware, deload-aware intensity engine + the in-workout `ProgressionBanner`/prep-chip signal): a deterministic (non-LLM) daily **`overload` challenge** surfaces the day's biggest recommended jump as a proactive workout-challenge card — a guaranteed +1 alongside the existing LLM-generated PR/Depth/Volume ones, §2. Mechanism/evaluator/display live in [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md). **The cross-load → volume engine is a separate, still-Phase-3 gap** — the weekly ramp above reacts to logged performance (reps/RIR) within Train itself, NOT to Sport/Running load; do not conflate the two. Also worth knowing: the mock's rich `volumePerMuscle`/`volumeRecompute` **prose** provenance is still fuller than what the real engine writes — the engine's `volumeRecompute.changes[]` is a terse per-muscle change list (`"+2 sets"`/`"tartás"`/`"deload"` + a one-line HU reason), not narrative. **The old inert `90s` timer chip is gone, not deferred** — Napiv S5 (`mezo-8141`) replaced it with a real rest countdown (since `mezo-xt65` an **in-card CTA-morph bar**, §2), whose 150s/90s duration is a **hardcoded** per-exercise-type heuristic (`restTimer.ts`), not a data field; a configurable per-exercise rest duration would need a new backend field (Phase 2 backlog candidate, not Phase 3).

**Driving design docs (read before changing this area):**
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-10-phase2-backend-design.md` — Phase-2 slice map.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md` — the "T0 clean slate" write/ghost rule.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-exercise-catalog-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-12-exercise-records-design.md`.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-14-train-running-slice-design.md` — the `Futás` slice.
- `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-progressive-overload-design.md` — Progressive Overload master design (mesocycle-aware intensity + volume + daily-challenge tie-in). **Plan 1 (RIR-aware intensity engine + in-workout signal, `mezo-5pfe`) and Plan 2 (weekly volume ramp — Phase A `VolumeProgressionService` PR #80 + Phase B `Mezociklus áttekintő` volume-arc surface, both `mezo-hi9m`; the ramp's ceiling became tier-resolved rather than always-MRV in `mezo-3m5m`) have shipped** — see §2/§4 below. **Only Plan 3** (the daily-challenge overload tie-in, `mezo-gj42`) is still design-only.

---

## 2. User-facing behavior

### The Edzés hub (`pages/EdzesHubPage.tsx`, `/train`, `mezo-d20.3.1`)

**The Train section shell is gone.** There is no `TrainSection`, no `AppHero`, no `SubNavDropdown`, no `TRAIN_TABS`/`tabs.ts` — the whole nine-tab shell was dissolved by the Design 2.0 IA decision ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md)), the same way the Mezo (`mezo-d20.5.1`) and Én (`mezo-d20.6.1`) shells were. `/train` renders the hub directly under the app's five-tab bar; the app-wide quick-log FAB floats over it like every other screen.

The hub itself renders no header — since `mezo-atry` that is the app shell's single `AppHeader` (`frontend/src/app/AppHeader.tsx`, mounted by `AppLayout` above every tab-root, [today.md](today.md#the-header-is-the-shells-not-the-hubs)): date eyebrow · daypart switch (always jumps to `/nap`) · Mezo-messages circle → `/nap/uzenetek` · notification bell (unread badge, 3-item popover, `Összes értesítés ›` → `/me/ertesitesek`) · profile orb → `/me`. Before `mezo-atry` this hub, like the other four, carried its own copy of that recipe. Below the shell header, the hub is a **Mozaik page** ([ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)) with two bands of its own, both inside one `EntranceGroup` (the one-shot rise-stagger; after it plays, the page is still):

1. **Today's-session hero** — `ClayIcon`/`ClaySpot` + eyebrow (`MA · {idő} · Meso W{n}/{N}`) + session title + the companion coach line + **exactly one primary CTA**. Its honest states are `TrainTodayPage`'s verbatim, and nothing self-completes (ADR 0010): **gym** — a completed instance wins (`Kész · {n} szett` → the review), else an open one resumes (`Folytassuk → · {n} szett kész`), else the fresh `Indítsuk →`; **sport/run** — a logged session renders its logged summary, an unlogged one only ever *opens* the shared `SportLogSheet`/`RunLogSheet`; **rest day** — says so and keeps the `＋ Saját edzés` escape hatch; **no active meso** — the whole hero ghosts with Mai's copy and the `+ Tervezz mesociklust` wizard CTA. The coach line is `workout.niggleWarning` gated on the mock-seeded AI scenario — in real mode it simply vanishes rather than showing a placeholder.
2. **The six-tile mosaic** — `Heti` (a bespoke 7-dot week strip instead of a clay spot: the weekday scaffolding is a calendar fact, only the fill states carry data) → `/train/week`; `Mesociklus` → `/train/mesocycles`; `Sport` → `/train/sport`; `Futás` → `/train/futas`; `Gyakorlatok` → `/train/exercises`; `Medálok` (the `s-medal` clay spot) → `/train/medals`. **Each tile's bottom line comes from that page's OWN hook**, and a line vanishes entirely while its source has nothing to say — no fabricated counts.

**Routes — full-page siblings, no nested shells.** `/train/mai` (`TrainTodayPage`, the whole day view), `/train/week` (`TrainWeekPage`), `/train/gym` (a thin alias, below), `/train/sport`, `/train/futas`, `/train/exercises`, `/train/medals`, `/train/mesocycles` (runs library), `/train/templates` (`Sablonok`). The **Heti drill-in contract survives**: `/train?day={0..6}` is forwarded by `TrainIndex` (`app/router.tsx`) to `/train/mai?day={n}` with the selection intact, because the day view — not the hub — is what a day deep-link means. Full-screen routes (never any tab chrome): `/train/session`, `/train/review/:workoutId`, `/train/mesocycles/new`, `/train/mesocycles/:id` (the run page), `/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day` (mesocycle pages v2 Task 3), `/train/mesocycles/:id/week` and `/train/mesocycles/:id/week/:muscle` (Task 4), `/train/mesocycles/:id/overview` (redirects to `.../week`), `/train/mesocycles/:id/report`, `/train/mesocycles/compare`, `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id`, `/train/futas/:id`, and `/train/custom/{new,:id}`.

**`GymPage` is a thin alias, deliberately kept** (`pages/GymPage.tsx`, `mezo-d20.3.2` + `mezo-d20.9.1`). Gym's content folded into `Heti` (below); the file survives as `export function GymPage() { return <TrainWeekPage /> }` — rendering `TrainWeekPage` directly, **not** a client `navigate`, so the URL is untouched while the content is unified. It exists because `/train/gym` is still a **live navigation target** for three real callers — `MesoStartSheet`'s post-start redirect, `MesocyclePlannerPage`'s post-create redirect, and `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`'s `useBackNav` fallback — plus any PWA bookmark. Removing the alias means rewriting those three call sites, which the F8 cleanup deliberately left alone.

**Page faces are not uniform, and that is honest.** The F2 wave re-faced the hub, `Heti`, `Medálok`, `Gyakorlatok`, the `Mezociklus` hub + its three full-screen siblings, and the whole session flow onto the Mozaik vocabulary (`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`/`StatStrip`/`Tile`/`Mosaic`/`CollapsibleStrip` from `@/shared/ui/mozaik`, clay sprites from `@/shared/ui/clay`, the `--mz-*` tokens in `styles/prototype.css`). `Mai` (`TrainTodayPage`), `Sport`, `Futás`, `Sablonok`, the planner/builders and the template editor still wear their pre-redesign DS `.page-header`/`.pghead-np` faces — Sport and Futás got **load-bearing behavior fixes** in their own slices (`mezo-d20.3.4`/`.3.5`, below) rather than a re-face. Nothing about their data or contracts differs; only the chrome is older.

### `Mai` — the one-day view (`pages/TrainTodayPage.tsx`, `/train/mai`)
> **Route moved, page unchanged (`mezo-d20.3.1`).** Mai used to BE `/train`; the hub took that path and Mai moved to `/train/mai` with its whole day view (`DayStrip`, per-day retroactive logging, the morning-training card) intact. The hub's hero is a one-session *summary* of the same logic — the depth below still lives only here. Mai keeps its pre-redesign DS face.

**A one-day page over a `DayStrip` navigator (`mezo-9bbc`), replacing the old ~2.5-viewport weekly scroll (re-measured at exactly 1.00 viewports — 956px scroll height at 440×956 — via the visual-suite's own mock fixture/clock-pinning; this is a snapshot of one fixture state, not a hard ceiling, so treat "≈1 viewport" as the design target rather than a universal guarantee).** `.page-header` (`Edzés · {shownDay} · W{n}`, or just `Edzés · W{n}` with no resolvable day, as a DS `Eyebrow`) + `PageTitle` (`Mai nap` while today is shown, else the shown day's HU label, with a `← Ma` `.pgact` action back to today) — **DS-migrated in `mezo-setx.6.2`** → the horizontal **`DayStrip`** (`components/DayStrip.tsx` — `role="tablist"`, one `.daychip` `role="tab"` per weekday: `MA`/day-number + per-session `SessionTone`-coloured dots + a done-count/`pihenő` line, pre-derived by the pure `logic/dayStripItems.ts`) → the Mezociklus áttekintő entry row (unconditional; since `mezo-setx.6.2` the DS canonical nav row — `.mesorow`, 56px, body-size label, trailing chevron, replacing a 13px line with a bare `→`) → the **shown day's** hero card(s) in time-of-day order — a coral `.trainhero` gym hero **only while today is shown** (the unchanged three-state gate below), or a read-only-capable `.todaycard` gym/sport/run card on any other day (`components/TodaySessionCard.tsx`, **one card per scheduled sport slot**), plus — on any day, today included — one permanently-logged gym-tone `.todaycard` per **completed custom (saját) instance** of that day (`SAJÁT · MEGVAN` tag, no state chip, `DoneBar` → `/train/review/{id}`; `mezo-9bbc` final review, so a day whose only session was a saját workout is no longer mis-rendered as a rest day) — → a **today-only open-instance resume card**, gated to a day with no gym schedule slot (a started-but-unfinished custom/meso-less workout would otherwise have no resume affordance anywhere on Mai, since the gym hero above only covers a scheduled gym day — `mezo-ws2x` Finding 4; `todaySession.openWorkout` + `workout` from `/today`'s open-wins resolution) → a rest-day card when nothing is scheduled on the shown day **and** no such open instance is showing (today's copy offers "+ Saját edzés"; a non-today rest day is copy-only, "Ezen a napon nincs tervezett edzés.", no CTA; the two cards are mutually exclusive by construction) → a **today-only dashed `+ Saját edzés` footer**, rendered exactly when the rest-day card is **not** (that card carries its own copy of the CTA, so exactly one entry shows; the `mezo-9bbc` rework had dropped the unconditional entry, leaving a day WITH scheduled sessions no way to assemble a one-off workout — restored by `mezo-eahv`; non-today selections stay read-only, no footer) → the **today-only** morning-training nudge. **The weekly list (`WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow` cards), the `LoadTiles` weekly-load summary, and the provenance note moved to the sibling `Heti` page** (`/train/week`, see the subsection right below) — Mai renders none of the three any more; nothing was dropped, only relocated. `TrainTodaySkeleton` mirrors **this** shape (page-head → a 7-chip `.daychip`-geometry `DayStrip` placeholder → the gym-hero block), not the weekly timeline that moved to Heti, so the loading→content swap does not reflow (`mezo-9bbc` final review). Mai's no-meso ghost likewise no longer promises a „Heti terv” section — only the hero ghost + the dashed "+ Saját edzés" row remain.

**Selected-day state — the URL is the single source of truth (`mezo-9bbc`).** `selectedDay: string | null` (`null` ⇒ today) is **derived from `?day={0..6}` on every render** (a `DAY_ORDER` index — the Heti drill-in contract, below), never mirrored into component state; a `DayStrip` tap and the `← Ma` chip both write it back via `setSearchParams(…, { replace: true })` (day-hopping inside Mai is a view switch, not a history step the back button should unwind chip by chip). Deriving rather than seeding is what keeps URL and view in sync in **both** directions: a reload or a back step keeps the day you were on, and any navigation that drops the query **without remounting the route element** snaps the page back to today. Hostile input still resolves to today: `null`/`''` skip `Number()` entirely (`Number(null) === Number('') === 0` would silently pin every plain `/train/mai` visit to Monday), and non-integer/out-of-range values are rejected; an unparseable value is left in the URL untouched. `shownDay = selectedDay ? agenda.find(day===selectedDay) ?? todayRow : todayRow`. 

**Which day is "today" — one date-based derivation (`mezo-9bbc` final review).** `todayRow = agenda.find(isToday) ?? agenda.find(date === localDateString())`, and `todayIso = todayRow?.date ?? localDateString()` feeds **all** of the page's date math (`sessionState`, `loggedSportOn`, the retroactive `date` threading). The **flag wins** because mock mode's "today" is a fixture flag pinned to `Csü` (`data/train/train.ts`) while the clock is whatever day it really is — reading the clock for the date math while the agenda's today came from the flag made every card on that row compute against the wrong calendar day (on any real weekday but Thursday the mock run card merged in by `todayRuns` read `TERVEZETT`/`ELMARADT` and lost its log CTA; the frozen visual clock, a Thursday, was the one day this was invisible). The **calendar fallback** covers real mode, where `deriveGymSchedule`/`toSportSchedule` only stamp `isToday` on a day that also has a gym/sport slot (§9): a true rest day or a run-only day flags no row at all, and without the fallback today's own agenda row — its rest copy, its completed custom instances — was simply missing. `isTodayShown` is date-based for the same reason, `selectedDay === null || (shownDay?.date ?? todayIso) === todayIso`; the old flag test rendered today as "not today" whenever you tapped today's **own** chip on a rest/run-only day (or drilled in from Heti's today row), which showed the weekday instead of „Mai nap”, offered a pointless `← Ma`, suppressed the morning nudge, and — functionally — dropped both the `+ Saját edzés` CTA and the in-progress custom-workout resume card, leaving no way to start or resume a custom workout from Mai. Switching days is a pure client-side re-render over the already-fetched week agenda — **no refetch**. `todayRuns` (the running hero's date-based prescribed sessions, `runSessionsForDay(activeRunningBlock, todayIdx())`) only merges into the shown day while today itself is shown; a non-today selection uses the agenda's own day-of-week-matched `running` list instead — the mock fixture's `isToday` flag (pinned to Csü) and the real calendar date are intentionally two different clocks (§9).

**Session-card anatomy (`mezo-lruy`).** All three modalities share one rhythm — tag → time → title → fact pills → full-width CTA — so gym, sport and running read as one family instead of three bespoke layouts: the gym `.trainhero` keeps its coral gradient — **re-tokenized onto `.todaycard`'s chrome in `mezo-setx.6.2`** (same gradient formula, `--r-2xl`, `--sp-4`, `--shadow-sm`, token border; h2 24/700, eyebrow 12/700/0.22em, fact pills on the shared `.metapill`) so the gym hero and its siblings finally read as one card family, while its bespoke COMPOSITION — and with it the done/in-progress/fresh gating — stays untouched; **`TodaySessionCard`** (`.todaycard`, `.todaycard-{tone}` modifier, `logged` state class — **since `mezo-jyua` a thin wrapper over the shared `shared/ui/ItemCard`**, which now emits this whole family for Today as well; the markup and every class name are unchanged, and the `train-*` visual goldens passing untouched are the proof the extraction was pixel-neutral, see [`_platform-design-system.md` §3](_platform-design-system.md)) is its lighter surface-card sibling for the recurring/prescribed sessions AND, since `mezo-9bbc`, for a **non-today gym day** too (`ctaLabel` omitted ⇒ read-only, e.g. a day with no `gymDayTarget`) — a `.todaycard-top` row opens with a 44px `.todaycard-icon` shield (the modality emoji, swapped for a ✓ check glyph once `.logged`) beside a `.todaycard-head` block: a `.typetag-{tone}` tag (**five tones** — `gym`/`sport`/`cross`/`trx`/`run`, `SessionTone` in `logic/sportKinds.ts`; appends `· MEGVAN` once logged) + `.todaycard-time` (hidden once logged) + a display `.todaycard-title`; while still unlogged, a trailing `.todaycard-state` chip carries one of exactly **four** `sessionState` labels (`MOST`/`MA`/`ELMARADT`/`TERVEZETT`, `logic/sessionState.ts`'s `SESSION_STATE_LABEL`) — **a logged card shows none of them**, its eyebrow reading only `{tag} · MEGVAN` instead. Below the head: the slot's facts as `.metapill`s (sport: `perc` · role · location; running: RPE target · rounds; non-today gym: exercise count · duration), then a full-width `.todaycard-cta` (log / start) — or, once logged, the shared `DoneBar` in place of the pills+CTA entirely — accent-tinted per modality through the `--tc-accent`/`--tc-wash` custom-property pair. **On Heti**, each session is its own padded `.s` block inside a `WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow` day card: a `.dayrow-head` label row (day + `MA` marker + chevron) over `.s-top` (type tag + `.s-title` + the `kész`/`log`/`folyamatban` chip) and `.s-meta` fact pills.

**Convergence, ordering & done-state.** Where gym, sport (volleyball/cross/TRX), and running converge. Both **Mai's hero cards** (for the shown day) and **Heti's weekly rows** (`WeeklyDayRow`) order **by time-of-day** rather than a fixed gym→sport→running modality order — a morning run renders above an evening gym; untimed sessions sort last. Both surfaces order through the same pure helper `daySessions(day)` (`features/train/logic/agenda.ts`), which flattens a day's `{gym, sport[], running[], custom[]}` — `WeeklyAgendaDay.sport: VolleyballSession[]` (`components/WeeklyDayRow.tsx`) holds **0..n slots per day**, each carrying its own `sport: volleyball|cross|trx` (resolved via `sportOf(slot)`, `logic/sportKinds.ts`) — into typed `AgendaItem`s carrying `timeOfDay` and sorts ascending (null/`''` → `'99:99'` last, then stable by modality). The union's **fourth member, `kind: 'custom'`** (a completed saját instance; untimed, so it always sorts last) was added in the `mezo-9bbc` final review so Mai's one-day view stops losing an already-shipped session type: it drives Mai's `SAJÁT · MEGVAN` card and a gym-tone `DayStrip` dot (`toneOf` maps `custom → 'gym'`, and Mai's `isDone` predicate returns `true` for it by construction). **`WeeklyDayRow` filters the member back out** (`daySessions(agenda).filter(kind !== 'custom')`) because it already renders completed saját instances in its own trailing block — that path is untouched. The Mon–Sun agenda itself is built once by the shared pure `buildWeekAgenda` (`features/train/logic/weekAgenda.ts`, lifted out of `TrainTodayPage` unchanged, `mezo-9bbc`) and consumed by both `TrainTodayPage` and `TrainWeekPage`. Gym time comes from the standalone gym schedule (§4 `GymScheduleSlot`); running time from each session's `timeOfDay`. On **Mai** you can log a sport (volleyball/cross/TRX) or running session inline via the shared `SportLogSheet`/`RunLogSheet` — since Task 9 (`mezo-9bbc`) this works on **today or any past day** of the shown week, not just today: each card's CTA is driven by the same three-way `sessionState` read as its `stateLabel` (above) — `planned` (a future day) ⇒ no CTA, read-only; `missed` (a past, unlogged day) ⇒ **`Pótold`**; `now`/`today` ⇒ the original copy (`Logold a session-t`/`Naplózd a futást`). Both sheets declare an optional `date?: string` prop, but **only `SportLogSheet`'s Mai wiring actually omits it on today** — `date={shownIso === todayIso ? undefined : shownIso}` — because `SportSessionCreateRequest.date` is optional and the server defaults to now when the body has no date (`sheets/SportLogSheet.tsx`); `RunLogSheet` (`sheets/RunLogSheet.tsx`) is wired to **always** receive `shownIso` from Mai (`date={shownIso}`, never omitted there) because its contract has no server-side default — `date` is resolved client-side regardless of whether the prop was passed, `date ?? localDateString()`, never the old UTC-shifting `new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)` (which mis-logged a late-evening/early-morning run onto the wrong day in CET). The omission semantics matter at other call sites too: `SportPage`'s own log CTA and `RunningPage`'s own log CTA both omit `date` entirely (today-only, no retroactive path there), relying on the same optional-prop/internal-default behavior each sheet already has — Mai is simply the one call site that supplies a non-today value. A logged past session's `DoneBar` stays tappable, re-opening the sheet against that same past date. **Mock mode honours the same `date`** (`data/train/trainHooks.ts` — `logSportMutation`'s mock branch appends `huMonthDayDow(req.date ?? localDateString())`, mirroring `runningHooks`' already-correct spread of the body): it used to hardcode today, so in the PWA demo a `Pótold` appended the session to **today** — the past day kept its `ELMARADT`/`Pótold` and today's card wrongly flipped to `MEGVAN` (`mezo-9bbc` final review). The sport CTA also **pre-selects the tapped slot's sport** (`SportLogSheet`'s `initialSport` prop, driven by `setSportLogSport(k)`); on **Heti**, tapping a non-gym session instead **drills into Mai with that day selected** (`/train?day={index}`, see below) rather than logging inline — Heti gained no retroactive-logging affordance of its own. **Once a session is logged, its hero/row flips to a `done` state — matched by date AND sport**, so a mixed day (e.g. a noon TRX slot + an evening volleyball match) flips each slot independently (`loggedSportOn(iso, k)`/`sportDoneOn`, day-parameterized since `mezo-9bbc` — the shown day's `iso`, not always today): the `.todaycard-state` label chip disappears and the type tag gains a `· MEGVAN` suffix instead, and the fact-pills + CTA are swapped out entirely for the shared `DoneBar`'s sage summary of the logged effort (volleyball: `RPE {n} · {n}p · váll {n}`; cross/TRX: `RPE {n} · {n}p` — no `váll`; running: `RPE {n}` (+ ` · {n} kör` when the round count is known); **gym**: a **three-state gate**, today only — see the gym-hero gating paragraph below). The `DayStrip` chips carry the same done-state, pre-computed per session by `dayStripItems(agenda, isDone)` into a `doneCount`/`dots` pair. Ghosts entirely when there is no active mesocycle ("+ Tervezz mesociklust") on **both** Mai and Heti — Mai's ghost still offers a dashed "+ Saját edzés" row below its own placeholder, since a custom workout needs no meso at all (`mezo-ws2x`, see the `Saját edzés` subsection below).

**Mezociklus áttekintő entry card (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m`).** Directly under the `DayStrip`, an active meso renders a `🗓 {shortTitle} · {phase} · W{n}/{N} →` entry card that opens `/train/mesocycles/:id/overview` — since mesocycle pages v2 (Task 4) that route is a redirect to `Heti vizsgálat` (`/week`, see §2's `Heti vizsgálat` subsection) — hidden entirely without an active mesocycle; unconditional on which day is shown.

**Morning-training nudge (`mezo-67rb`, `MorningTrainingCard`) — today only.** When a gym slot falls **after** the wake-derived window end, a gentle sage `.mtr` card offers moving the offending slot(s) into the morning window. The window is `morningWindow(useSleepGoal().goal.wakeTime)` = **[wake + 60′, wake + 6h]** (`features/train/logic/morningWindow.ts` — a Train-only circadian window; **since `mezo-u6jx`** it has no backend mirror, the habit engine's `training_done_today` tick is date-presence, see [habit.md](habit.md)); a slot *earlier* than the start is still morning training, so only `time > end` offends. „Áthelyezés a reggeli ablakba" is a **one-tap `saveGymSchedule` replace-all** (offenders land on the window start, the rest pass through; mock also `setQueryData(['train','gymSchedule'])` for parity), and „Maradjon így" **content-key-snoozes** it in `localStorage` (`mezo-morning-training-snooze`, keyed on wake + the offending slots so any wake/schedule change re-arms the card). Since `mezo-9bbc` the card only mounts while `isTodayShown` — selecting another day hides it. No red, no streak pressure — the ADR 0010 tone. See [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md) §3 for the `.mtr` family.

**Gym-hero done-day gating (`mezo-cd8s`) — today's three-state hero, unchanged by `mezo-9bbc`.** The gym hero is a **three-state gate** driven by today's instance, not by `weekDoneDates`, and only ever renders while today is shown: (1) **no instance** → the `Indítsuk →` start CTA (`openSession` → `/train/session`); (2) an **open** (started, unfinished) instance (`todaySession.openWorkout`) → a `● Folyamatban` eyebrow chip + a `Folytassuk → · {n} szett kész` resume CTA, `n` = the non-skipped set count; (3) a **completed** instance (`completedTodayWorkout` — since the cross-day start (`mezo-p7rp`) the resolved day's most recent finished instance **of the current Mon–Sun week**, not just today's) → a success-tinted `Kész · {n} szett — Megnézem →` button that opens the read-only review `/train/review/{completedTodayWorkout.id}` (`WorkoutReviewPage`, renders the shared `WorkoutSummary` in `closed` mode). Both `todaySession`/`completedTodayWorkout` come from `/today` and are **real-mode only** (null in mock → the hero is always `Indítsuk →`, byte-identical to Phase 1). **Since `mezo-p7rp` the `/today` day resolution is `open instance > ?day= param > weekday label`** (§4) and the session route accepts **`/train/session?day={templateDayId}`** (`ActiveWorkoutPage` threads `useSearchParams` → `useTrain({ workoutDay })`) — this is how a non-today direct-start (Heti weekly row / Gym day-card, `mezo-bxpg`) pins its template day; the open instance winning resolution means the hero's `Folytassuk` and a hard reload resume a cross-day session too. **A non-today gym day on Mai** (`mezo-9bbc`) is a plain read-only-capable `TodaySessionCard`, not the three-state hero: title/exercise-count come from the meso template (`activeMeso.days`, since `MesoDay` has no `title` — its human label is `type`) rather than `/today` (which only describes today), `logged` comes from `gymDoneDates`, and the CTA (`Kezdjük el`, omitted when `gymDayTarget` resolves to `null`, e.g. a rest template day) routes via the same `gymDayTarget` helper Heti uses. **On Heti**, a completed (`kész`) weekly gym row — today OR a past day this week — is **tappable → opens its review**: `WeeklyDayRow`'s gym branch routes its click to `onReviewGym` when `gymLogged`, else `onStartGym` when today, else `onOpenGymDay`; `TrainWeekPage` resolves the per-date instance id from `useWeekWorkouts()` (this Mon–Sun week's completed summaries mapped date→id, `workoutIdByDate`) and passes `onReviewGym` only for dates present in that map. Since `mezo-j3x0` (the p7rp D3 follow-up) the **non-today, not-done weekly gym rows are tappable too** — since the direct-start flow (`mezo-bxpg`) they navigate straight into `/train/session?day={templateDayId}` with **no intermediate sheet**, so a cross-day start is reachable from Heti; today's row still starts directly and done rows still open the review. Today's not-yet-completed gym row also shows a `folyamatban` chip while an instance is open (`gymInProgress = a.isToday && todaySession.openWorkout`). The `/train/session` route itself is **gated**: when today is completed with nothing open, `ActiveWorkoutPage` redirects to the review (`<Navigate to={/train/review/{id}} replace />`, after the pending/no-workout guards) so the prep screen (challenges included) is unreachable until next week; **since the direct-start flow (`mezo-bxpg`) the gym day-row tap handler (originally `GymPage`'s, now Heti's) resolves the same done/not-done state up front** (`weekWorkouts.find` by `templateSessionId`) and routes straight to `/train/review/{id}` instead of `/train/session` — no intermediate sheet mediates the choice any more (`GymDaySheet` is deleted).

**App-wide resume FAB (`mezo-78sd`).** Beyond the hero, an open instance also surfaces a shell-level coral resume FAB (done-set badge → `/train/session`) on every route via `app/FloatingReturnLayer.tsx` — plus a lavender chat bubble that stays available ON `/train/session` itself (the tab-bar-less screen), so a mid-workout Mezo chat is one tap out and one tap back. The layer, its `useOpenWorkout()` data hook and the route rules are documented in the [_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) integration table; nothing in the Train pages changed for it (the hook reuses `useTrain`'s param-less `['train','workoutToday', null]` key and the TrainTodayPage `completedWorkout` gate).

### `Heti` — the weekly agenda + the folded-in Gym view (`pages/TrainWeekPage.tsx`, `/train/week`)
**Mozaik 2.0 re-face + Gym fold-in (`mezo-d20.3.2`).** Heti is now a `MozaikPage tone="gold"`: `PageHead` (`‹ Edzés` back + two `.mz-pgact` chips — **`Időpontok`** → `GymScheduleSheet` and, active-meso-gated, **`Mezociklus áttekintő`** → `/train/mesocycles/{id}/overview`, which redirects to `Heti vizsgálat`) → `PageHero` (`i-edzes` clay icon + a `{done}/{sessionCount}` big number + `Heti edzések`, no subtitle) → `PageBody`: a 3-cell `StatStrip` (`szett terv` · `gym nap kész` · `medál e héten`) → the week's `WeeklyDayRow` day list → the `LoadTiles` weekly-load summary → the dashed `+ Saját edzés` CTA → an `Izom-zónák · e hét` panel wrapping `ZoneMiniGrid` (tap → `MuscleWeekSheet`) → the gym/sport provenance note. Without an active meso the page ghosts to the `+ Tervezz mesociklust` placeholder inside the same scaffold.

**What folded in from the old `Gym` tab.** The entire Gym meta card moved here: the `Időpontok` schedule chip, the `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip, the live zone rows (`weekZone.ts` → `ZoneMiniGrid`) and the `MuscleWeekSheet` detail. Its per-day `GymDayCard` list was **dropped as a true duplicate** — Heti's own agenda list already routes every gym day through the same `gymDayTarget` direct-start/review logic. `GymDayCard`, `GymStat` and `PhaseDots` were deleted outright in the F8 cleanup (`mezo-d20.9.1`) once the fold left them with zero references; `GymSkeleton` went with the fold. **Two deliberate drops, both honest-states calls:** the meta card's `Fázis`/`Split`/phase-dots readout (the prototype's Heti never repeats the active phase — that is Mesociklus's job, and the áttekintő chip still reaches it), and the prototype's `RPE átlag` stat (no cross-domain gym-RIR + sport-RPE weekly aggregate exists, so pixel-matching it would have meant fabricating a number).

Everything Mai's Napiv-era weekly scroll used to close with still lives here: the `LoadTiles` weekly-load summary (`logic/weeklyLoad.ts` + `components/LoadTiles.tsx`, one `.loadtile` per active modality, renders nothing on an empty week) and the `WeeklyDayRow` `.dayrow` day cards (one per weekday, one block per session — see the session-card-anatomy paragraph above). Built from the same `buildWeekAgenda` + `daySessions` helpers as Mai, so the two pages can never drift on ordering/done-state logic. A sport row's `.stag` (and its not-yet-logged `log-chip`) follow **`SPORT_TONE`** like every other surface — `stag-sport`/`stag-cross`/`stag-trx` — so a TRX day's row tag and its DayStrip dot finally agree; the tag was hardcoded to `stag-sport` until the `mezo-9bbc` final review, which also made `.stag-cross`/`.stag-trx` dead CSS. **A gym row's tap keeps the direct-start/review routing** (`onStartGym`/`onReviewGym`/`onOpenGymDay`, identical to Mai's pre-`mezo-9bbc` wiring, see the gym-hero gating paragraph above); **tapping any other session (sport/run) drills into Mai with that day pre-selected** — `toMai(day) = navigate('/train?day=' + DAY_ORDER.indexOf(day))` — since Heti itself doesn't log inline. That URL is deliberately still `/train?day=`: `TrainIndex` forwards it to `/train/mai?day=` (see the hub subsection), so the drill-in contract Mai consumes on mount never changed. Ghosts to the same "+ Tervezz mesociklust" placeholder as Mai when there's no active mesocycle.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.3`) — the layer under the Mozaik re-face.** The page head part is superseded (`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero` replaced `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow` in `mezo-d20.3.2`); everything the migration did to the *components* still stands, which is why the re-face could reuse them untouched. Head → `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow`; the section head drops `.secthead-np` for the **eyebrow + label-mono count** idiom the other migrated Train pages use (one section-head style across the domain, not two); the `Saját edzés` footer moves to the new shared **`.dashedcta`** (14/600, 48dp — it was a 10px uppercase strip at three call sites; Mai's two inline copies adopt the class when their own bead next touches them); the provenance note sits on `--primary-bg` at caption size. `LoadTiles` and `WeeklyDayRow` ride along (used only here): `.loadtile` takes DS card chrome + the StatStrip label voice, and `.dayrow` takes `--r-2xl`/`--sp-4`/`--shadow-sm`/token border, a 12/700/0.18em day label, **16/600 session titles**, a **48dp floor on every tappable session block**, and DS Tag stamps for `kész`/log chips. Legacy `--surface`/`--warm`/`--ink`/`--sub`/`--faint`/`--np-shadow-row` are gone from both. **One browser-only find:** the load-tile row holds free-form values (`2× · sprint`) and at DS type sizes three tiles can exceed a phone width by a few px — a nowrap value in a shrinking flex child is then *silently cut off*, so `.loadrow` scrolls (`overflow-x: auto`, tiles `flex: 1 0 auto`) exactly like `.pgactrow`, and the values sit at caption 14 rather than the 16 body floor.

### `Mesociklus` — the run library + hub hero (`pages/MesocycleLibraryPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles`)

**Mozaik re-face (`mezo-d20.3.6`).** The page carries a **4-tile mosaic** sitting above the active/planned/history sections — the tiles are the hub's real navigation, replacing the old `.mesorow` nav row: **`Heti vizsgálat`** (below, active-meso-gated), **`Sablonok`** → `MesoTemplatesPage`, **`Új blokk`** → the planner, and **`Történet`**, which has no route of its own (a dedicated history route is out of scope) and therefore **scrolls the already-rendered history section into view** rather than pretending to navigate. The `mezo-meyc.4` `Összevetés` selection mode inside `Történet` is unchanged.

**Hub hero, clickable (mesocycle pages v2 Task 2, `mezo-d20.15`).** `ActiveMesoCard` (`components/ActiveMesoCard.tsx`) is a whole-card `<button aria-label="Aktív mezociklus megnyitása">` — tapping it opens the run page (the `Builder` bullet below), so the active section is real navigation now, not chrome. Its face reads three pure derivations off `logic/mesoBands.ts`: **week dots** (`weekDots(meso)`, one tile per week toned `done|now|future|deload`), a **phase chip** (`phaseChip(meso)` — the current week's `phaseCurve` entry read as `Deload`/`Csúcs`/`Rámpa`), and up to five **band chips** (`runBands(meso).slice(0,5)` — a `RunBand` per muscle group with `current`/`ceiling`/`step`: `label current→ceiling ▲` while ramping, `label current · tart` on a maintain tier, bare `label current` at the ceiling, `★`-prefixed when the tier is emphasize), plus a `Ma · {day} · {type}` line keyed off `todayDayToken()` (`logic/mesoDates.ts`, a pure Monday-first weekday token matching `meso.days`). The **`Heti vizsgálat`** mosaic tile — the old `Volumen` tile's replacement, only rendered while a run is active — shows `W{n} · {Σ current} szett` and opens `/train/mesocycles/:id/week` (the subsection below); the mosaic's other three tiles are untouched.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.8`) — the layer under it.** `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow` head with a `.pgact` `+ Új`; the `ActiveMesoCard` hero takes `.todaycard`'s gradient formula and the primary rail (its coral was UI chrome, not data-viz); `MetaStat` becomes the **third** component on the shared StatStrip cell vocabulary, after `GymStat` (`.6.4`) and `SportStat` (`.6.5`). The planned/archived cards move to the DS type roles (18/600 and 16/600 titles, caption bodies, the notes line on `.text-meta-sm`) and their inline-sized `Chip`s drop the overrides. The `+ Új mesociklus tervezése` footer joined the shared `.dashedcta` (and **moved to the `Sablonok` tab as `+ Új sablon tervezése` in `mezo-tlwa`** — the library has no dashed footer any more). **Two carried-forward lessons:** `MetaStat` drops the ported `whiteSpace: nowrap` — a nowrap PHRASE in a three-across strip silently overflows at phone widths (the `.loadrow` finding, `.6.3`) — and its value sits at caption 14, not the strip's 20px numeral size, because `Pull / Push / Legs` broke over three lines at 20.
**Template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`) — the library is RUNS-ONLY since `mezo-tlwa`.** Sections in this order: the 4-tile mosaic (above; it carries the `Sablonok · {n}` entry that used to be a `.mesorow` nav row — the whole template section, its cards and its dashed CTA moved to the dedicated `Sablonok` page, next subsection) → **`Aktív`** hero → **`Tervezett`** → **`Történet`** (was `Archív` — the closed runs; each `ArchivedMesoCard` carries an `Újrafuttatás` **and**, since `mezo-tlwa`, a `Sablonná` action). Header chip "+ Új" opens the planner — with the dashed CTA gone it is this page's only creation entry. Run cards open the builder. **Both start paths still funnel into one sheet:** the `Sablonok` tab's `Indítás` opens `MesoStartSheet` on the template directly, `Újrafuttatás` first calls `rerun(meso.id)` (which materializes a template for a legacy run, backend-side) and opens the same sheet on the returned `templateId` — **the sheet wiring stays on the library page** precisely because of that second path, even though no template card lives here any more. `ArchivedMesoCard` is no longer a whole-card `<button>` (buttons cannot nest) — the body is the tappable region, the rerun chip sits in a footer row; **since `mezo-meyc.2` the body opens the run's frozen report** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/report`), not the builder — a closed run has no builder to open. **Napiv S4 (`mezo-8141`) gave this page, the planner, and the builder a `.pghead-np` page-head only** (over `Edzés · Mesociklusok`) — their body content still runs on `--coral`. The planner and builder **kept that face through Design 2.0**: `mezo-d20.3.7` audited the 5-step wizard against the prototype and found only one real fidelity gap worth fixing (below), not a re-face; deeper Builder/Planner restyling stays deferred, see §9.
**Történet selection + report state (`mezo-meyc.4`).** Each `ArchivedMesoCard` footer now states whether the run HAS a frozen report — a `riport` chip when `meso.hasReport`, a muted `nincs riport` label otherwise (a plain `<span>`, not a button: the card body already goes to the report, and a nested button would fight the selection mode). The card's eyebrow date reads the actual `closedAt` (formatted like every other date on the card), falling back to the plan's `endDate` only for a legacy run closed before `closedAt` existed. The section head gained an **„Összevetés" toggle chip** (`aria-pressed`, rendered only when there are ≥2 closed runs — nothing to compare otherwise) that flips the section into **selection mode**: a card tap now SELECTS the run instead of opening its report (the card becomes an `aria-pressed` toggle with a ✓/○ marker, full opacity + coral border when selected, and **both** footer actions — `Újrafuttatás` and the `mezo-tlwa` `Sablonná` — step aside so the only meaningful tap is the selection), max **two** runs, kept in **tap order** — which is exactly what makes the taps the `a`/`b` choice. With two picked, an „Összevetés megnyitása" `CtaGhost` navigates to `/train/mesocycles/compare?a={first}&b={second}`; below two, a `Válassz két lezárt futamot (n/2)` line stands in its place. Toggling the mode off clears the selection (a pick surviving an invisible mode would fire out of nowhere the next time it is switched on). Outside selection mode every existing behavior is untouched: tap → report, `Újrafuttatás` → rerun, `Sablonná` → fork (below).

**Run report + close flow (`mezo-meyc.2`, spec §2).** Closing a run is no longer a bare status flip: the builder's `Meso lezárása` button opens **`MesoCloseSheet`** (`sheets/MesoCloseSheet.tsx`, chrome mirrors `MesoStartSheet`) — a short "the report freezes at this moment" summary line + an optional „Saját értékelés" textarea → confirm → `closeMesocycle(id, selfEval?)` → on success navigates straight to the freshly-written **report page**; on failure the sheet stays open and retryable (the mutation-cache toast carries the message, §7a). `MesocycleBuilderPage` itself now redirects an **archived** run away on load — `<Navigate to=".../report" replace>` (`MesocycleBuilderPage.tsx`) — since a closed run's plan is history, not something to edit.
- **Report page** (`pages/MesoReportPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/report`) — the FROZEN end-of-mesocycle report, reached from a `Történet` card tap, `MesoCloseSheet`'s post-close redirect, and the builder's archived-run redirect above. Full-screen sibling route, **on the `MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero` scaffold since `mezo-d20.3.6`** (its volume section also took the prototype's name, `Heti szettek · a blokk íve`); the composition below is otherwise unchanged: back breadcrumb → header (title, `Lezárva · {date}` eyebrow, start–end date range + weeks, a `Sablon megnyitása` chip when `templateId` is set) → an **adherence** `StatStrip` (`Edzés {done}/{planned}`, `Hét {done}/{planned}`, `Teljesítés {pct}%`) → the **frozen volume arc** rendered by the shared **`MuscleArcSwitch`** (`components/MuscleArcSwitch.tsx`, lifted verbatim out of `MesoOverviewPage` — presentational + local pill-selection state over a `MuscleVolumeArc[]` prop, so a live arc and a close-time snapshot render through the exact same component and can never visually drift) → **strength** cards (per exercise: the `{firstTopKg} → {lastTopKg} kg · {reps} rep` movement line, a `{±kg}` LOAD-delta pill and a separate `{±%} e1RM` pill — each hidden individually at exactly 0, and a weightless exercise shows reps only) → **records** (medal count + top highlights) → an **`Életmód-kontextus` block** (`mezo-meyc.3`, rendered only when `report.context != null` — a planned/archived-but-uncomputed report shows nothing, not an empty shell) — a wrapping row of compact `Chip` pills over the totals (`😴`/`🍽`/`⚡`/`😰`/`⚖️`/`🏐`/`🏃`, each individually omitted when its metric is null — never shown as a fabricated 0; the kcal pill's "vs cél" is the mean of the weeks' own `kcalTargetAvg`, since `MesoContextTotals` carries none of its own) over a horizontally-scrolling (`overflow-x` on its own inner wrapper, DS wide-content rule) per-week table (`W`/`Alvás`/`Kcal`/`Energia`/`Stressz`/`Súly Δ`/`Sport`/`Futás`, null cells render `–`; the `Sport` cell's RPE sub-line is explicitly labelled **"Sport/futás RPE"**, not "gym" — `gymRpeAvg` is actually the sport+running RPE average despite its name) → an **`Ezt akartad`** block (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — rendered only when `meso.notes` is set (the wizard's freeform goal text, read back once the run is closed): an `Ezt akartad` eyebrow over the quote `„{meso.notes}"`, with an optional `— és ez lett: {meso.summary}` trailer when the report also carries a summary → an **`Izmonként · indulás → elért csúcs / plafon`** card — one row per muscle from `peakBands(arcs)`, each `{start} → {peak} / {ceiling}` → the owner's **read-only self-eval** (rendered only when non-empty) → an **AI section that does not render at all while `aiEvalEnabled` is false** (the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch; since S3 it is on by default, so the section renders and the `pending` poll below is live). Three AI states inside the card: `pending` ⇒ a `Spinner` + "Az értékelés készül…"; `ready` (with a non-null `aiEval`) ⇒ the narrative split into `<p>`s on blank-line separators (no markdown lib) + a `Generálva · {date}` caption + an **`Újragenerálás`** `CtaGhost`; `failed` **or** `ready` with a null `aiEval` (a defensive fallback — the backend should never produce this) ⇒ "Nem sikerült az AI-kiértékelés." + an **`Újrapróbálás`** `CtaGhost` — both buttons fire the SAME `regenerate()` the page's bottom `Riport újragenerálása` chip does. Below that: `Újrafuttatás` (the shared rerun-into-`MesoStartSheet` flow, `mezo-meyc.1`), a second ghost CTA **`Sablon mentése ebből a futamból`** (`mezo-tlwa` — `createTemplate(runToTemplate(meso))` then straight into the new template's editor; rendered only once the meso list has resolved the RUN, since the fork copies its day plan and the frozen report DTO carries none) and the `Riport újragenerálása` chip. Three page-level states beyond the happy path: `pending` (loading ghost), a genuine `error` (fetch failure → `Újrapróbálás`), and `notFound` (the run exists but carries no report yet) — which itself branches on the run: still running ⇒ "a riport a lezárás pillanatában készül el", archived with none ⇒ **„Riport generálása"**, the legacy-backfill CTA that calls the same regenerate endpoint. Data via `useMesoReport(id)` (`data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts`, §4), which polls every 3s once a report has landed only while `aiEvalStatus === 'pending' && aiEvalEnabled` — which since S3 is exactly the window in which the companion's async generator is running — plus a short post-regenerate window while the row doesn't exist yet.
- **Compare view** (`pages/MesoComparePage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/compare?a=&b=`, `mezo-meyc.4`, spec §4) — two closed runs side by side, reached from the library's „Összevetés" selection mode above. Full-screen sibling route, **on the `MozaikPage` scaffold since `mezo-d20.3.6`** (the `MesoReportPage` idiom, which is now the Mozaik one). **There is no compare endpoint** — the page runs **two `useMesoReport` reads** and composes client-side through PURE helpers in `logic/mesoCompare.ts` (`alignVolumeWeeks` / `sharedStrengthDeltas` / `contextDiff` / `peakVolumeRows` / `focusDiff`, §4). Blocks: a **two-column header** (each column `A`/`B` + title + `{start} → {end}` + `{weeks} hét`) → **`Fókusz-különbség`** (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — `focusDiff(run)` per side: a `régi modell` note (`isLegacyPlan`, below) when the run's plan predates the current tier model, plus its non-`grow` `musclePriorities` chips (emphasize before maintain) so a shared-exercise delta can be read against each side's actual focus → **`Adherencia`** (each side's `completionPct` big, over `{done}/{planned} edzés` and `{done}/{planned} hét`) → **`Csúcs-volumen · szet/hét`** (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5) — `peakVolumeRows(a, b)`: one row per muscle in either run's arc, `aPeak`/`bPeak` (the max planned value across that run's own weeks) against `aCeiling` (A's MRV), sorted by A's ceiling then A's peak → **`Volumen · szet/hét`** — the muscle-pill switch (the `MuscleArcSwitch` pill row re-styled locally; the chart itself can NOT be reused, it draws one run's arc) over a per-week `Hét / A terv / A tény / B terv / B tény` table in its own `overflow-x` container → **`Közös gyakorlatok`** — one card per exercise trained in BOTH runs (name + muscle, then each side's `{±kg}` LOAD line and `{±%}` e1RM figure), the **better side's percentage** in `--sage-deep` and bold, the weaker side plain secondary — deliberately **no red**: this compares two finished blocks, it does not condemn one → **`Kontextus-átlagok`** (the `contextDiff` rows, `–` where a run has no such measurement) → chips to open either run's own report. **States:** missing/blank/identical `?a=&b=` ⇒ a Hungarian „Válassz két lezárt futamot…" ghost + a `Történet megnyitása` CTA (a self-comparison is treated as an unusable link, not a state); per column, `notFound` ⇒ **„Előbb generálj riportot"** + a `Riport megnyitása` chip pointing at that run's report page (where the generate CTA lives), `error` ⇒ a per-column `Újrapróbálás`, otherwise a `Riport betöltése…` line — the other column keeps rendering either way, and the comparison body simply waits until BOTH reports exist.
- **Start sheet** (`sheets/MesoStartSheet.tsx`) — the single "stamp a run from a template" surface: start date (today by default, `localDateString()`) + an `Aktív`/`Tervezett` `.segtab` pick → `startTemplate(id, {startDate, status})`. On success `active` navigates to `/train/gym`, `planned` just closes (the run shows up under `Tervezett`); on failure the sheet stays open and retryable (the mutation-cache toast carries the message, §7a of the FE conventions).
- **Template editor** (`pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id`) — full-screen sibling route, still on its pre-redesign `.pghead-np` face (the F7.x deep design round for the editors has not run): back breadcrumb (`useBackNav('/train/mesocycles')`) → `Sablon · n× futtatva` eyebrow + title + goal + weeks/split meta → the shared **`MesoEditor`** over the template's `days` + `ExercisePickerSheet`. Persistence mirrors `MesoExercises`: local day state is authoritative and updates synchronously, each add/remove/change/reorder fires a background **full-template `updateTemplate` PUT** (a template has no per-day endpoint). The response is deliberately **not** re-seeded into state — the server regenerates every exercise id on a full write, so re-seeding would swap ids out from under the open accordion. The inner `TemplateDayEditor` is keyed on `template.id` and mounts only after the list query resolves, so its one-shot seed always sees real days.
- **Planner** (`MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/new`) — **rewritten wizard v2 (`mezo-d20.14`, meso-body.html `#page-wizard`).** The old 5-step AI planner (`Cél` / `Hossz + fázisok` / `Split + napok` / `Fókusz` / `Program`, goal presets, per-phase MEV/MAV/MRV editor, split picker) is **retired outright** for a **3-step** flow — `Mikor és miért` → `Fókusz` → `Program` — driven by one pure reducer (`wizard/wizardState.ts`) with the page itself as a thin dispatcher; each step is a view component (`wizard/StepWhen.tsx`, `StepFocus.tsx`, `StepProgram.tsx`). A `.mz-wprog` progress strip (tappable, forward jumps disabled) sits above the body; `STEP_LABELS` = `Mikor és miért / Fókusz / Program`.
  - **Step 1 — `Mikor és miért`** asks exactly three things: a day-count/weekday picker (2–6 training days via `DAY_CHIPS`/`COUNTS`, `canLeaveStepWhen` gates `Tovább →`; `recommendedDays(n)` seeds a sane default set for a tapped count), an optional free-text goal (`goalText`, no length cap in the UI beyond `maxLength=400` — feeds the generator's `goalText`, never blocks progress), and a static "Ami magától megy" `StatStrip` (`5 + 1` ramp+deload weeks, `+2` sets/week/muscle, `~8` set/session cap) — informational only, not editable. No goal-preset select, no per-phase curve editor, no separate split step: the split is derived from the day count alone (`logic/mesoPlan.ts`'s `SPLIT_TYPES`/`splitLine`).
  - **Step 2 — `Fókusz`** is the unchanged `MusclePriorityPicker` (≤`EMPHASIZE_CAP` 2 Emphasize groups, optional Maintain, else Grow) with a live week-1/peak `StatStrip` fed by `logic/mesoPlan.ts`'s `weekTotals(priorities)` — so the tier picker shows its own set-count consequence before the program even exists.
  - **Step 3 — `Program`** calls the generator **once per visit**: `goToStep` fires `useMesoPlanGenerate().generate(generateInput(state))` (`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`, §4 `#### Plan generator`) only when `step === 2 && !state.proposal` — a step round-trip (back to `Fókusz` and forward again) keeps the existing proposal AND every manual day edit; body is never empty during the wait (`mz-genwait` pulsing orb) or on a generator failure — which has **two faces** (`mezo-d20.14` review): with **no** proposal yet, a full-body `↺ Újrapróbálom` retry; with a proposal already standing, a **failed RE-generation keeps the program** and only adds an inline `mz-confirm` strip above the hero ("Nem sikerült az újragenerálás — a korábbi program megmaradt." + `Újra`/`Mégse`), so a hand-edited draft is never stranded. Once generated the step renders a **compact hero + day-mosaic**, not a long scroll: the hero (editable name, `weeks/nap-hét/W1/csúcs` `StatStrip`, the generator's own `rationale` line, and a `coachsub` line that reads **`Gemini · a determinisztikus kereteken belül`** when `proposal.llmUsed` is true or **`alap gyakorlat-kiosztás — újragenerálhatod`** when it is false — mock mode's `mockMesoPlan` always returns `llmUsed: false`, so the mock hero always shows the deterministic-filler line) sits above a `Mosaic` of `DayTile`s (`wizard/dayTiles.ts`'s `dayTileData`: sets/`~minutes` (4.4 min/working set)/muscle bars/type tone via `dayTone`), a `WeeklyBandsCard` (§4 `#### Weekly bands` below) and a collapsible `Csúcshét · terhelés-ellenőrzés` strip (`peakWeekFit`).
  - **Tapping a day tile opens `ProgramDayView`** (`wizard/ProgramDayView.tsx`) — **page-STATE** (`state.activeDay`), not a route, so the URL and the still-unsaved draft both survive the round trip (the session-prep pattern): it renders a per-day hero + the shared `MesoEditor` scoped to `[day]` for EDITING but handed the whole 7-day program as `weekDays` (`mezo-d20.14` review) — the tab strip/breakdown/exercise list stay on the one day, while every WEEK-scope derivation (hero week totals, `WeeklyBandsCard`, `structureLint`, `peakWeekFit`, `sessionCapWarnings`) reads the real week instead of judging one Monday as the whole week (same add/remove/reorder callbacks the builder uses) plus the `ExercisePickerSheet`, with **`‹ Program`** closing back to the mosaic rather than navigating away.
  - **Regenerating after a manual edit is guarded:** any `editProgram` dispatch sets `state.dirty`; tapping `↺ Újragenerálás` while dirty opens an inline confirm strip ("Kézzel szerkesztett napjaid vannak — az újragenerálás felülírja őket.") instead of calling the generator immediately. Nothing auto-regenerates: moving the `Hossz` segmented control (4–8 weeks) — or stepping back to change the training days or a focus tier — leaves the standing program alone (a silent regeneration would discard the manual edits). Instead the reducer keeps `proposalInput` (the exact `MesoPlanGenerateRequest` that produced the current proposal) and the pure **`inputChanged(state)`** compares it with `generateInput(state)` — days, weeks and the sparse tier map, order-independently; when they diverge the hero shows one `coachsub` note ("A bemenetek változtak a generálás óta — az újragenerálás a friss napokra/fókuszra rakja össze a programot."). Without that baseline a post-generation tier change was silent and `toUpsert` wrote the NEW `musclePriorities` next to the OLD program (`mezo-d20.14` review).
  - **Save is unchanged in shape:** the two buttons live on the terminal step — `✓ Mentés + indítás · {startDate}` creates the template then chains `startTemplate(id, {startDate, status: 'active'})` and lands on `/train/gym`; `Mentés sablonként` stops at the template and lands on the library. Failure paths stay honest (a failed create keeps the wizard mounted; a created-but-failed-start lands on the library, never Gym). `toUpsert` (`wizardState.ts`) writes `phaseCurve: phaseCurve(s.weeks)` — **derived from the CURRENT `weeks` at save time**, never carried over from the proposal's own curve — because the Hossz strip can move the length after generation, and a stale N-entry curve on a resized block would be a lie the save must not write; `musclePriorities`/`days` are the live `state.priorities`/`state.program` (sparse tier map via `sparse()`, `toDayInputs`).
  - **The wizard never touches the standing gym-time schedule.** There is no `saveGymSchedule` call anywhere in the flow and no invented weekday time — the varázsló asks nothing about clock time. `deriveGymSchedule` tolerates a day with no matching slot (`time: null`) rather than fabricating one; editing the actual HH:mm times is the standalone gym-schedule editor's job (`GymScheduleSheet`, §4 `GymScheduleSlot`).
- **Run page** (`MesocycleBuilderPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id`, mesocycle pages v2 Task 3, `mezo-d20.15`) — **the old three-view switcher (`Áttekintés`/`Volumen`/`Gyakorlatok`) is gone**: `Volumen` moved to its own route (`Heti vizsgálat`, below), `Gyakorlatok` is now per-day (`MesoDayPage`, below), and there is **no in-cycle `Fókusz` picker any more** — `MusclePriorityPicker` is planning-time only (the wizard's Step 2), because changing tiers mid-run used to silently re-plan the remaining weeks. The page itself is now status-first: a `PageHero` (name + sub) → an **"A blokk íve"** card (phase chip + week dots off `logic/mesoBands.ts` + a one-line `arcLine()` textual arc, `W1 · W2 · W3 · most · …`) → a **decider sentence** card (`deciderSentence(meso)`, only rendered when non-null — it maps the backend `VolumeChange.reason` vocabulary, e.g. `'kezdő hét (MEV)'`/`'cél teljesítve, nincs grind'`/`'tartás'`/`'deload'`, to a Hungarian sentence) → **two tiles** (`Mosaic`): **`Heti vizsgálat`** (a mini bar cluster of the top-5 `runBands`, tapping opens `/week`) and **`Hétfőn jön`** (`nextRolloverChips(meso)` — deliberately **not** clickable, it is a forecast of next week's rollover, not a destination) → a **day mosaic** ("A heted", one `DayTile` per training day; tapping a tile opens `/days/:day`, below) → the lifecycle footer: planned → `Aktiválás`, active → **`Meso lezárása` opens `MesoCloseSheet`** (`mezo-meyc.2`, see the `Run report + close flow` paragraph above), never closes directly. "Heti terv másolása" is inert (deferred). **An archived run redirects away to its report** (`<Navigate>`, `mezo-meyc.2`) before any of this renders. A day tile's `status` is only ever `'now'` (today) or `null` — never `'done'`: `useTrain()` exposes this week's completed instance for TODAY alone, never a per-day list, so a `✓ kész` on another day's tile would be invented.
- **Day page** (`pages/MesoDayPage.tsx`, `/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day`, Task 3) — the per-day editor a run-page day tile opens (URL-encoded day token, e.g. `H%C3%A9t`). `PageHero` (day letter, `{type} nap`, a "the edit lands at the next training day" sub-line) → a per-muscle set `StatStrip` → `<MesoExercises meso={meso} day={day.day} />`. **`MesoExercises`** (`components/MesoExercises.tsx`) lost its in-cycle `Fókusz` `<details>`/`MusclePriorityPicker` and the `PUT .../muscle-priorities` call it used to fire — `meso.musclePriorities` is read-only there now, feeding only the bands/lint math — and gained a **`day?: string`** prop that restricts the editor to that one day's exercises (`days.filter(d => d.day === day)`) while still passing the WHOLE week to `MesoEditor` as `weekDays`, so week-scope band/lint derivations keep judging the real week instead of one day. Persistence is unchanged: local day state is authoritative, each add/remove/change/reorder fires a background `saveDayExercises` PUT. While the block list is still in flight (real mode) the page holds a `role="status"` skeleton — the sibling week/muscle pages' pattern — and only a RESOLVED block without the day (or an unknown id) falls through to the "nincs ilyen nap/mesociklus" guard; rendering the guard during the fetch made every valid deep link flash as a dead one (`mezo-d20.15` fix wave). **Since the unified-editor merge (`mezo-7rdg`, spec `2026-08-01-set-budget-unified-editor-design.md`, superseding `mezo-n46i`'s `MesoDayTabsEditor`)** both the day page and the wizard's per-day `ProgramDayView` **render the shared `MesoEditor`** (`components/MesoEditor.tsx`): day tabs (exercise-count badge, `current` day preselected, a **red session-cap warning dot** on any day with a `sessionCapWarnings` breach), a gradient **`MesoEditorHero`** (day focus stats — sets/exercise count, weekly total + training-day count — amber-shifted only via the session-cap `warningCount`, since the weekly-band % overage alarm retired with `SetBudgetCard`, `mezo-d20.14`), a **`WeeklyBandsCard`** (`current → ceiling` per tier, no percentages, `▲ +2 / hét` step chip — replaced the old percent-pill `SetBudgetCard`, §4 `#### Weekly bands` below), a **`PeakFitCard`** ("Csúcshét · időbecslés", `mezo-3m5m` — flags training days whose session length would fall outside the 45–90 min band once every group actually reaches its tier's target; distinct from the structural-lint session-length rule below, which reads the template's OWN minutes today, §4 `#### Weekly bands`), and a **single-expand `ExerciseAccordionRow` list** (`components/ExerciseAccordionRow.tsx`; one row open at a time): collapsed rows show name + muscle pill + a `🔥`/`🌿` style chip; the expanded row adds a **`🔥 Failure` / `🌿 Volume`** segmented toggle (writes `targetRIR` 0/2, D1) above a 2×2 stepper grid (working sets, rep range, the nullable **`Kiinduló kg`** anchor — `auto` when unset, `mezo-anm4` — and warmup sets) plus a **`Finomhangolás`** disclosure for the raw RIR/rep-min/rep-max knobs. `ExerciseAccordionRow` **replaces the always-open `ExerciseRecipeRow` in this surface** — `ExerciseRecipeRow.tsx` itself is **retained**, but only for `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage` (see the spec's §7 reality note). Off-days are still detected by **muscle** (`'' = rest`, `'sport'`), now via the shared `isOffDay` helper (`logic/offDay.ts`) rather than a per-component copy. Drag-reorder (`SortableList`) is preserved on collapsed rows. The exercise picker (`ExercisePickerSheet`) is still **multi-add**: stays open across picks (per-row `Hozzáadva ✓` flash + live counter), takes a `dayLabel` context prop, and closes via the header **`Kész`** chip / ✕ / backdrop / Escape; a newly added exercise is filled by `logic/exerciseDefaults.ts`'s `addExerciseWithDefaults`/`libraryToGymExercise` — a **single hypertrophy scheme** (`mezo-d20.14`; the multi-goal `goalPreset`-keyed `SCHEMES` table and its `planner.ts` picker are retired) crossed with the picked exercise's `type`: a `compound`/`isolation` pick gets the hypertrophy reps/RIR/working-sets plus a type-seeded `warmupSets` (compound 2, isolation 1), while a `plyo` pick always gets the fixed weightless `PLYO_SCHEME` (3×5, 0 warmup sets) and is exempt from the weekly bands the same way a generator-seeded plyo row is (`mezo-gbo7`). The seeded `warmupSets` is then immediately refined by `suggestedWarmupSets` once the exercise is in the day (position/anchor-weight aware — see the recommendation-engine subsection in §4), so it usually differs from the type-seeded value by the time the row renders; a **`cut-prep`/`recovery`** isolation pick lands on `repMax 15`, which trips `suggestedWarmupSets`'s bodyweight-ish rule (`anchorWeightKg == null && repMax ≥ 15`) straight to **0 warmup sets** — contract-consistent, but worth knowing if it looks surprising in the UI. Every pick lands **expanded**; added-exercise ids use `crypto.randomUUID()`.

### `Sablonok` — the template library (`pages/MesoTemplatesPage.tsx`, `/train/templates`, `mezo-tlwa`)

A **full-page sibling** of the Mezociklus hub, reached from its `Sablonok` mosaic tile. It **deliberately stayed on the plain DS page-header shell** rather than taking the `MozaikPage`/`PageHero` scaffold its full-screen siblings (`MesoWeekPage`/`MesoMusclePage`/`MesoReportPage`/`MesoComparePage`) use — `train.nav.test.tsx` pins that distinction, and it is a list page rather than a drill-in leaf. DS page head (`Edzés · Sablonok` + h1 `Sablonok`, a `.pgact` `+ Új` → the planner) → a `Sablonok · {n}` eyebrow → the `MesoTemplateCard` list in served (created-date) order → the shared dashed **`+ Új sablon tervezése`** CTA that closes every DS list. Real-mode loading renders the layout-matched `MesoTemplatesSkeleton` (`role="status"`); an empty list adds a `Még nincs sablonod.` `GhostState` above the dashed CTA (which stays the entry either way). Data: `useMesoTemplates()` — no new endpoint, the whole tab is the existing CRUD.

**"régi modell" legacy note (mesocycle pages v2 Task 5, `isLegacyPlan`, `logic/mesoPlan.ts`).** `MesoTemplateCard` renders a dashed `régi modell` chip when `isLegacyPlan(template)` — `(goalPreset != null && goalPreset !== 'hypertrophy') || !phaseCurve.includes('Deload')`: a template written under the retired multi-goal `SCHEMES` model (a non-hypertrophy `goalPreset`), or one whose `phaseCurve` has no closing Deload week. A template with **no** `goalPreset` at all is not itself legacy — its absence is the current model's own steady state, not a signal. The chip carries a note that starting the template converts it onto the new model.

**Why its own page:** a blueprint list answers "what can I run" while the library answers "how are my blocks going", and once a template gained four actions the two jobs stopped fitting in one scroll. `MesoTemplateCard` now carries **two action rows** — the primary pair (`Szerkesztés` → `/train/mesocycles/templates/{id}`, `Indítás` → `MesoStartSheet`) over a quieter lifecycle pair:
- **`Duplikálás`** — re-sends the template's own document as a fresh `createTemplate` (days through the shared `toDayInputs`, `volumePerMuscle` baselines passed straight through) with a **`{title} (másolat)`** title, then navigates into the **copy's editor**: a duplicate exists to be changed, and the editor is also what proves it landed. `runCount` starts at 0 (server-computed — a copy has no runs).
- **`Törlés`** — a **two-tap confirm on the button itself** („Biztos? Törlés", the `CatalogExerciseSheet` idiom; card-local state, no modal), then `deleteTemplate(id)`. It is a **soft delete**: every run ever stamped from the template — and every frozen report — is untouched, which is exactly why a full confirm dialog would be over-ceremony here. **Known edge (`mezo-tlwa`, not fixed here — the bead is FE-only):** a run keeps its `templateId` after the template is deleted, and `MesoTemplateService.rerun` returns that id blindly while `start` resolves templates `…AndDeletedFalse` — so `Újrafuttatás` on such a run opens the start sheet and then fails with a `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` toast. The FE workaround is the run's own `Sablonná` fork (which needs no surviving template); the real fix is backend-side (re-materialize on rerun when the linked template is gone, the legacy path already does exactly that).

**Run → template (the other direction, `mezo-tlwa`).** Two entry points fork a *run's* plan into a NEW template, both `createTemplate(runToTemplate(meso))` → the new template's editor: the report page's `Sablon mentése ebből a futamból` CTA (above) and a `Történet` card's compact **`Sablonná`** chip (beside `Újrafuttatás`, hidden in selection mode). `logic/runToTemplate.ts` is the pure mapping — title `{title} — sablon`, plan fields copied (an empty-string `goal`/`split`/`style`/`shortTitle` normalizes to `null`), days via `toDayInputs` (rest days included), and `volumePerMuscle` collapsed from each `VolumeProfile` onto its **provenance baseline** (`profile.source.baseline`), skipping any muscle whose baseline can't be resolved (nothing resolvable ⇒ `null`). The baseline — not the profile's live `mev/mav/mrv` — is deliberate: those numbers are that run's adjusted/ramped state, and freezing one run's fatigue into a reusable blueprint would be wrong. **Fork vs rerun:** `Újrafuttatás` reuses the run's ORIGINATING template (`rerun`, materializing one for a legacy run), `Sablonná` creates an independent copy you can diverge. In mock mode both paths work off `useMesoTemplates`' `setQueryData` cache mutators (the copy appears in the list, `runCount` 0) — note the mock's own archived fixtures carry no `days`, so a mock fork of one yields an empty day plan; a real run always has its plan.

### `Heti vizsgálat` + izom-részlet — the volume week + per-muscle drill-in (`pages/MesoWeekPage.tsx` + `pages/MesoMusclePage.tsx`, mesocycle pages v2 Task 4, `mezo-d20.15`)

**`MesoOverviewPage`/`MesoOverview`/`MesoVolume`/`DayDetailSheet` are deleted outright** (components + tests) — the old provenance-overview route `.../:id/overview` is now a **redirect** (`RedirectToWeek` in `app/router.tsx`, `<Navigate to=".../week" replace>`), so every existing deep link (the `Mai` entry card, `Heti`'s `Mezociklus áttekintő` chip) still lands somewhere real. The whole surface — weekly rollup, per-muscle band, and the old provenance derivation steps — was rebuilt on `logic/mesoWeek.ts` rather than recomposed from `MesoVolume`.

- **`MesoWeekPage`** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/week`) — `PageHero` (total sets this week + the delta vs last week, `weekSummary(arc, tiles)`) → a `StatStrip` (total/delta/up-count/hold-count — the up/hold counts read off the per-tile `status`, not the raw `RunBand.step`, because a grind-held muscle reads `up` under the bare band math but its tile plainly shows a hold) → a live-rollover banner (`nextRolloverChips`) → a `Mosaic` of per-muscle `.mz-wtile` buttons, one per `muscleTiles(arc, meso)` entry: pill + tier chip, `current → ceiling`, a `VolumeBand` bar (hidden on a maintain tier), a 6-bar week spark, and a status line (`MEV-n tart` / `plafonon` / `= tartás · grind a múlt héten` / `▲ +N e héten · M a plafonig` — the `+N` is `MuscleWeekTile.step`, i.e. `nextStep(band)` clamped to the headroom, never a hard-coded `+2`). Tapping a tile navigates to `/week/:muscle`. A bottom coach line surfaces the block's peak week via **`peakWeek(series)`** — the last non-deload arc entry, shared with `MesoMusclePage`'s `csúcs` label, replacing a hard-coded `series[4]` that only ever held for a 6-week block. A **failed** arc fetch is told apart from „nincs még ív": the page renders `Nem sikerült betölteni a heti vizsgálatot — próbáld újra.` plus an `Újra` retry (the arc hook returns `refetch`), and both pages hold a `role="status"` skeleton while the block or the arc is pending.
- **`MesoMusclePage`** (`/train/mesocycles/:id/week/:muscle`) — absorbs the retired `MesoOverviewPage`'s provenance view for ONE muscle: a hero (current→ceiling, or the flat maintain value) → `StatStrip` → **"A sáv · hol tartasz"** (`VolumeBand`, tier≠maintain only) → "A blokk íve" spark chart → the coach sentence → **"Ezen a héten · hol dolgozik"** (`whereItWorks(meso, group)` — the actual training days the group trains, via `daySessionBreakdown`) → **"Honnan a sáv · levezetés"** (`<DerivationSteps>`, fed `profile`/`tier`/`ceiling`/`weekOneValue`/`series`/**`step`** (the resolved clamped ramp, so the `/ hét` and `hétfőn` cells never promise more sets than fit under the plafon) — the `01 Baseline → 02 Rád szabva → 03 Eredő·most` steps; **`02 Rád szabva` renders the real `VolumeProfile.source.adjustments`**, honestly empty for an engine-seeded run today rather than fabricating a personalization step) → **"Előző blokk"** (`previousBlock(archived, group)` — the most recent archived run that trained this group, read straight off that run's own `volumePerMuscle` snapshot rather than a fetched arc, captioned `indulás → csúcs / plafon · utolsó ismert`). `components/VolumeBar.tsx` (with `FinalStat`) is **deleted** — `VolumeBand`/`DerivationSteps` are its successors, and keeping an importer-less copy on disk was just an orphan.

### The former `Gym` tab — folded into `Heti` (`pages/GymPage.tsx` is a thin alias)
**There is no Gym page any more** (`mezo-d20.3.2`). Its whole surface moved onto `Heti` (see that subsection): the meta card's live zone rows, the `Időpontok` and `Mezociklus áttekintő` chips and the `MuscleWeekSheet` detail. Its per-day `GymDayCard` list was dropped as a duplicate of Heti's own agenda list, and `GymDayCard`/`GymStat`/`PhaseDots` were deleted in the F8 cleanup. `/train/gym` still resolves — `GymPage` renders `TrainWeekPage` — because three in-app callers and PWA bookmarks still point there (hub subsection above).

The **behavior** that folded in, in its own words. The meta stats `Fázis`/`Split` (plan-only) are gone with the fold (deliberate, above); **`Szetek`/`Gym napok` — live since the zone unification (`mezo-oyhy.7`)** survive as Heti's `StatStrip` cells, rendered `{kész}/{terv}` against the meso-plan-only `totalSets`/`gymDays.length` denominators: `doneWorkingSets` sums working sets across **all** of `useWeekMuscleLog()`'s this-week completed instances (meso **and** custom — a completed "Saját" session's sets count toward Szetek too, so its `{kész}` can exceed `{terv}`, e.g. `80/74`), while `doneGymDays` counts only completed instances with `origin === 'meso'` (custom sessions don't move the Gym napok numerator). **Since the muscle-week slice (`mezo-ly27`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-24-muscle-week-sheet-design.md`) the zone surface is tappable**; **since the live zone unification (`mezo-oyhy.7`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-04-live-zone-unification-design.md`) it is the group-level `ZoneMiniGrid`** (`components/ZoneMiniGrid.tsx`, superseding the old mezo-ly27 per-head pill grid) — `selectGymRows(weekZoneRows({ plannedDays: days, completed: weekLog.details }))`, one two-column mini `ZoneTrack` bar per budget group (done solid + remaining-plan ghost on the green optimal-zone underlay, `gymSegments`), with a `{doneSets}/{plannedSets}` numeric that marks the **WEEKLY PLAN** (not the live done state): `⚠` when `planBudget > 1`, `↓` when `plannedSets < mev` — mirroring the old pill grid's warning semantics one level coarser (budget group, not per-head). Per-head muscle detail remains one level down, on **`MuscleWeekSheet`** (`sheets/MuscleWeekSheet.tsx`), the weekly per-muscle detail: **① Izomcsoportok** — one rail-colored card row per muscle (weekly sets big, Σ rep-range, exercise count, `n×/hét gym` + per-sport `▲` stimulus chips, `+~XP` estimate); **② Sport & futás terhelés** — one card per planned sport slot / prescribed run session with region-aggregated `▲` loads from the **static heuristic table** `logic/sportMuscleLoad.ts` (a product heuristic, the Phase-3 cross-load engine's interim — the mock-only `crossLoad` fixtures are untouched); **③ Growth előrejelzés** — estimated athletic-skill XP for the planned week from `logic/growthForecast.ts` (mirrors the backend `mezo.progression.*` economy + curve by hand — documented drift risk; anchor-less exercises contribute no volume/e1RM estimate; PR/HR bonuses, robustness and quest/activity XP are NOT forecast), each row with the profile's progress bar and a sage `Lv n → n+1 ↗` flag when the estimate crosses the threshold. The whole surface is labeled `~ becslés`. The sheet itself calls `useRunning()` + `useProgressionProfile()` (lazy — queries fire only when opened); `meso` + the sport slots arrive as props from `TrainWeekPage`'s `useTrain()` data. Works in both modes with no mode branch (all inputs live). **Since the direct-start flow (`mezo-bxpg`) tapping a day navigates straight into the session or its review — `GymDaySheet` is deleted** (component + tests + both call sites), and that resolution now lives entirely in `WeeklyDayRow`'s gym branch on Heti: a day with a **completed instance this Mon–Sun week (any date)** — via `weekWorkouts.find` on `templateSessionId`, once per week per template day (D5) — routes to its review (`/train/review/{id}`); otherwise a day carrying exercises starts the session, plain `/train/session` on today's own row or `/train/session?day={templateDayId}` on any other day (the cross-day start, `mezo-p7rp`); a rest day is a no-op. **The old "another day's workout open" blocked-note state is gone**: direct-start always navigates, and the `/train/session` route's own day resolution (`open instance > ?day= param > weekday label`, §4) silently resumes whichever workout is actually open regardless of the day tapped — the accepted D6 simplification (`mezo-bxpg`), same missed-day chips retired (the prep hub shows the day identity instead).
