The **`Időpontok`** chip (now in Heti's `PageHead`) opens `GymScheduleSheet` (the weekly gym-time editor, §4 `GymScheduleSlot`); since `mezo-4t43` it is **always visible — including mock mode** (a local optimistic `gymOverride` keeps a mock edit visible in-session, as `saveGymSchedule` no-ops there). It is the **mid-cycle** editor; the **mesocycle planner (Step 2) is the primary schedule editor** — both write the same `PUT /api/train/gym-schedule` (§4). The `+ Saját` chip did not survive the fold as a chip — the shared `CustomWorkoutSheet` is reached from Heti's dashed `+ Saját edzés` CTA and the hub hero's ghost/rest-day CTA instead (see the `Saját edzés` subsection below). The **`Mezociklus áttekintő`** chip (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m`) is the second chip in Heti's `PageHead`, opening `/train/mesocycles/{id}/overview` (redirects to `Heti vizsgálat` at `.../week`) — active-meso-gated.

### `Saját edzés` — custom (meso-less) workout templates (`mezo-ws2x`)
A repeatable workout template that needs **no active mesocycle** — for ad-hoc/one-off days or for training with no plan running at all. Five entry points open the shared **`CustomWorkoutSheet`** (`sheets/CustomWorkoutSheet.tsx`, not mock-gated): the `Mai` rest-day card's dashed CTA, `Mai`'s today-only dashed footer (restored by `mezo-eahv` — the two are mutually exclusive, see §2), the `Mai` no-active-meso ghost's dashed row (all three `TrainTodayPage.tsx`), `Heti`'s dashed footer (`TrainWeekPage.tsx` — the old Mai weekly-plan footer that moved there with the list, `mezo-9bbc`), and — since the shell dissolution replaced Gym's `+ Saját` header chip — the **Edzés hub hero's `＋ Saját edzés` ghost CTA** (`EdzesHubPage.tsx`, on the no-meso ghost and the rest-day hero). The sheet lists saved templates (tap a row → starts it; ✎ → edit) plus an **`Új összeállítása`** CTA into the composer **`CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`** — full-screen sibling routes `/train/custom/new` (create) / `/train/custom/:id` (edit), same idiom as the mesocycle/running builders, no sub-nav. Its own **"Indítsuk ma →"** CTA lands on `/train/session?day={templateId}` — the ordinary `ActiveWorkoutPage` flow, entirely unchanged (§4 has the backend contract: the D5 once-per-week guard is skipped for custom, D6 one-open-workout is kept). A completed custom instance surfaces as an extra **`SAJÁT`** row on its date in the `Mai` weekly agenda (`WeeklyDayRow`'s `agenda.custom` list, tap → opens the review) and counts toward the `Gym` load tile's count (`weeklyLoad`'s `gymCount = planned + custom`; the duration average stays planned-only) — but it is **plan-adherence-exempt** (see §5 for the full counting-semantics split). Data layer: `useCustomWorkouts`/`useCustomWorkoutActions` (`data/train/customWorkoutHooks.ts`, barrel-reexported from `@/data/hooks`); mock mode serves the `customWorkoutsMock` fixture and no-ops every write, byte-parity preserved like every other Train write. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-custom-workout-design.md`.

### Active workout (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`, `/train/session`)
Full-screen takeover, **four phases** (`mezo-cd8s` replaced the old prep→active→auto-finish flow): **`prep`** (since `mezo-d20.3.8` the **6-tile mosaic hub** — breadcrumb → hero tile with the 4-cell forecast strip and the `⚡ Kezdjük el →` CTA above the fold → six tiles, each opening its own in-route page; see the dedicated paragraph below) → **`active`** (free-navigation per-set weight/reps/RIR logging — **the viewed exercise is the logging target**, see the navigation paragraph below — "Múlt hét" comparison, set dots, set history, PR toast, RP feedback debrief) → **`summary`** (the explicit-finish **`WorkoutSummary`** in **`closing`** mode — since the colorful redesign (`mezo-w943`, see the dedicated paragraph below) a hero-number + fire halo, muscle-region pills, a 4-cell stat strip, medal/target cards and a full per-exercise set-chip map, plus the **closing note field** (§ below), with **"Edzés lezárása ✓"** as the ONLY finish trigger) → **`complete`** (the same `WorkoutSummary` read-only in **`closed`** mode — calm halo, the saved note read-only — rendered after the finish POST resolves). A mid-workout hard reload **resumes** straight back into `active`. **Every phase flip resets the app scroller (`mezo-vad0`):** the four phases swap the whole screen *without* a route change, so `ScreenContent`'s navigation-keyed reset never fires — the long prep briefing's bottom-anchored "Kezdjük el" would otherwise hand its scroll offset straight to the execution card (and to the closing summary). A `useEffect` on `phase` calls `scrollToTop(screenScroller())` (`shared/lib/screenScroll.ts` — the `.screen-content` lookup + the instant, never-smooth-animated top jump, now shared with `ScreenContent` itself). **Done-day gate (`mezo-cd8s`):** when today's instance is already **completed** and nothing is open, the outer `ActiveWorkoutPage` redirects to the review (`<Navigate to={/train/review/{completedTodayWorkout.id}} replace />`, placed **after** the `workoutPending`/no-workout guards) — the prep/session screen (challenges included) is unreachable until next week; mock mode has no `completedTodayWorkout`, so it never fires there. **Explicit finish (`mezo-cd8s`) + gamified level-up (`mezo-te8k`, P5).** The workout no longer auto-finishes — the summary's **"Edzés lezárása ✓"** CTA is the ONLY completion trigger. It calls `finishAndCelebrate` → `finishWorkout(workoutId ?? 'mock', { onSuccess, onSettled })` (a `finishPending` flag disables the CTA while the POST is in flight; **`onSettled` re-enables it so a failed finish can be retried** — never stuck-disabled); on the response's `levelUp` it calls `showLevelUp(r.levelUp)` (the shared `LevelUpProvider` host, §5) and flips the phase to `complete`. The full-bleed `LevelUpScreen` overlay portals **over** the **closed `WorkoutSummary`** and its single `Tovább` CTA dismisses it to reveal the closed summary underneath. **Medal collection replaced the old demo PR path outright (`mezo-wp6n`)** — see the **`Medálok`** subsection of §2 and §4 below for the full engine: `PRToast.tsx`/`showPR`/`PR_DEMO_THRESHOLD_KG`/`hadPrFromSignal` are all deleted. Each `logSet` response now carries the set's real `medals[]`; a RECORD-tier medal (`WEIGHT`/`REPS_AT_WEIGHT`/`E1RM`) fires the real `MedalToast` right after the set is logged (typically during the rest window) and leaves a gold `MedalChip` on the set row (since the v4 table redesign, `mezo-8xmf`: inside the row's status cell, alongside its `✓`/`▼ cél alatt`/`▲ cél felett`/`MOST ↑` rep-range status — see the Prescribed-sets paragraph below), while a `TARGET_HIT` medal adds no glyph of its own — the v4 status cell's rep-range comparison already conveys hit/miss (the pre-v4 "double-tick" done-check recolor this superseded is gone). The old `· PR ✨` title suffix is gone; the closed `WorkoutSummary` instead lists the session's `medals: Medal[]` (incl. `SESSION_VOLUME`, which only ever resolves at finish), sorted RECORD-first. On a successful finish real mode also **invalidates the day's challenges** (`['challenges', templateSessionId, today]`) so the server-resolved outcomes land on the closed summary. When the progression switch is off the response carries no `levelUp` → no overlay, the closed summary shows directly. In **mock mode** `workoutId` is null (no instance), so the `?? 'mock'` sentinel still drives the no-op finish mutation, which returns a seeded `LevelUpResult` fixture so the prototype shows the overlay. **Since Napiv S5 (`mezo-8141`) weight/reps use the giant `SetStepper`** (`features/train/components/SetStepper.tsx`) instead — a `.stepper` tile per field with ± buttons clamped to the contract bounds (weight 0–999 step 2.5, reps 1–100 step 1, integer) on every tap; **the center value is tap-to-edit again (`mezo-o7ds`)** — at rest the `.n` value renders as a reset-styled `<button>` (`aria-label` `"{label} pontos megadása"`, visually unchanged), tapping it swaps in an `<input>` backed by the same `useEditableNumber` hook as `NumberStep` (free keyboard entry, HU decimal comma, commit + clamp on blur/Enter), so exact non-±2.5 weights (microplates, odd dumbbells — `weightKg` has no `multipleOf` in the contract) are loggable again, restoring the CompactStepper capability S5 dropped. **The tile layout deviates from the S5 mockup (`mezo-eerq`):** the value line sits ABOVE a centered ± button row instead of the mockup's side-flanking buttons — at real phone widths (two tiles in a ~330px card) a flanking layout cannot fit a `107,5 kg` value without squashing the 40 px round buttons into ovals. Its CSS is **scoped as `.steprow .stepper`** (`prototype.css`) because the bare `.stepper` class is the older generic grid stepper (RecipeStepper et al.), whose `display:grid` used to bleed in and overflow the excard (the `CompactStepper` component that provided logging here pre-S5 is gone from this screen — it survives only in the run-block builder's `RunWeekEditor`, where its `useEditableNumber` hook still backs `NumberStep`, same as in the sport/run log sheets — but its tap-to-edit UX is back on this screen via `SetStepper`, above). See the S5 layout paragraph below for the full `.wk-top`/`.excard` structure this stepper sits in. The active-phase session state — logged sets, the per-exercise cursor, and per-exercise set counts — lives in a pure, **exerciseId-keyed** model (`workoutState.ts`: `Session{order,logged,extra,removed,skipped,planned,prescribed}` + `makeSession`/`currentExerciseId`/`nextSetIdx`/`nextUnfinishedAfter`/`effectiveSetCount`/`completeSet(s,exerciseId,set)`/`addExtraSet`/`skipExercise`/`seedFromOpen`/`mergePlan`/`canRemoveSet`/`removeSet`/`updateLoggedSet`/`attachSetId` — the last four power the tappable set-list rows below, `mezo-l3on`), so the screen is a thin view over it (resume rebuilds the model from the open instance via `seedFromOpen`). **The cursor is per-exercise (`mezo-cd8s`):** there is no scalar `setIdx` — an exercise's next-set index derives from `nextSetIdx(s,id) = logged[id].length`, `completeSet` appends to a **given** exercise (not a linear cursor), and the page tracks a `viewedId` (the on-screen exercise = the logging target) so navigation can be free; `nextUnfinishedAfter(s,id)` picks the auto-advance target (next non-skipped, not-fully-logged exercise after `id`, wrapping) after a debrief/skip. The default flow is behavior-identical — `viewedId` seeds from and advances through the linear `currentExerciseId` order — but the athlete can now **navigate freely** (`mezo-cd8s`): the two-way **`.pagerbar`** below the card (‹ Előző / Következő › — plain order-neighbours of the viewed exercise, disabled at the list edges), **tappable `.exdots`**, a **horizontal swipe** on the `.excard` (a ≥60 px drag; left = next / right = previous, so taps on the inner controls never trigger it), and the header counter (now a `▾` button, `aria-label="Gyakorlatlista"`) opening **`ExerciseOverviewSheet`** (`sheets/ExerciseOverviewSheet.tsx`) — a jump-to list of every exercise with its live state (`✓ kész` / `● folyamatban n/m` / `○ hátravan` / `⊘ kihagyva`, current row tinted). Every jump routes through `jumpTo(id)`, which **no-ops while a debrief is open** (`feedbackEx` pins the on-screen exercise). **Mid-session plan growth is reconciled via `mergePlan`** (`mezo-ohvm`): the session is seeded once at mount, but the server can append template exercises while a workout is open (the fix-zárás closing block, §4) — an id missing from `session.planned` would read as 0 planned sets and count as done, silently auto-finishing the workout. A `useEffect` on `W.exercises` folds new plan exercises into `order`/`planned`/`prescribed` (appended at the end, progress untouched; identity-stable when nothing is new). This non-linear, id-keyed cursor is the foundation for the in-session reorder / add-set / skip / note features. The active-phase header carries a **`⋯` "Gyakorlat műveletek"** button that opens `ExerciseActionSheet` (`sheets/ExerciseActionSheet.tsx`) — a **five-row** menu (↕ Áthelyezés · ⊘ Kihagyás · ＋ Szett · ✎ Jegyzet · ✓ **Edzés befejezése…**) built on the shared `Sheet`; the last row (`onFinishWorkout`) is the **early exit to the summary** — it lands the session on the `WorkoutSummary` closing screen *before* every exercise is resolved (`mezo-cd8s`). **Áthelyezés (reorder)** switches to a sub-view hosting the shared `SortableList` over the **viewed exercise ITSELF plus everything after it** (`session.order.slice(ci)`) — only the exercises *before* it stay fixed, and the reorder is **client-only / ephemeral** (it just replaces `session.order` via a functional `setSession` updater, no persistence). Because `nextUnfinishedAfter` (the post-debrief auto-advance) derives "next" from `order` rather than an array index, reordering the tail directly changes which exercise comes up after the current one. **The current exercise is movable too (`mezo-vad0`)** — the real gym case is "this machine is taken, push the one I'm on back", which the old current-excluded cut (`slice(ci+1)`) made impossible. It heads the list carrying a **`most`** badge (`reorderable[].current`, the prop is `reorderable`, not `remaining`), and moving it off the head **hands the screen over**: `handleReorder` then points `viewedId` at the first non-skipped, not-fully-logged id of the new segment (falling back to its head), so the session continues with whatever is genuinely up now instead of staying on the exercise the user just postponed. No `feedbackEx` guard is needed — the sheet is unmounted while a debrief is open. Note the header counter/`.exdots` stay keyed to the **static plan index** by design (a reordered-behind exercise must not read as done), so the eyebrow can show e.g. `2/5` on the first exercise of a reordered session. **＋ Szett (add-set, F2)** is wired via `onAddSet`: it bumps `session.extra[id]` (`addExtraSet`), growing `effectiveSetCount` (planned + extra) so the header set count and the set dots gain one more. **The added set carries a distinct dashed marker again**, restored in the final-review fix (`mezo-8141`): a set-dot at/past the exercise's `session.planned` baseline (`i >= session.planned[current.id]`) renders `.setdots .sd.extra` — a dashed coral outline (`prototype.css`) that only applies while the dot is still pending, yielding to the normal `.don`/`.cur` styling once it's logged/current. The prototype's older, separate `.set-dot.extra` class family (over the pre-S5 `.set-dot`) stays dead CSS, superseded by `.setdots .sd` ([design-system doc](_platform-design-system.md) §3/§7) — only the new `.sd.extra` modifier above is live. The added set logs through the **existing `logSet` path** like any planned set (the persisted `setIndex` is just the next index — **no new data hook**). After the add, a small **`sheet-nested` prompt** offers **"Csak ma"** (session-only, the default) vs **"Minden hétre"**: the latter calls `writeExtraSetToTemplate`, which finds the meso day whose exercise list contains the current exercise (by id — the `/today` exercise id IS the template-day exercise row id), bumps that exercise's `sets` by 1, and persists the full day list via the existing **`saveDayExercises` PUT** so future weeks inherit the higher count. **Only add-set offers a template write — reorder and skip never touch the plan.** (Mock mode shows the prompt but the write no-ops, like every other mock write; if no meso day contains the exercise, the write is a safe no-op.) **⊘ Kihagyás (skip, F3)** is wired via `onSkip` → `handleSkip`: it marks the current exercise skipped in the model (`skipExercise` → `session.skipped`) and advances to the next non-skipped exercise **without an RP debrief** (or **lands on the `summary` phase** when nothing remains — mirroring `advanceAfterFeedback`'s all-done branch, which since `mezo-cd8s` also routes to `summary` instead of auto-finishing); real mode persists via `useTrain.skipExercise` (`POST .../{id}/skip`, idempotent), mock no-ops. Per spec, **already-logged sets on a skipped exercise stay** (skip only abandons the remainder): the `WorkoutSummary` per-exercise recap (each row's `SummaryExercise.skipped` flag) marks a skipped exercise **"kihagyva"** — struck through when nothing was logged, or the real `n/m szet` count + a muted `· kihagyva` tag when some sets were done — and a mid-workout reload restores skipped state because `seedFromOpen` routes `skipped` markers to `session.skipped` (not `logged`). **✎ Jegyzet (note, F4)** is wired via `onEditNote`: each exercise carries a durable per-exercise **note** (`TodayExercise.note`, surfaced through `toWorkoutPlan` onto `LoggedWorkoutExercise.note`). An always-visible **note pill** (`aria-label="Gyakorlat-jegyzet"`) renders under the exercise name whenever the effective note is non-empty (`effectiveNote = localNotes[current.id] ?? current.note ?? ''`); `⋯` → "Jegyzet" opens a `sheet-nested` editor (a 500-char `<textarea>` prefilled with the current note) whose "Mentés" calls `useTrain.saveExerciseNote` (`PUT /api/train/exercises/{exerciseId}/note`, real persists + invalidates `workoutToday`; mock no-ops) and writes a **local override** (`localNotes`, keyed by exercise id) so the pill updates instantly in both modes. Saving an **empty** note clears it (the pill hides and `hasNote` flips the row label back to "Jegyzet"). The note is **durable on the exercise template** (not the instance), so it pre-loads on the next session. The `⋯` button is itself **disabled while a debrief (`FeedbackModal`) is open**, so the sheet always operates on the unambiguous current exercise; the sheet's mount condition is also debrief-gated (`actionSheetOpen && !feedbackEx`), so when the debrief pins, the page **explicitly resets `actionSheetOpen`** (`mezo-91rw`) — the conditional unmount skips the sheet's animated `onClose`, and a stale `true` would re-open the menu (already re-titled to the next exercise) once the debrief resolves; pre-`mezo-91rw` this was masked by the `Sheet`'s leaked exit timer, the same leak behind the post-teardown `window is not defined` CI flake (see [\_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) §1 item 3) (during a debrief the on-screen exercise is the pinned just-finished one via `feedbackEx`, which overrides `viewedId` until the debrief resolves). The current exercise also carries a **`▶ Demo`** (`VideoDemo`) that tap-reveals its catalog-resolved demo video inline (`TodayExercise.videoUrl`, §4) — no navigation away from the session. **Challenges (`mezo-hbwi`, live) — proactive-owned, not in `WorkoutPlan`:** the prep-screen carousel is fed by `useChallenges(templateSessionId, localToday)` (not `W.challenges`, which stays `[]` in real), and `useChallengeActions.decide()` persists the L2 accept/dismiss. The `accepted` map derives from the server `status` (`accepted | hit | miss`) in live and from a local toggle in mock (byte-parity). Once a workout is decided each `ChallengeCard` renders its **outcome chip + line** (`hit → ✓ Megerősítve` / `miss → ◯ Nem igazolódott` / `inconclusive → ◌ Nem értékelhető`) with the accept/skip row hidden; live confidence-null reads „tanulom" and the `tools` chips are hidden (mock keeps `conf 72%` + chips). Type catalog is now **PR / Depth / Volume / `overload`** (`Tempo` still deferred — no tempo is logged, so it can't be honestly evaluated); `overload` is the deterministic daily-challenge tie-in (Progressive Overload Plan 3, `mezo-gj42`) — see the Progression-signal paragraph below and [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md). The backend is proactive-owned: a lazy-on-prep `ChallengeGenerator` (structured targets, honest confidence/refs) + a NEW set-level `ChallengeOutcomeEvaluator` that judges hit/miss/inconclusive from the logged `exercise_set` rows (which FK straight back to the **template** exercise — `WorkoutService.java:204` — so no template→instance mapping) over the `challenge` table; the `/api/proactive/challenge` list+decide flow, the write path, and the full lifecycle live in [proactive.md](proactive.md) (§HBWI/§3/§4).

**Summary/review redesign — colorful pill/chip language (`mezo-w943`, design spec `2026-08-10-workout-summary-redesign-design.md`; supersedes the grey 2026-07-15 layout) — one shell, two modes.** `WorkoutSummary.tsx` (`components/WorkoutSummary.tsx`) stays the single component for both the `summary`/`complete` phases here AND the `/train/review/:workoutId` done-day review (`WorkoutReviewPage`, below) — frontend-only, no API/backend change. Every derivation is pure and table-tested in **`logic/summaryStats.ts`**'s `deriveSummaryStats(exercises, medals)` (below), keeping the component itself presentational. Top to bottom: a **hero** — eyebrow (`closing`: `EDZÉS VÉGE` coral; `closed`: `Lezárva · {huMonthDayDow}`, the `complete` phase inside `ActiveWorkoutPage` uses `Lezárva · ma`) + title + a **hero-number** (`{doneSets}/{plannedSets} szett`, Geist 200 tabular) + a fact subtitle (`{volumeT} t összvolumen · {doneEx}/{totalEx} gyakorlat`, `· ~{durationMin} perc` appended only when `durationMin` is truthy) in front of a decorative `aria-hidden` **halo** blob — `fire` (coral-amber) in `closing`, `calm` (sage-amber, slower) in `closed`; `prefers-reduced-motion` freezes it to a still image. Below the hero, **muscle-region pills** — `muscleRegion()` aggregation over each exercise's `muscle`, wash/deep family-toned, most-sets-first; a region whose every exercise logged 0 sets renders a struck-through, faded `off` pill; an unmapped/legacy muscle key (`muscleRegion() === null`) is excluded from the aggregation entirely. A **4-cell stat strip** — `Volumen {t} t` · `Rekord {n} 🏅` (amber only when `n > 0`) · `Célszett {n} ✓` (sage only when `n > 0`) · `Ø RIR {x,y}` (or `–` with 0 logged sets) — replaces the old three-box "Mai mérleg". **Medálok** (rendered only when `medals.length > 0`): RECORD-tier medals get their own amber celebration cards (🏅 + `MEDAL_TYPE_LABEL` + exercise name + `medalValueLabel()` + an `előző: {previousValue}` line); TARGET-tier medals no longer get individual cards — they collapse into one sage summary row (`✓ {n} célszett teljesítve` + a per-exercise `{name} ×{n}` chip row); the section label carries a `{n} rekord · {n} cél` mono counter. **Kihívások** (only when present) is layout-unchanged (status roundel + type/exercise + detail + outcome chip, `{n} megvan · {n} kimaradt` counter). **Gyakorlatonként — the set-chip map is the redesign's core**: every exercise gets a family-rail card (`muscleColor(muscle)`, the execution-card-v2 language, §2 Active workout) with a header (name + muscle tag pill + `n/m` set counter, warning-toned on a partial completion) and a wrapped row of `{weight} × {reps} @{rir}` chips — the matched **record set** (a RECORD medal's `exerciseName` + `setIndex`, falling back to a weight+reps value match when `setIndex` is absent or out-of-bounds) renders an amber 🏅 chip, the **heaviest set** (ties break to more reps, then the earlier set) renders a family-wash "top" chip, and any unfinished planned sets collapse into a single dashed ghost chip (`— kimaradt`); an exercise with 0 logged sets renders a faded card with a struck-through name, a `kihagyva` tag, and no chip row at all. **The chip map now renders identically in BOTH modes** — the old `showSetLines` prop, which limited `closing` to a top-set-only view, is **deleted**. The **note is real** since `mezo-d20.8.2.2` — see the dedicated paragraph below. (It was removed outright by F7.2 in between, because until then it accepted what you typed and dropped it; `mezo-s52z`, which tracked the same deferral, is superseded.) CTAs are unchanged (`Edzés lezárása ✓` + `← Vissza az edzéshez` in `closing`; `← Vissza` in `closed`). **`WorkoutReviewPage.tsx`** (the `/train/review/:workoutId` consumer, `closed` mode) previously rendered no medals at all — since this redesign it sources the session's medals from the existing **`useMedals()`** cabinet (`GET /api/train/medals`, dual-mode) filtered to `m.workoutSessionId === detail.id` (an empty filter result still renders no medal section, same honesty rule as everywhere else); the `complete` phase inside `ActiveWorkoutPage` keeps sourcing medals from the in-memory `sessionMedals` earned during the just-finished POST, so the two call sites source medals differently but render identically. `SummaryExercise` gained a required `muscle: string` (both call sites map it straight off `WorkoutDetailExercise.muscle` / `LoggedWorkoutExercise.muscle`) and `WorkoutSummary` gained an optional `durationMin?: number | null` (review: `detail.durationEst ?? null`; active: `W.durationEst`).

**The workout-level closing note (`mezo-d20.8.2.2`, spec [`2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-design.md)).** One sentence about the WHOLE session — distinct from the per-exercise note (`PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}/note`, durable on the template) and the per-set note (`ExerciseSetResponse.note`), both of which were already real. **Storage** is a NEW `workout_session.closing_note` column, deliberately not a reuse of `workout_session.note`: that one carries the TEMPLATE day's plan note (written by `TrainService` on mesocycle creation, published as `MesoDay.note`, snapshotted into `MesoDayJson`), and the table holds both template and instance rows — one column with two meanings could leak an instance's closing note onto the mesocycle-plan surface. **Two write paths, one schema** (`WorkoutNoteRequest`, `maxLength: 1000`, blank clears): the finish endpoint gained an **optional** request body (`requestBody: required: false`, the `MesocycleCloseRequest` precedent — a bodyless finish still works), and a new **`PUT /api/train/workouts/{id}/note`** mirrors `saveExerciseNote`. Their semantics differ on purpose: the finish body is **fill-if-empty** (finishing is contractually idempotent, so a re-finish or a bodyless retry after a failed one must never erase what was written), while the PUT is **last-write-wins**. `WorkoutDetailResponse.note` reads it back. **UI**: in `closing` the field sits ABOVE the CTA block — which is in flow, never `position: fixed`, so the mobile keyboard pushes the finish button instead of covering it — and is labelled as a question, **"Hogy ment?"**, because a bare box is the shape users skip. It is optional and skippable; nothing ever remarks on an empty one (ADR 0010). In `closed` the saved sentence returns as a Fraunces-italic block ("Amit aznap írtál"); **with no note, nothing renders** — except on `WorkoutReviewPage`, the one surface whose whole job is revisiting, where a `✎` edits in place and an absent note offers a quiet **`＋ Jegyzet ehhez az edzéshez`**. The draft and the writes are **page-owned props** (`note` / `draftNote` / `onDraftNote` / `noteEditing` / `onEditNote` / `onNoteSave` / `onNoteCancel`); `WorkoutSummary` has two callers and stays presentational. Data layer: `trainApi.finishWorkout(id, note?)` + `trainApi.saveWorkoutNote(id, note)`, `useTrain().finishWorkout(id, { note })`, and `useWorkoutNote()` (`data/train/workoutNoteHooks.ts`) — whose mock arm writes the detail cache rather than no-oping, so a note the user just typed cannot vanish while the UI pretends it saved. Deliberately NOT included: a mood/effort chip row (RIR is already collected per set and consumed by the review's Ø RIR cell). **The note now also feeds the companion and the Memoár** (`mezo-d20.13`, spec [`2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-kontextus-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-09-01-edzes-jegyzet-kontextus-design.md)) — see [companion.md](companion.md) and [proactive.md](proactive.md); it rides the existing `companion → train` / `proactive → train` read edges, verbatim and merely truncated, never summarized.

**The review is a CONTEXT report (`mezo-d20.8.2.1`, F7.2, design spec [`2026-08-31-edzes-review-kontextus-riport.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-31-edzes-review-kontextus-riport.md)).** Until this round the `closed` mode was a colour swap of the closing report — it knew nothing the `closing` mode did not. The revisit is a different job (*mihez képest*, not *mi történt*), so everything `WorkoutReviewPage` now adds on top of the shared shell is context, and **all of it rides existing endpoints — no contract changed**.

- **The „Mihez képest" tile** (`.wr-cmp`, between hero and stat strip) names the **previous instance of the same template day** — the most recent earlier one, whatever the gap, because pretending to a weekly rhythm that was broken would be a lie — and gives three deltas mirroring the strip's own cells: volumen · célszett · Ø RIR, each with the reference's raw value beneath so reading the delta needs no mental arithmetic. The reference's age is the **distance between the two sessions** (`gapLabel`), not its age from today: stepping back onto the reference must not silently re-scope the phrase. **The tone rule is ADR 0010 in code** (`logic/workoutComparison.ts`, table-tested): the number is always signed and honest, the colour only ever rewards — sage upward, neutral graphite downward, and there is deliberately no `down` tone to reach for. **Ø RIR is hard-wired neutral**, because there less is harder, so an automatic tone would say the opposite of what happened. `frontend/src/features/train/workoutReviewTokens.test.ts` fails the build if a coral/terracotta/error token ever reaches the tile.
- **No reference → no tile.** A first instance of a template day, and any custom workout (no `templateSessionId`, hence no chain at all), renders neither the tile nor the stepping — not an empty state for something that legitimately does not exist.
- **The exercise inventory is a swimlane** (`.wr-lane`), replacing five near-identical stacked cards whose chip rows made the individual SET unreadable. One tile per exercise in its muscle family's wash: monogram disc, `REKORD` stamp, **one anchor number** (the heaviest working set, ties → more reps) and a set-bar row — solid logged · gold medal · faint warmup · dashed missed.
- **Depth is on the exercise's own view, not in the scroll** (`components/ExerciseReview.tsx`). Every set is its own tile — `90 kg × 8 · RIR 2 · CÉLSÁVBAN · 720 kg` — with the medal set in gold, missed slots as ghost tiles **numbered along the working sequence** (`B · 1 · 2 · 3 · 4`, never jumping over a logged warmup), and **the set note under its own set**: `ExerciseSetResponse.note` has always been on the wire and was displayed nowhere. Like the prep tile pages above, this is a **local view, not a route** — the closing report lives inside `ActiveWorkoutPage`'s phase machine and has no route of its own, so a routed variant would mean two mechanisms for one screen. Its stat strip's fourth cell (`Előzőleg`, the reference's top set) follows **the same gate as the comparison tile**, so comparison cannot enter the closing report by a side door.
- **Stepping runs on the template-day chain** (`useTemplateDayChain`, `data/train/workoutDetailHooks.ts`) — the same axis the comparison uses, so there is one mental model rather than two. Page-owned (passed to the shell as `footer`); the shell knows nothing about routes. At either end the button is **disabled, not hidden**, so the chain's existence stays visible.
- **The dead workout-note `<textarea>` is gone.** It had no `value`, no `onChange` and no field on the wire — it accepted what you typed and dropped it. Making it real is its own slice (`mezo-d20.8.2.2`: `finish` request body, column, `note` on `WorkoutDetailResponse`). Nothing is offered that cannot be kept.
- **Mozaik face**: the report gained a page tone (`.mz-p-coral` closing / `.mz-p-sage` closed) it never had, the `.mz-statstrip` floating cells in place of its own divider-seam grid, a clay `s-medal` disc instead of 🏅, and an `EntranceGroup` stagger — it was squarely in the F9 audit's "no entrance choreography at all" class. The challenge glyphs `✓ ◯ ⊘ ◌` stay: they are the designed geometric language, not leftover emoji.


**Prep is a 6-tile mosaic hub (`mezo-d20.3.8`) — the Huawei tile→own-page pattern, inside one route.** The `prep` phase no longer scrolls one long mission briefing. It composes: the **breadcrumb** (`← Vissza`, `.sticky-top`) → a coral **hero tile** (`{huWeekdayFull()} · {weekLabel}` eyebrow + `W.title` + a 4-cell `StatStrip` — `várható XP` / `szett` / `idő` / `izomcsoport`, each honestly `—` when its source is null — + the sticky-equivalent `⚡ Kezdjük el →` CTA **above the fold**) → a **`Mosaic` of up to six tiles**, each carrying a live line and opening its OWN page: `Gyakorlatok` (`{n} gyakorlat · {n} szett`), `Fejlődés` (`+{totalXp} XP`, rendered only when a forecast exists), `Heti zóna` (`kész {done}/{plan} edzés`, only when there are zone rows), `Küldetések` (an accepted-count badge on the `i-kihivas` clay icon), `Bemelegítés` (`{n} perc · {n} blokk`) and `Niggle` (only when the day plan actually flags one, with a `!` badge until confirmed).

The tile pages are **local components, not routes** — `prepTile` state swaps the whole prep render, so `/train/session` stays one URL and the session is never remounted: `pages/prep/{PrepGyakorlatokPage,PrepFejlodesPage,PrepHetiZonaPage,PrepKuldetesekPage,PrepBemelegitesPage,PrepNigglePage}.tsx`, each a `MozaikPage`/`PageHead onBack`/`PageHero`/`PageBody` scaffold with a `StatStrip` and a closing principle line. They render the SAME data the flat briefing did, just one subject at a time — `PrepGyakorlatokPage` uses the new `PrepExerciseTile` (a tile port of `PrepExerciseCard`'s data reads), `PrepKuldetesekPage` stacks the unchanged `ChallengeCard`s vertically (same accept/pass contract, no scroll-snap rail), `PrepHetiZonaPage` wraps the unchanged `WeekZoneCard`, `PrepFejlodesPage` renders the XP/skill forecast, `PrepBemelegitesPage` the fixed 3-block `WARMUP_ROWS` protocol.

**Superseded by the above, still true as history — the mission-briefing prep (`mezo-bxpg`).** It composed, top to bottom: **`PrepHero`** (`components/PrepHero.tsx`) — a centered overline `{huWeekdayFull()} · {weekLabel}` + `W.title`, a bordered XP ring **"+{totalXp} VÁRHATÓ XP"** beside up to 3 top skill progress bars (amber when `willLevelUp`, else sage; a **"⚡ szintlépés-esély!"** micro-badge appears under a bar whose forecast XP crosses `xpThreshold(level+1)`), and a stat pill **"{szett} szett · ~{rep} rep · ~{perc} perc · {n} izomcsoport"** — → **`WeekZoneCard`** (`components/WeekZoneCard.tsx`, live zone unification, `mezo-oyhy.7`, below `PrepHero`) — rows = the muscle groups trained **today** (`selectPrepRows`, biggest `todaySets` first), each with a `✓ zónában` / `▲ a mai edzéssel zónába érsz` / `⚠ a mai edzéssel túlmennél a kereten` status hint (the four `WeekZoneStatus` values) — where **`below` splits in two by `setsToZone` (`mezo-zr6p`)**: `→ a héten még +N szett jön a terv szerint` (neutral — the week's plan still covers MEV, `setsToZone === 0`, N = `remainingPlanSets`) vs `↓ a heti terv is a zóna alatt — még N szett kellene` (the whole week's plan misses MEV, i.e. the planner card's own `under` level). Before that split every `below` row read `még N szett hiányzik a héten`, which contradicted `SetBudgetCard`'s `✓ optimális zónában` for the same group on day 1 of the week (the prep card scores done+today only, the planner card scores the full week's plan); renders nothing while `useWeekMuscleLog().pending` or with no active meso — → the **niggle pre-flag** → **`ChallengesCarousel`** (below) → the **warmup block** → **muscle-region-sectioned** `PrepExerciseCard`s → the sticky **`⚡ Kezdjük el →`** CTA. Every one of those subjects still exists; the mosaic just gave each its own page. `PrepHero`, `PrepExerciseCard` and `ChallengesCarousel` are the three components the re-face left behind — still in the tree with their tests, but with **no production import** (§9).

The hero's numbers come from `logic/prepBriefing.ts` (pure view-data adapters — no hooks, no fetching), fed by `useTrain`'s existing `activeMeso`/`exerciseRecords` and `useProgressionProfile().athletic`:
- **XP/skill forecast (D2)** — `prepForecast(day, athletic)` wraps the existing single-day `growthForecast({days:[day], slots:[], runSessions:[], athletic})` (gym-only estimate, `slots`/`runSessions` empty). `day` is **today's real `MesoDay`** (`activeMeso?.days?.find(d => d.current)`) **only when it's genuinely the plan on screen** — an **id-subset gate** (`isPlannedDay`: every meso-day exercise id is present in `W.exercises`) — else `pseudoDayFromPlan(W)` adapts the `WorkoutPlan` itself into a `MesoDay` shape, covering custom/saját templates, no-meso sessions, and a defensive fallback should the meso day ever drift from what's actually rendered. The ring/skill rows are **honestly null-hidden** (not zero-rendered) when the progression profile is empty AND every forecast skill's `xpEst` is 0 — never a fabricated ring from no real data. The stat pill (szett/rep/perc/izomcsoport) is a separate, always-live `prepStats(W)` sum over `W.exercises` — it renders even when `forecast` is null.
- **1RM badge (D3)** — `oneRmByIdentity(exerciseRecords)` maps each record to a `catalogId`-else-name identity key (`identityKeyOf`, the same records/challenge-grounding idiom as `ExerciseRecordService.findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted`) → rounded `bestE1rm` kg; a record with no `bestE1rm` (bodyweight-only history) is simply **omitted**, never zero-stated.
- **Starting-weight pill (D4)** — `startWeightOf(e)` reads the exercise's first **working** `prescribedSets` entry's `targetWeightKg` (the engine's TODAY recommendation) — hidden (no pill) when null (plyo / first session with no anchor / hypertrophy-drive switch off); distinct from `e.anchorWeightKg`, the established anchor `pseudoDayFromPlan` forecasts FROM.
- **Exercise sectioning** — a local `groupExercisesByRegion(W.exercises)` (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`, not the `muscleWeekFromMeso`/`muscleRegionGroups` MuscleWeekSheet helper) groups by the `muscleColors` region family (`muscleRegion`/`REGION_LABELS`, the same 6-region system as the Heti zone panel (the former Gym meta-card), `mezo-ly27`) in **first-appearance plan order** (not the sheet's fixed region order); a muscle with no mapped region (e.g. a custom/saját `'full'`-type exercise) falls into a single **`Egyéb`** catch-all bucket so nothing is ever silently dropped. Each exercise renders as a bigger **`PrepExerciseCard`** (`components/PrepExerciseCard.tsx`): a muscle-color rail + name + muscle pill, the optional top-right **🏆 {kg} kg / 1RM REKORD** badge, an accepted-challenge accent line (sparkle + `{typeLabel} · {target}`, ported from the old flat list) when an accepted challenge targets that exercise, and a pill row — 🔥 `{n} bemelegítő` (hidden at 0 warmup sets) · `{n} × working` · rep range · RIR · the `↑ {kg} kg-ról indul` starting-weight pill above.

**Challenge pending state (D5, an `mezo-hbwi` follow-up).** `useChallenges` (`data/train/challengeHooks.ts`) now returns a **`pending: boolean`** alongside `challenges`/`mode` — real mode: the list query's `isPending` (the lazy backend LLM generation in flight); mock: always `false`. **The three states survived the mosaic re-face onto `PrepKuldetesekPage`** (`pages/prep/PrepKuldetesekPage.tsx`, `mezo-d20.3.8`), in the same priority order: **pending** → a `SkeletonText` placeholder — closing the old silent-gap bug where an empty-during-generation list looked identical to a resolved "no quests" state; **resolved empty** → the honest `"Ma nincs kihívás"` line; **else** the `ChallengeCard` list (vertical stack, not the old horizontal scroll-snap rail) with its `⚔️ Elfogadom`/`Passz` actions and the existing „tanulom"/outcome-chip states (unchanged, [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md)). The page's `PageHero` carries `{accepted}/{total}` and its `StatStrip` `ajánlat ma` / `elfogadva`, and the principle line says out loud that passing is fine — the ADR 0010 tone. The old `ChallengesCarousel` (its `⚔️ A mai küldetések · {n}` eyebrow, subline and dot-pager) is the component this replaced; it is now unreferenced (§9).

**Layout — the calm logging default (`mezo-d20.3.9`, the last Edzés slice; reshaping Napiv S5 `mezo-8141` + the v4 table `mezo-8xmf` without touching one behavior).** The `active` phase is a full-bleed session screen: the route (`/train/session`) hides the bottom `TabBar` (`AppLayout.tsx` — `hideChrome` on the route string, the `wk-top` header owns its own back affordance, §5), replacing it with a sticky **`.wk-top`** header — a back pill, the title + `n/m gyakorlat · x/y szett` counter and the `⋯` actions chip — then a **`.wkx-progressbar`** strip (one flex segment per exercise, weighted by its planned set count, colored by `muscleColor(ex.muscle).rail` — pure derivation `sessionProgressSegments`, below — opacity 1 done / 0.45 current / 0.25 upcoming), with the **`.exdots`** per-exercise dots now on their own centered row rather than crammed into the header. Below that a single **`.excard.wkx-excard`** — still the sole live-logging surface, now the ONLY expanded surface on the screen.

**The organising principle is „a kártyán logolsz, a sávokban utánanézel"** — you log on the card, you look things up in the strips. Inside the card, top to bottom: ① **`.wkx-exhead`** — the `.exo` eyebrow (`{idx}/{n} · {izomlabel} · {type}`, family-deep) + a **single-line** `h2` name, with the media as small round **icon buttons** on the right (`⛶` still image, `▶` demo video — both hidden-until-asked, and the video now reveals *inline* instead of behind `VideoDemo`'s own second chip); ② a muted **`.wkx-metaline`** (`🔥 Failure` / `🌿 Volume · {repMin}–{repMax} · RIR {n}` from `setStyle(targetRIR)`, plus a dashed challenge chip when one targets this exercise) — this single line replaced BOTH the old 3-cell `.wkx-statstrip` AND the full-width "Aktív kihívás" banner; ③ the one-line durable note pill; ④ **`.wkx-logbox`** — the one white panel that holds everything you type: a `.wkx-logtop` slot label carrying its own target (`Logolás · B1 · cél 52,5 × 8`) beside a live `{done}/{working} szett` counter, the **`.setdots`** (one dot per planned+extra set: done `✓`, current family-colored, a pending warmup labelled `B{n}` via `.wu`, a pending extra/added set dashed via `.extra`) + the mono `warmupPctLabel` note (`B{n} = {pct}% · {kg} ✓` for the last LOGGED warmup), the giant `SetStepper` pair, the RIR row (**hidden on a warmup set** — effort tracking is working-set-only, `mezo-eerq`; the logged warmup also *omits* `rir` from the `SetLogRequest` payload) with its `🔥 bukásig!` / `🌿 hagyj 2 rep tartalékot` hint and an `L/B/R` Side row on isolation exercises, the per-set note **collapsed behind `＋ megjegyzés a szetthez`**, and finally the **`.donebtn`** (`Szett kész ✓`) **or, while a rest is active, the `RestTimerBar` in the same slot** (`mezo-xt65`). Where the CTA used to simply vanish there is now an explicit **`✓ Minden szett megvan ennél a gyakorlatnál`** line — the CTA stays cursor-gated (`mezo-l3on`: a free-navigated, already-complete exercise must not offer a set that renders nowhere yet counts toward the summary), while a rest genuinely still running keeps its bar regardless.

**Reference content moved into two thin `CollapsibleStrip`s** (`@/shared/ui/mozaik`), whose CLOSED headers already carry the summary — `⚡ Progresszió · +2,5 kg ▾` and `Szettek · 1/5 ✓ · 420 kg ▾` — so nothing has to be opened to know the state. The **`ProgressionBanner`** lives inside the first (with a new `bare` mode so the strip header isn't echoed), which is also the **one place the last-week comparison now lives** — the old `.wkx-subrow` is gone. The **v4 set-list table** lives inside the second. The plain **`.aistrip`** rationale banner (first-session/anchor exercise: `rationale` present, `progression` null) stays uncollapsed — one muted sentence is already calm. The two-way **`.pagerbar`** (‹ Előző / Következő › with each neighbour's live `n/m`, `mezo-cd8s`) sits outside the card as before.

**Muscle-family theming (`mezo-8xmf`) survived intact:** the card sets `--fam-rail`/`--fam-wash`/`--fam-deep` inline from `muscleColor(current.muscle)` (`logic/muscleColors.ts`) — a wash gradient + a 5px rail + a radial glow — and every family-tinted element (the CTA gradient, the active RIR pill, the current set-dot glow, the set table's target pill) re-themes on every exercise change. **`Szett kész ✓` still morphs into the in-card rest countdown** whenever the exercise continues — and the rest timer ships its **real** duration; the prototype's 10× demo speed was deliberately not carried over. **Two deliberate deviations from the prototype, both for the 44pt rule:** the stepper ± buttons stay 44px (prototype 28px) and the RIR/Oldal buttons keep a 44px min-height (prototype 26px). The zone-derivation pure helpers (`warmupPctLabel`/`setStatus`/`exerciseTonnage`/`topSetDeltaPct`/`avgWorkingRir`/`sessionProgressSegments`) live in `logic/workoutCardMeta.ts` — no hooks, no server call, tested in `workoutCardMeta.test.ts` (§10).

**Kept verbatim by the re-face**, and worth stating because a face change this large invites the opposite assumption: the 5-way navigation (dots / pager / `ExerciseOverviewSheet` / swipe / `⋯`), the medal toast, the `⋯` action sheet (reorder-with-handover, skip, `＋ Szett` with its `Csak ma` / `Minden hétre` prompt, the durable note, the early finish), set-table row edit+delete with the one-slot floor, the RP debrief, and every honest state — nothing self-completes, misses are never red, inert affordances stay inert. Summary/level-up were already the designed screens (`mezo-w943` + `LevelUpScreen`); the closing report only gained the prototype's quiet principle line.

**Prescribed sets (hypertrophy drive, `mezo-dhdr` + `mezo-anm4` anchor; presentation reshaped by S5, then by the v4 table `mezo-8xmf`).** When `mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled` is on, `GET /workouts/today` carries a per-exercise `prescribedSets: PrescribedSet[]` + a `rationale` string (§4) — this engine contract is unchanged. **Pre-S5 (`mezo-gphs`) the active phase rendered all sets up front as a list** — warmup rows first (amber `Bemel.` chip) then working rows (teal `Working`), each pre-filled to its target, with the **current row expanding in place** to a `CompactStepper` + RIR + `Set kész` panel — the list itself was the single logging surface. **Napiv S5 demoted the list to plain status rows** (target-or-actual + a kind tag), and **the v4 redesign (`mezo-8xmf`) turned it into a real, strict table — `.wkx-slist`**, since `mezo-d20.3.9` rendered **inside the `Szettek` `CollapsibleStrip`** (whose closed header already carries `{cursor}/{count} ✓ · {tonnage} kg`, so opening it is optional) with a `sorra koppintva szerkeszthető` hint: a **`.wkx-shead`** header shows the eyebrow `Szettek` + a right-aligned family-tinted **`.wkx-tgt`** pill carrying the exercise-level target ONCE — `cél: {repMin}–{repMax} rep · RIR {targetRIR} {🔥|🌿}` — instead of repeating it on every row (the pre-v4 kind-tag layout's main noise source). The table itself: a header row (`SZETT`/`kg`/`ism`/`RIR`/blank status column, fixed widths, right-aligned mono numerals) then one **`.wkx-srow`** `<button>` per set (same tap-to-edit contract as before, below) — a **`.wkx-mark`** circle per row (`.wkx-mark-warm` amber-filled `B{n}` / `.wkx-mark-done` family-filled / `.wkx-mark-cur` outlined+glow on the current (next-to-log) row / `.wkx-mark-pend` plain), KG/ISM/RIR showing the **logged actual** on a done row or a **ghosted TARGET** on a pending one (a pending working row's rep cell shows the exercise's own `repMin–repMax` RANGE, not the engine's single `targetReps` — a pending warmup shows its own single target, since a warmup's reps aren't range-bound), and a **status cell** driven by the pure `setStatus(ex, {reps,kind})` (`logic/workoutCardMeta.ts`, below): sage `✓` when a logged working row's reps fall in `[repMin,repMax]`, amber-deep `▼ cél alatt`/`▲ cél felett` outside it, family-deep `MOST ↑` on the current (not-yet-logged) row, plain `✓` for a done warmup (no range to violate) — a RECORD-tier `MedalChip` still appends into the same status cell when the row earned one (§5 `Medálok`, below). Row washes: `.wkx-srow-warm` (faint amber) on a warmup row, `.wkx-srow-cur` (faint family wash) on the current row. A **`.wkx-sfoot`** footer closes the table with the exercise-level summary, also shown ONCE: `Volumen` = `exerciseTonnage` (Σ weight×reps over ALL logged sets, warmups included) in mono kg; `vs múlt hét` = `topSetDeltaPct` (± rounded % of the top logged working weight vs `lastWeek.weightKg`, sage positive / amber negative, `–` with no `lastWeek`); `Átl. RIR` = `avgWorkingRir` (mean logged working RIR, 1 decimal, mono). **The pre-v4 medal "double-tick" (a `TARGET_HIT` medal recoloring the row's done-check sage instead of coral) is gone** — the v4 status cell already conveys hit/miss directly via the rep-range comparison above, so a `TARGET_HIT` medal (still evaluated server/mock-side) no longer drives any visual of its own; only `RECORD`-tier medals still render a chip. Logging itself still happens once, in the `.excard` above, never per-row in this table.

**A szett-sorok szerkeszthetők (`mezo-l3on`; a sorok maga a v4 táblázat `.wkx-srow` gombjai, `mezo-8xmf`).** Az aktív fázisban minden sor gomb: koppintásra a `SetEditSheet` nyílik, amely a logolás saját beviteli elemeit hozza (`SetStepper` súly/ismétlés + RIR sor working szetten + Side izolációs gyakorlaton + szett-megjegyzés). Logolt soron `Mentés ✓` és `Szett törlése`; még nem logolt sloton a stepperek a célértéket mutatják letiltva, és csak a törlés érhető el. **A törlés a slotot is elviszi** (4 tervezettből 3 lesz, a mögöttes sorok előrelépnek), gyakorlatonként **egy slot padlóval** — az utolsónál a gomb inaktív, a gyakorlat elhagyására a `Kihagyás` való. A szerkesztés/törlés kizárólag az **aktív** instance-on él (a `/train/review/:id` és a `complete` fázis változatlan); a záró lapról a `← Vissza az edzéshez` visszavisz. A slot-darabszám — a `＋ Szett` `extra`-jához hasonlóan — **kliens-állapot**, egy edzés közbeni újratöltés visszahozza a tervezett darabszámot; a logolt szettek javítása/törlése viszont perzisztens.

`makeSession`/`seedFromOpen` fold the recipe (`warmupSets + workingSets`) into `session.planned` + a typed `prescribed[]`, and the pure helper `prescribedAt(session, exerciseId, idx)` (`logic/workoutState.ts:56`) resolves each row's target; the current set's `SetStepper` pair (pre-S5: `CompactStepper`) **seeds from its prescribed target** rather than from last week, and the old static "+2.5 kg" hint is **replaced by the engine `rationale`** (surfaced via `ProgressionBanner` when a progression signal exists, else the `.aistrip` banner — both above). **The prefill inherits within the exercise (`mezo-eerq`)**: a working set prefers the **just-logged working set's weight** over the static engine target (a mid-session deviation carries forward; the engine seeds only the *first* working set), and a **null target weight** (first session, no anchor) falls back to the previous logged set's weight instead of resetting to 0 — only the warmup ramp (per-set distinct targets) overrides inheritance. Reps/RIR still follow the target (goal-seeking), inheriting only in the no-prescription ad-hoc path. **Only genuinely load-less (`plyo`) sets hide the kg stepper** (reps-only) — a first-workout null recommendation still shows the stepper for manual entry (the starting weight comes from the plan-time **`Kiinduló kg` anchor**, `mezo-anm4`), fixing the earlier bug where a null target also hid the input. When the switch is off, `prescribedSets`/`rationale` are null and the screen falls back to ad-hoc logging exactly as before (no pre-fill). The prescription is computed from **completed-instance history only** (with the anchor as the pre-history base), so it is stable within a live session and resume-safe (§9).

**Progression signal — RIR-aware, deload-aware (Progressive Overload Plan 1, `mezo-5pfe`).** The engine no longer just holds/bumps load off the rep count — it now also reads the last completed working set's **RIR** and the active meso's **deload-week** state, and emits a structured `TodayExercise.progression: ProgressionSignal | null` beside `prescribedSets`/`rationale` (the corrected double-progression matrix lives in §4; this paragraph is the UI it drives). When the viewed exercise carries a `progression`, the **`ProgressionBanner`** (`components/ProgressionBanner.tsx`) renders instead of the plain `.aistrip` — since `mezo-d20.3.9` inside the `⚡ Progresszió` `CollapsibleStrip`, in a new `bare` mode so the strip header (which already shows the delta chip) isn't echoed: a `⚡ Progresszió` label + a **delta chip** (`+2,5 kg ↑` in coral for a weight bump, `+1 rep ↑` in sage for a rep build, `tartás` in amber for hold/deload), a two-cell **`Múlt hét → Ma a cél`** comparison (left cell from the exercise's existing `lastWeek`, right cell the new target weight×reps), and the engine's HU `rationale` line — three tones keyed off `progression.lever` (`po-weight`/`po-rep`/`po-hold` in `prototype.css`, deload sharing the hold/amber tone). When `progression` is null (first session / anchor-only, no real history yet) the exercise still falls back to the **unchanged** plain `.aistrip` line, exactly as before this change. The **prep screen** surfaces the same signal ahead of the workout, now one tile-page down: `PrepGyakorlatokPage`'s `PrepExerciseTile` renders a compact **delta chip** in its `múlt héten → ma` footer row whenever `progression` carries a non-null `deltaKg`/`deltaReps` (hold/no-op progressions show no chip — both are null for `hold`), and the day-level **`⚡ Túlterhelés: n× +súly · m× +rep`** summary — sourced from the `TodayExercise`-sibling `overloadSummary`, hidden when both counts are 0 and each half omitted independently — moved from `PrepHero` onto `PrepFejlodesPage` (`overload` prop). **Mock mode is not gated out**: `data/train/train.ts`'s `trainWorkout` fixture now seeds representative `progression`/`overloadSummary` values (one weight-lever, one rep-lever exercise + a day-level `overloadSummary`), so the banner and both chips render in the prototype exactly like real mode — no special-casing needed in the hook. **The daily overload challenge (Progressive Overload Plan 3, `mezo-gj42`) is the last progressive-overload surface**: the day's biggest jump above doesn't just drive `ProgressionBanner`/the prep chips — it ALSO becomes a deterministic proactive **`⚡ Túlterhelés`** challenge card in the prep carousel (a guaranteed +1 alongside the LLM-generated PR/Depth/Volume challenges), completing the progressive-overload checklist (Plan 1 → Plan 2 → Plan 3). Generator/evaluator/display mechanism: [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md).

**Rest timer (in-card CTA-morph, `mezo-xt65` — replaced the S5 `mezo-8141` Live-Activity island).** `Szett kész ✓` calls `rest.start(restSecondsFor(current.type))` whenever more sets remain on the **same** exercise (`completeSet`'s `else` branch) — never on the exercise's last set, where the RP debrief (`feedbackEx`) takes over instead and no rest starts. Duration (`features/train/logic/restTimer.ts`): a **hardcoded** 150s for `compound`, 90s for everything else — **there is no rest-duration data field anywhere** in the model or DB (§9). The state is **page-local**: `useRestTimer` (`features/train/logic/useRestTimer.ts`) holds `idle | running {endsAt,total} | paused {pausedRemaining,total}`, self-ticks a 500ms interval off `endsAt` while running and self-clears at 0; `pause` freezes a whole-second remaining, `resume` re-anchors `endsAt` from it. While the status is non-idle, the excard's CTA slot renders **`RestTimerBar`** (`features/train/components/RestTimerBar.tsx`, `.restbar`) instead of `.donebtn` — pixel-matched height/radius, so the morph causes **zero layout shift**: a draining coral fill (paused: greyed `--faint`), a `Pihenő`/`Szünetel` + `m:ss` label (in `var(--ink)` — readable over both the fill and the exposed track), and two round buttons, ⏸/▶ (`Pihenő szüneteltetése`/`folytatása`) and ⏭ (`Pihenő kihagyása`). **The bar body is deliberately inert** — the old island's tap-to-skip was an accidental-skip hazard; only the explicit buttons act. There is **no "next" label** (mid-exercise the next set is visible right above; the old island's dead-code "next" finding is resolved by removal). The rest is the user's, not the exercise's: free navigation to another exercise keeps the bar in that exercise's CTA slot, so logging elsewhere mid-rest costs one ⏭ tap. Exercise skip (`handleSkip`), every exit path (`onExit`), and the summary/complete phases call `rest.skip()`; unmount needs no cleanup because the state dies with the page. A mid-rest reload loses the rest (in-memory only, unchanged from the island).

### `Gyakorlatok` — catalog + records (`pages/ExercisesPage.tsx`, `/train/exercises`)

**Mozaik tile pass (`mezo-d20.3.3`) — face only, zero behavior change.** The DS-migrated cards below now carry the **domain wash on the card itself** (handoff §10, „tile pass") and the record/ghost list plays the one-shot rise-stagger (`EntranceGroup` + `.rise`) instead of settling static. The page also gained the prototype's compact hero (clay icon + catalog count) and an honest **3-cell stat strip** (`rekord` / `saját` / `videóval`) under the head. **The prototype's 4th `PR e héten` cell was deliberately dropped** — no dated week-boundary contract exists to derive it truthfully, and every dual-mode behavioral test (dual-mode list, two-level muscle filter, ghost rows, gated roundels, sheets) passed unchanged.

Default state "Top gyakorlatok · rekordjaid" ranked by `sessionCount`. Searching or muscle-filtering shows the full catalog: record rows first, then dashed ghost rows for catalog items with no history. **Since the PR-card redesign (`mezo-kaui`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-pr-card-redesign-design.md`) both row kinds are variant-A three-zone cards** on a **muscle-color system**: a 5px rail + wash/deep pill tints keyed by muscle via `logic/muscleColors.ts` (**21 live head/zone-specific muscles** since `mezo-wu1s` + legacy coarse keys `back`/`chest`/`shoulder`/`lats`/`rear-delt`/`biceps`/`triceps` — still emitted by the volume log — → 6 existing token families: chest*=coral, back*/traps=sky, shoulder*=lav, biceps*/triceps*=rose, legs=sage, core=amber; neutral fallback). The 21 tokens grouped by region live in `muscleColors.ts` `REGION_MUSCLES`/`LIVE_MUSCLES` — the single source for both the picker filter and the authoring picker. Card zones: **header** (rank plaque in muscle wash — top-list state only — + display name), **tag row** (muscle, type — `plyo` gets the card's only *filled* stamp, `--amber` bg + warm-ink text —, `N alkalom`, plus `Saját` on user-authored rows), **stat strip** (3 hairline-divided cells: weighted `Legjobb szett / e1RM (coral) / Összvolumen`; bodyweight `Max rep / Összes rep / Szettek`). **DS-migrated in `mezo-setx.6.7`** onto the `.excat*` family ([\_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) §3): h3 title, DS Tag stamps, eyebrow stat labels, sp-4 padding, DS chip filters and the DS SearchInput. Three structural changes came with it, each fixing a rule violation rather than a taste call: the **`▶`/`⋯` roundels moved out of the absolute header overlay into a trailing action column**, which is what lets them carry real 48dp targets without stealing the title's line (the old 26px roundels sat at half the minimum); the **ghost row is no longer wrapped in a button** — with no record there is nothing to open, and a dead button promised a sheet that could not appear; and the **muscle rail is an element, not a thick leading border** — a 5px border under the 22px card radius curves into a leaf at the corners, which reads as a rendering bug on the dashed ghost card. The stat labels **wrap to two lines by design**: at the DS eyebrow role (12/700/0.22em) `LEGJOBB SZETT` is wider than its third of the card, so `.excat-stats .eyebrow` drops `nowrap` and reserves two lines to keep all three values on one baseline. **Bodyweight detection requires an actual load** (`bestSet.weightKg > 0`): the live backend logs bodyweight sets as `weightKg: 0` (SetLogRequest requires the field), so `bestSet`/`bestE1rm` arrive as `0` rather than the contract-documented absent shape — both shapes render the rep branch (MSW fixtures cover both: Box Jump absent-shape, Dead Hang zero-shape). Ghost rows keep the dashed frame + STIM meter (ticks in the muscle rail color). Tapping a record card opens `ExerciseRecordSheet`. A header **`+ Új gyakorlat`** action (`.pgact`, top-right = the low-frequency slot per DS anti-pattern 19; the frequent action here is search) opens **`CatalogExerciseSheet`** (`sheets/CatalogExerciseSheet.tsx`), the authoring form — name / muscle (**21-token region-grouped picker**, `mezo-wu1s`) / type (`compound|isolation|plyo`) / stim + fatigue decimal steppers (0–1, step 0.05) / demo-video URL — that **creates a user-authored catalog exercise** (`createCatalogExercise`). Your own rows are **editable + deletable**: the card's **`⋯` roundel** (editable rows only) opens the same sheet in edit mode (`edit` prop → `updateCatalogExercise`), and **delete lives inside that sheet** as a two-tap-confirm `Gyakorlat törlése` action (`deleteCatalogExercise`) — the old detached `RowActions` bar under each row is **deleted**. Built-in (master) rows are read-only for the full edit (the write is server-rejected, §4). **The demo video is the exception: every catalog row (master or user) carries a round `▶` button on the card** (gated on a backend `catalogId` so mock/static rows show none; muscle-wash colored when a video exists, neutral when not) opening **`VideoUrlSheet`** (`sheets/VideoUrlSheet.tsx`) — a one-field URL editor calling `setExerciseVideo` (the ownership-free `PUT /…/{id}/video`, §4), with an **Eltávolítás** action that clears an existing video; so built-in exercises get demo videos too, even though their name/muscle/etc. stay locked (`mezo-bnsk`). An inline **demo video** (`VideoDemo`, `components/VideoDemo.tsx`) — a `▶ Demo` chip that tap-to-reveals a lazy-mounted **youtube-nocookie** 16:9 iframe (renders nothing without a recognized YouTube URL) — appears in **three places**: the active workout (on the current exercise, no navigation away), this `Gyakorlatok` browser (via `ExerciseRecordSheet`), and the mesocycle builder's exercise picker (`ExercisePickerSheet`).

### `Medálok` — the medal cabinet (`pages/MedalsPage.tsx`, `/train/medals`, `mezo-wp6n`)
A read-only, dated history of every medal derived from the owner's logged sets — reached from the Edzés
hub's **`Medálok`** tile (the `s-medal` clay spot). **Mozaik 2.0 re-face (`mezo-d20.3.2`):** a compact
`PageHero` (the `s-medal` spot + the total + an `ebből N e hónapban` sub) over `.mz-facttile` cabinet rows
carrying a `REKORD`/`CÉL` chip — the grouping, ordering and every data read are verbatim from before.
Nothing here is a life-domain badge (the 9
`Kitüntetések` on `/me/growth` are untouched, see [growth.md](growth.md)); medals stay train-domain.
Rows are grouped by date, newest first, within-day order exactly as `GET /api/train/medals` returns it
(§4) — each row names the exercise, the medal type, the achieved value, and what it beat
(`previousValue`/`previousDate`, always null on a `TARGET_HIT`). Because the
four RECORD types (`WEIGHT`/`REPS_AT_WEIGHT`/`E1RM`/`SESSION_VOLUME`) are **replayed over the entire
existing set history** rather than stored ([ADR 0016](../decisions/0016-medals-derived-not-materialized.md)), the cabinet is populated the first time it's
opened with medals the user never saw live — the cabinet copy says so honestly, calling the history
reconstructed rather than implying it was tracked all along. `TARGET_HIT` cannot be backfilled the same
way (its prescription snapshot didn't exist before this change, §4), so it only accrues from the ship
date forward. Medals also surface live during and right after a workout — see the `Active workout`
subsection below and §4 — this page is the durable, browsable record of all of them.

### `Sport` — volleyball · cross · TRX (`pages/SportPage.tsx`, `/train/sport`)

**Design 2.0 did NOT re-face this page** — it is a full-page sibling of the Edzés hub that keeps its own `.pghead-np` head (`Edzés · Sport`, h1 `Röplabda`, a `+ Log` chip). `mezo-d20.3.4` spent its budget on **load-bearing fixes + the designed additions** the prototype/README checklist called for instead: the `Napló` session cards' type tag was hardcoded to `stag-sport` (a cross or TRX session read `RÖPI`) and now grades kind-correctly through `sportOf`/`SPORT_TAGS`/`SPORT_TONE`, with the fact row branching `Setek (röpi)` vs `Körök (cross/TRX)` off a new `SportSession.rounds` field threaded through the API mapper and the mock cache; the big **RPE readout grades off RPE itself** (7+ coral, 8+ amber, **never red**) rather than falling back through `intensity`; today's `Heti terv` slot gained an inline **`Logold ›`** chip that pre-selects that slot's sport when opening `SportLogSheet`; and `SportLogSheet` gained the designed **notes textarea** — `SportSessionCreateRequest.notes` had existed on the contract with no UI surfacing it.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.5`).** `.page-header` + `PageTitle`/`Eyebrow` head with a `.pgact` `+ Log`; the hero borrows `.todaycard`'s gradient formula/radius/padding/shadow; `SportStat` renders the shared **StatStrip cell** vocabulary (the same move `GymStat` made in `.6.4`), keeping its rose `highlight` because a stat VALUE is data-viz — the one band where the domain accents are still legal ([ADR 0018](../decisions/0018-adopt-exist-zen-design-system.md) D5). **The view switcher is the point of the bead:** it was three 10px uppercase buttons tinted in the sport rose, which D5 forbids on buttons. It becomes the new shared **`.segtabs`/`.segtab`** control, whose selected segment speaks **primary** like every other selected control in the app. Session rows move to the ListItem voice (16/600 time, 14px meta), the `MA`/`EGYSZERI` markers to DS Tag stamps, and the `+ Egyszeri esemény` footer to the shared `.dashedcta`.
Hero with week stats (Sessions/Idő/RPE/Váll) and a 3-button switcher: **`Heti terv`** (7-day recurring schedule, editable in real mode via `SportScheduleSheet`), **`Napló`** (logged sessions), **`Cross-load`** (impact rows). Header chip "+ Log" opens `SportLogSheet`. **`SportScheduleSheet` is a per-day slot-list editor** (`mezo-05v6`, rewritten from a one-slot-per-day draft): each of the 7 days holds an independent **list** of slots (`+ Sport hozzáadása` adds another), every slot carrying its own sport selector (Röpi | Cross | TRX, the same chip idiom as `SportLogSheet`) plus time/duration/location/intensity — so a day can hold any number of slots, including two of the same sport (e.g. a noon TRX + an evening volleyball match). **`kind` (training|match) stays volleyball-only**: the kind selector is hidden for cross/TRX slots, which always save `kind: 'training'` (no DB/contract change to `kind`). Saving emits the full slot list to `PUT /api/train/sport-schedule` (full-replace), unchanged. The switcher's selected segment is **sticky** (`useStickyTab('train.sport.view')`), so leaving and returning restores it rather than snapping to `Heti terv`. Re-skinned to the Napiv rose `--wash-sport`/`--tag-sport` vocabulary (S4, `mezo-8141`) — superseding the pre-S4 `--cat-tendency` (pink) accent. **`Heti terv` renders every slot of a day** (`SportWeekView`, `SportPage.tsx:247`): a day card stacks all of that day's slots (e.g. a Tue cross/TRX session + a Tue volleyball match) rather than only the first match, each slot's time/duration/MA-chip/role-location row tagged with a `.stag-sport` chip (`SPORT_TAGS[sportOf(session)]`) whenever it's not volleyball (volleyball, the implicit default, stays untagged); `isToday` is now `daySlots.some((s) => s.today)`. The hero eyebrow reads **`Heti ritmus · {weeklyHours}h`** (the `court` suffix was dropped since a mixed week's hours are no longer purely court time) — `weeklyHours` is a plain JS number with no `hu-HU` locale formatting, so e.g. `2.5` renders with a dot, not a comma. **One-off (non-recurring) events (`mezo-e1sp`):** below the week list (and below the no-schedule ghost too) the `Heti terv` view renders a **`SportEventsSection`** — the upcoming (today+future) one-off events with per-row delete, plus the single dashed **`+ Egyszeri esemény`** entry point opening **`SportEventSheet`** (`sheets/SportEventSheet.tsx`: date defaulting to today + sport selector + time/duration/location; volleyball defaults `kind: 'match'`, cross/TRX force `'training'` like the schedule editor). Saved events persist via `POST /api/train/sport-events` (§4) and the **current week's** events merge into `sport.schedule` in `trainHooks` (`mergeEventsIntoSchedule`), so a one-off lands on its day card here (tagged `EGYSZERI`), on `Mai`/`Heti`, and in the fuel day-plan/calorie budget with **zero extra wiring** — its `today` flag is **date-based**, and `weeklyHours` deliberately excludes one-offs (the eyebrow describes the recurring rhythm). Works in **both modes** (mock emulates the server in the client-owned `['train','sportEvents']` cache). `SportScheduleSheet`'s `initial` filters `oneOff` sessions out so the full-replace weekly editor can never convert an event into a recurring slot.

### `Futás` — running (`pages/RunningPage.tsx`, `/train/futas`)

**Same story as Sport: a full-page sibling on its pre-redesign `.pghead-np` face, with `mezo-d20.3.5` spent on load-bearing fixes rather than a re-face.** Four of them: the `E heti edzés` session cards got a real **three-way CTA** keyed on `blockId + week + sessionKey` — today ⇒ `Naplózd`, a past weekday ⇒ **`Pótold`** (retroactive, logging against that weekday's real calendar date via the new `dateForDayOfWeek`), a future one ⇒ a disabled grey `Naplózás ▸`, already-logged ⇒ a `KÉSZ ✓` badge — where the page previously collapsed all of that into a binary onLog/no-onLog toggle; **pyramid session pills** regained the honest derived-rest note (`pihenő = szakasz × 2`) beside the `15／30／45` work ladder, which had been silently dropped; `RunLogSheet`'s **`Teljesített körök` stepper now shows for pyramid sessions too**, not only sprint — the FE half of the `completedRounds` scoring bug (F6.3 owns the backend half), defaulting to the week's prescribed segment count when no explicit round count exists; and `Napló` gained the **pulzus-megnyugvás (HR-recovery) trend chart** off the already-existing `hrRecoverySec` field. The RPE-target chip's hot (sprint 9+) tint also moved onto `color-mix` over `--error` instead of a hardcoded rose rgba that never matched the token.

**DS-migrated (`mezo-setx.6.6`).** Same treatment as `Sport`, and deliberately the same shape: DS head, the shared **`.segtabs`** switcher (sky off the buttons, primary on the selected segment), stat labels on the StatStrip label voice, session titles at 16/600, the run-type and status markers on the DS Tag spec, and the Fraunces `.text-meta-sm` for the empty-state asides. The two pages ship in one change because `.6.6` consumes the `.segtabs` class `.6.5` introduces — splitting them would have meant a branch whose control does not exist yet.
3-segment switcher: **`E heti edzés`** (active block hero with `RunWeekStrip` + prescribed `RunSessionCard`s — each now shows its `timeOfDay` next to the weekday — + `RunCrossLoadCard`), **`Napló`** (logged runs newest-first), **`Tervek`** (Aktív/Tervezett/Archív library; "+ Új terv" → builder). Re-skinned to the Napiv sky `--wash-run`/`--tag-run` vocabulary (S4, `mezo-8141`) — superseding the pre-S4 `--info` (blue) accent; the builder (below), `WeekdayGrid`, and `RunWeekEditor` were migrated off the legacy `--info` alias onto the semantic `--sky` accent in the S8 cleanup (`mezo-mifi`) — they carry the mid-hue accent rather than the full `--tag-run`/`--wash-run` vocabulary. The switcher's selected segment is **sticky** (`useStickyTab('train.futas.view')`), so creating a plan from `Tervek` and pressing the builder's breadcrumb `← Futás` returns to `Tervek`, not the default `E heti edzés` (mezo-0h9). The **builder** (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx`, `/train/futas/:id`) edits title/goal, a **1–8 week** add/remove row (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:153-177`), and the selected week via `RunWeekEditor`. Editing is **auto-saved** — no Save button; a debounced write (`:65-69`) persists ~600 ms after the last keystroke and flushes on back, with a `Mentés…`/`✓ Mentve`/`Nem mentve` status pill (`:126-128`). Lifecycle collapses to a **single status-dependent CTA** (planned→`Aktiválás`, active→`Lezárás`) plus a `⋯` **overflow menu** (`OverflowMenu`, `:206`) carrying `Duplikálás`/`Törlés`. Each `RunWeekEditor` session is a **two-zone card** (`RunWeekEditor.tsx:59-92`): **Menetrend** (`WeekdayGrid` single-select weekday + a `type="time"` time-of-day input — both plan-level) over **Terhelés** (week-level load: sprint rounds+rest steppers / pyramid work-second pills). The old hardcoded "Kedd · Sprint" / "Péntek · Piramis" labels are gone — weekday + time are user-editable.

---

## 3. Architecture & data flow

The whole area flows through **two hooks**, both re-exported from the single data boundary `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts:202-203`:

```
View (features/train/**)
  → useTrain()      (data/train/trainHooks.ts)   |   useRunning()  (data/train/runningHooks.ts)
    → isMockMode()  (@/data/_client/mode → VITE_USE_MOCK !== 'false')
       ├─ MOCK: TanStack Query initialData = static fixtures (data/train/train.ts, data/train/running.ts)
       │        writes no-op (useTrain) OR emulate the server via queryClient.setQueryData (useRunning)
       └─ REAL: api client (data/train/trainApi.ts, data/train/runningApi.ts) over apiFetch → /api/train/*
            → TrainController.java (implements generated TrainApi)
              → {Train,Workout,Sport,ExerciseCatalog,ExerciseRecord,Running}Service
                → repositories → Postgres
```

**Invariants of the seam:**
- **Mock mode is synchronous** (`initialData` set) so the first render equals the Phase-1 static return — preserved for the visual baselines and component tests. **Real mode has NO static fallback**: an empty backend surfaces as `null`/`[]` and every view ghost-guards (the "T0 clean slate" rule, `2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md`). The full rationale is in the comment block at `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.ts:166-173`.
- **Mode is read inside hook bodies, never at module scope** (`frontend/src/data/_client/mode.ts`), so tests stub per-case with `vi.stubEnv`.
- Default mode is **mock** (`VITE_USE_MOCK !== 'false'`), but per `CLAUDE.md` the project runs FE in REAL mode by default in dev/test.
- Contract types are generated: `api/feature/train/train.yml` → merged `api/openapi.yml` → FE `frontend/src/data/_client/api.gen.ts` (re-exported as named types in `trainApi.ts`/`runningApi.ts`) and BE `io.mrkuhne.mezo.api.dto.*` + the `TrainApi` interface that `TrainController` implements.

**Real-mode mapping notes** (`trainHooks.ts`): `toWorkoutPlan` (`:51-94`) collapses the API response to the Phase-1 `WorkoutPlan` shape and sets `challenges: []` (live challenges are proactive-owned and sourced separately via `useChallenges` on `ActiveWorkoutPage`, not through `WorkoutPlan` — see §Active workout); since Progressive Overload Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`) it also null-coalesces each exercise's `progression` and the plan-level `overloadSummary` straight through from the wire (no shape change, just the two new optional fields); `deriveGymSchedule(meso, slots)` (`:100-116`) builds the gym week by joining the active meso's template days (WHAT) with the standalone weekly gym slots (WHEN) — `DAY_ORDER` index `==` `slot.dayOfWeek`, a gym day with no matching slot keeps `time: null`; `duration` stays `null` (no DB home, out of scope). The active-meso real fallback is `realActiveMeso ?? (mock ? activeMeso : null)` (`:565`).

---

## 4. Data model & API

All tables: UUID PKs (`gen_random_uuid()`), `created_by` ownership via `OwnedEntity`, soft delete via `@SQLDelete`/`@SQLRestriction`. Migrations live under `backend/src/main/resources/db/changelog/1.0.0/script/`. Generated DTOs live in `io.mrkuhne.mezo.api.dto.*`; never hand-write boundary DTOs. The API fragment is `api/feature/train/train.yml`.

### Mesocycles (planning)
- **Tables** (`202606111400_mezo-n5q_create_train.sql`; `mezo-7ot` adds `exercise.catalog_id` + plyo): `mesocycle`, `muscle_group_volume_log` (UNIQUE `(mesocycle_id, muscle)`, jsonb `source` = `ProvenanceEnvelope`), `workout_session` (template rows = `template_session_id IS NULL`), `exercise` (FK→session CASCADE, `catalog_id` FK→`exercise_catalog` SET NULL). **Template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)):** `202608161200_mezo-meyc.1_create_meso_template.sql` adds **`meso_template`** — plan metadata + **`days jsonb`** (typed `MesoDayJson`/`GymExerciseJson` records) + **`volume_per_muscle jsonb`** (typed `VolumeBaselineJson`), no dates/status; `202608161210_mezo-meyc.1_mesocycle_template_link.sql` adds `mesocycle.template_id UUID NULL` (FK `fk_mesocycle_template_id_meso_template_id`, nullable — legacy runs stay `null`) and `mesocycle.closed_at timestamptz NULL`. **Run report (`mezo-meyc.2`):** `202608161600_mezo-meyc.2_create_mesocycle_report.sql` adds **`mesocycle_report`** — 1:1 via `mesocycle_id` FK CASCADE (`fk_mesocycle_report_mesocycle`) + `uq_mesocycle_report_mesocycle`, typed jsonb `report`/`context` (both nullable; **`context` is written by the companion's `MesoContextAssembler` since S3, `mezo-meyc.3`** — §5 *Meso AI review*), `self_eval text` (the owner's close-time note, takes over the dead `mesocycle.summary`'s role — the old column is untouched, nothing new writes it), `ai_eval text` + `ai_eval_status text` (`pending|ready|failed`, `ck_mesocycle_report_ai_status`, default `pending`) + `ai_eval_generated_at timestamptz` (all three written by the companion generator since S3). **Goal preset (`mezo-dq60`):** `202608242315_mezo-dq60_goal_preset.sql` adds `mesocycle.goal_preset text` and `meso_template.goal_preset text` — a machine-readable sibling to the free-text `goal` column (which stays the human-readable prose shown in the UI). The wizard's `Cél` step writes the chosen `GOAL_PRESETS[].id` into `goalPreset` on the template/run it creates (`MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx:196`); the template editor's `Cél` `<select>` (`MesoTemplateEditorPage.tsx`) lets it be changed later on an existing template. The migration backfills existing rows by matching their `goal` text against the exact `GOAL_PRESETS[].description` strings the wizard has always written (a point-in-time snapshot, same convention as the `mezo-gbo7` slug backfill); a row whose `goal` was hand-edited or predates the presets stays `goal_preset IS NULL` — never guessed. **The multi-preset picker this column originally served is gone (`mezo-d20.14`, hypertrophy-only wizard):** `addExerciseWithDefaults` (below) no longer branches on `goalPreset` at all — every new exercise gets the single hypertrophy scheme regardless — so `goalPreset` is now write-only from the FE's perspective: `TrainService.stampRun` still copies it straight through with no fallback and nothing server-side branches on it, but nothing reads it back either.
