The column and its `hypertrophy`-literal writes (`toUpsert` in `wizard/wizardState.ts`) are kept for schema/history continuity, not for any live behaviour. **Muscle priorities (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD3/GD4):** `202608251200_mezo-3m5m_muscle_priorities.sql` adds `mesocycle.muscle_priorities jsonb` and `meso_template.muscle_priorities jsonb` — a **sparse** per-coarse-muscle map (`{"back": "emphasize", "glute": "maintain"}`; an absent key means **grow**, the default) with **no backfill**: existing rows land on `NULL`, which `PriorityTier.of` treats identically to an all-`grow` map, so live behaviour does not jump on deploy (GD3). Surfaced as `musclePriorities` on `MesocycleResponse`/`MesoTemplateResponse`/`MesoTemplateUpsertRequest` — a template save (wizard save, template editor) round-trips it through the existing full-upsert `PUT`/`POST` paths — and, for a mid-cycle change on a live run that shouldn't force a full day/template re-upsert, a dedicated `PUT /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/muscle-priorities` (`operationId updateMesocycleMusclePriorities`, body `MusclePrioritiesUpdateRequest`, `TrainService.updateMusclePriorities`) that replaces the map wholesale (an empty map is stored as `NULL`, matching the sparse convention) and returns the full assembled `MesocycleResponse`. **GD7:** the map is persisted immediately but only changes weekly-ramp behaviour at the **next** volume-progression rollover (§4 `#### Volume progression` below) — nothing about the current week is recomputed retroactively; the run editor's Fókusz picker used to surface this with a helper line ("A módosítás a következő heti görgetésnél lép életbe."). **Since mesocycle pages v2 (`mezo-d20.15`) there is no in-cycle Fókusz picker any more** — tiers are planning-time only (the wizard's Step 2) — so the endpoint's own GD7 lag is now moot for the FE: `PUT /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/muscle-priorities` stays live in the contract/backend but has **no caller at all**. That includes the template editor, which never used it: a template's tiers ride the full-upsert `PUT /api/train/meso-templates/{id}` like every other template field, and this endpoint only ever served a mid-cycle change on a live RUN. The FE keeps the whole chain — `trainApi.updateMusclePriorities` → the `updateMusclePriorities` mutation exposed on `useTrain()` — **wired but app-unused**, kept for contract parity (and covered by its own hook test), not because any screen calls it.
- **Entities:** `MesocycleEntity` (`phase_curve text[]` mapped as `List<String>` for Hibernate dirty-checking; `volume_recompute` jsonb = `VolumeRecomputeJson`), `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity` (first typed-jsonb persistence, `ProvenanceEnvelope`), `WorkoutSessionEntity`, `ExerciseEntity`. **`MesoTemplateEntity`** (`entity/MesoTemplateEntity.java`, `mezo-meyc.1`) is the plan-document twin — same soft-delete/`OwnedEntity` house rules, `days`/`volumePerMuscle` typed jsonb via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` (`entity/json/{MesoDayJson,GymExerciseJson,VolumeBaselineJson}.java`), `phaseCurve` the same `List<String>` dirty-checking trick as `MesocycleEntity`. **`MesocycleReportEntity`** (`entity/MesocycleReportEntity.java`, `mezo-meyc.2`) — the frozen report row, same soft-delete/`OwnedEntity` house rules; `report`/`context` are typed jsonb via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)` onto the record shapes `MesoReportJson`/`MesoContextJson` (`entity/json/`), each mirroring its matching contract DTO field-for-field so freezing/reading is a straight MapStruct pass (`mapper/MesoReportMapper.java`).
- **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/mesocycles` (batch-loads volume + days, stitches; response now carries `templateId`/`closedAt`/**`hasReport`** — one extra batched `MesocycleReportRepository` query over the page's run ids, `TrainService.java:110`, `mezo-meyc.2`), `POST .../{id}/activate`, **`POST .../{id}/close`** (`mezo-meyc.2`: optional body `{selfEval?}`; sets `status=archived` + `closed_at=now()` + computes/persists the frozen report **in the same transaction** — see the `MesocycleReportService` bullet below for the idempotency contract), `PUT .../{id}/days/{dayId}/exercises` (soft-delete + re-insert; unknown `catalogId` → 400, not FK 500), **`GET .../{id}/volume-arc`** (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m` — read-only, not switch-gated; see `#### Volume progression` below). **`GET .../{id}/report`** (`mezo-meyc.2` — the full frozen report; 404 `TRAIN_MESO_REPORT_NOT_FOUND` when the run has none yet) and **`POST .../{id}/report/regenerate`** (**202**; archived-only, else 409 `TRAIN_MESO_NOT_CLOSED`; upserts the report and resets the AI half (narrative + `context`) to `pending` — serves both a stale-report refresh and the legacy-backfill „Riport generálása" path for pre-S2 archived runs). **`POST /api/train/mesocycles` (direct run create) is REMOVED from the contract (`mezo-meyc.1`)** — the wizard now saves a template and starts it; `TrainService`'s create method is gone, `MesocycleCreateRequest` deleted. Its replacement, all served by `MesoTemplateService` (`service/MesoTemplateService.java`) via `TrainController`: `GET /api/train/meso-templates` (owner's templates, created-date ascending, each with `runCount`), `POST /api/train/meso-templates` (create), `PUT .../{id}` (full-replace update — every exercise gets a fresh server id on every save, ids are NOT stable across writes), `DELETE .../{id}` (soft delete; runs already started from it are untouched), **`POST .../{id}/start` `{startDate, status: active|planned}`** (stamps a full run via the extracted `TrainService.stampRun` — metadata copied, `endDate`/`currentWeek`/`orderIndex` server-computed exactly as the old create did, `days` materialized into `workout_session`+`exercise` rows, unknown `catalogId` → 400 as before, and — only for an active start — `volume_per_muscle` seeded into `muscle_group_volume_log`; starting as active archives other actives, the existing single-active invariant), **`POST /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/rerun`** (`{templateId}` response — a run started from a template just returns its `templateId`; a legacy run with none gets one **materialized** from its own `workout_session`/`exercise`/`muscle_group_volume_log` rows on first call — including a fallback baseline name **"Örökölt kiindulás"** when a volume-log row carries no provenance name — then links it back onto the run so a second rerun of the same run is a plain lookup). Ownership: a missing/foreign template is a 404 with the new `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` `SystemMessage`. **`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`** (`operationId generateMesoPlan`, mesocycle wizard redesign) — the wizard's Hypertrophy plan generator; nothing is persisted, the response's `template` is meant to be posted straight back to `POST /api/train/meso-templates` unchanged. See `#### Plan generator` below.
- **DTOs:** `MesocycleResponse` (gained `templateId?`/`closedAt?`/**`hasReport`**), `MesoDay`/`MesoDayInput`, `GymExercise`/`GymExerciseInput`, `VolumeProfile`/`VolumeSource`/`VolumeBaseline`/`VolumeAdjustment`/`VolumeRecompute`/`VolumeChange`/`VolumeUserOverride`, **`MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`/`MuscleVolumeArc`/`VolumeArcWeek`** (the volume-arc endpoint's response shape, below — also **reused verbatim** as the report's `volume` block). New template family (`mezo-meyc.1`): `MesoTemplateResponse` (adds `runCount`), `MesoTemplateUpsertRequest`, `MesoTemplateStartRequest` (`startDate` + `status: active|planned`), `MesoRerunResponse` (`{templateId}`). New report family (`mezo-meyc.2`): `MesocycleCloseRequest {selfEval?}`, **`MesocycleReportResponse`** (identity fields read live off `MesocycleEntity` — `mesocycleId`/`templateId`/`title`/dates/`weeks`/`closedAt` — plus the frozen `adherence: MesoReportAdherence`, `strength: MesoStrengthDelta[]`, `records: MesoReportRecords` (nested `MesoRecordHighlight[]`), the reused `volume: MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`, `context: MesoContext?` (nested `MesoContextWeek[]` + `MesoContextTotals`, populated since S3 — null only for a report whose context has not been assembled yet), and `selfEval`/`aiEval`/`aiEvalStatus`/`aiEvalGeneratedAt`/**`aiEvalEnabled`** — the last one now the presence of the `MesoReviewGate` bean, i.e. the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch).
- **`volumePerMuscle`/`volumeRecompute` now have a real write path** (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase A, `mezo-hi9m`, PR #80): `VolumeProgressionService.rolloverIfDue` (see `#### Volume progression` below) writes `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity.currentSets` + a `volumeRecompute` audit **live, per calendar week**, behind `mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled` (default **on**) — it is no longer seed-only. **Since `mezo-xlmp` the rows themselves have a real birth path too:** a wizard create-as-active / activate seeds them from the fixed `mezo.volume.baselines` RP table (see `#### Volume progression` below), so a user-created meso's weekly ramp, DA6 effective-set distribution and volume-arc are live without demo fixtures. The mock fixtures (`data/train/train.ts`) and the `demofixtures` seed (`TrainSeedData`) still carry richer **narrative prose** in their provenance than the real engine writes (the engine's per-week `changes[]` is a terse `{muscle, change, reason, deload}` list, not free text) — that gap is real and worth keeping in mind. The provenance envelope round-trips through the DB (proven by `ProvenanceRoundTripIT`).
- **`MesocycleReportService`** (`service/MesocycleReportService.java`, `mezo-meyc.2`, spec §2) computes the report ONCE, inside the close transaction, and never again drifts even if the data underneath later changes. **Adherence:** non-empty template days (`countNonEmptyTemplateDays`, distinct `workout_session`/`exercise` join) × weeks elapsed (`MesoWeeks.weekOf(startDate, windowEnd, weeks)`) vs the run's meso-origin instances actually `completed` in `[startDate, closedAt]` (`WorkoutSessionRepository.findCompletedMesoInstancesInWindow`). **Volume:** `VolumeArcService.arc` frozen verbatim, via a package-visible 3-arg overload (`VolumeArcService.java:65`, `arc(createdBy, mesoId, effectiveCurrentWeek)`) that substitutes `max(currentWeek, weeksElapsed)` for the stored (possibly stale — it only advances on the weekly rollover) `currentWeek`, so the frozen arc can never disagree with adherence about which week was reached; the public 2-arg `arc(createdBy, mesoId)` is unchanged. **Strength:** per exercise IDENTITY (catalog id else exact name, the `ExerciseHistoryResolver`/`MedalService` idiom, resolved over `findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted` so a mid-run day-edit's soft-deleted rows still resolve history) over **non-skipped working sets only**, bucketed into meso-weeks, first-vs-last-bucket top set compared by Epley e1RM (`w×(1+reps/30)`) — an identity needs **≥2 distinct trained weeks** to earn an entry; `deltaKg` is the top-set LOAD difference (null if either end was weightless), `deltaPct` is the e1RM-based relative gain, so "same weight, more reps" reads as real progress instead of a flat 0 kg. **Records:** replays the existing `MedalService.forSessions` over the run's instance ids (medal count + top 5 by date) — nothing is stored, there is no medal table, this is why the block has to be frozen here at all. `closeWindowEnd` falls back to `endDate` when `closedAt` is null (the legacy-backfill case). **`aiEvalEnabled` (S3, `mezo-meyc.3`)** now reports the presence of the **`MesoReviewGate`** marker bean (`feature/train/MesoReviewGate.java`, `@ConditionalOnProperty` on `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled`, declared `true` in `application.yml`), read via `ObjectProvider<MesoReviewGate>` rather than a direct injection (the bean is conditional, so a plain field would fail to wire when the switch is off) — off ⇒ the FE hides the AI section and skips polling instead of waiting on a `pending` that will never resolve. The gate deliberately lives in `feature.train`, not `feature.companion`: `companion` already depends on `train`, so declaring it there and consuming it here would close a brand-new `train↔companion` package cycle (`ArchitectureTest.feature_slices_are_cycle_free`). Both entry points that reach `computeAndStore` — `TrainService.closeMesocycle`'s REAL-close branch (never an idempotent re-close, never the fill-if-null self-eval branch) and `regenerate` (every accepted call) — publish **`MesocycleClosed(userId, mesocycleId)`** (`feature/train/MesocycleClosed.java`) in the same transaction right after the row is persisted; the companion consumes it AFTER_COMMIT and writes `context` + `ai_eval` back (see §5 *Train ↔ Companion*). `computeAndStore` UPSERTs onto the run's single `mesocycle_report` row, **clears the ENTIRE AI half — `ai_eval`/`ai_eval_generated_at`/`ai_eval_status='pending'` AND `context`** (a lifestyle context computed against the old numbers/window must not survive beside fresh ones; with the meso-review switch off nothing would ever overwrite it, so the page would show last-window buckets next to this-window results — the companion's generator re-populates it from `pending`), and never touches `selfEval`; `TrainService.closeMesocycle` (`:256`) is the sole writer of `closed_at`/`status` and is fully IDEMPOTENT — re-closing an already-archived run neither recomputes the report nor overwrites an existing self-eval; the one thing a second close still does is **fill** a still-null self-eval (the auto-archived-by-starting-the-next-run case never otherwise gets a close call with a note).
- **FE (`mezo-meyc.2`):** `useMesoReport(id)` (`data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts`, exported `mesoReportQueryKey`) — dual-mode read that resolves a real-mode 404 to `notFound` rather than throwing (`{report, pending, notFound, error, refetch, regenerate, regenerating}`); `useTrain().closeMesocycle(id, selfEval?, opts?)` posts the extended close body. `apiFetch` (`data/_client/api.ts:35-41`) now tolerates an empty response body (reads text first, only `JSON.parse`s when non-empty) since the 202 regenerate response carries none and the old unconditional `res.json()` threw a `SyntaxError` on it. `MesoCloseSheet`/`MesoReportPage`/`MuscleArcSwitch` are covered in §2 above. Mock parity: `mockClose` (`trainHooks.ts:294`) flips the mock run to `archived` and seeds an — honestly empty, since mock has no logged history to freeze — report at the report query key, deliberately kept at `aiEvalEnabled: false`/`context: null` even after S3 (a freshly closed demo run has nothing to evaluate yet, and flipping it to `true` would poll forever offline); `mesoReportMock` (`data/train/train.ts`) is the one pre-seeded report fixture with real derived numbers (`meso-rec-03`), its frozen arc built from the SAME `mockMuscleArc` helper the live mock volume arc uses so the two fixtures can never drift apart, and since `mezo-meyc.3` it also carries a `ready` `aiEval` (4 Hungarian paragraphs) plus a full 8-week `context` (a couple of deliberate null holes — missing sleep/kcal/run weeks — to exercise the report page's `–` cells) so the S3 demo shows both new blocks by default; `mockRegenerate` (`mesoReportHooks.ts:26`) also writes a report into the cache — a demo-only quirk is that it can "generate" one for a still-active run, since mock mode has no archived-only guard. The MSW `mesoReportFixture` (`test/msw/handlers.ts`) mirrors the same S3 shape (`ready` + a small 2-week `context`) but with its OWN short literal values rather than importing `mesoReportMock` — `data/train/train.ts` is one of the largest mock modules in the app, and `handlers.ts` is transitively imported by nearly every test file, so pulling it in measurably raised full-suite CPU load and surfaced as an unrelated flake (`MesoCloseSheet.test.tsx`) under full local parallelism.
- **FE (`mezo-meyc.4`) — the compare composition is client-side only.** No endpoint, no new hook: `MesoComparePage` calls `useMesoReport` twice and the three pure helpers in `features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts` do the lining up. `alignVolumeWeeks(a, b)` — muscles are the **union** of both frozen arcs (a's order first, then b-only ones, so the pill row never reshuffles), weeks aligned by **week number** (`W1..max`), every cell nullable so the shorter run's tail is `–` and a null `actual` stays null (an unlogged week is not zero volume); a report with `volume: null` contributes nothing, both null ⇒ empty ⇒ the block disappears. `sharedStrengthDeltas(a, b)` — the heart of the view: an exercise is the same identity when **both** sides carry a `catalogId` and they match, else on an **exact** name match (a fuzzy match would put two different lifts on one row); only shared identities survive, sorted by the **larger |deltaPct| descending** (magnitude, so a big regression ranks as high as a big gain) with pct-less lifts last — note the ordering key (magnitude) and the sage highlight (**higher signed** pct) answer two different questions. `contextDiff(a, b)` — the six run-level totals (alvás h / kcal / energia / stressz / súlyváltozás kg / sport perc) only; a metric NEITHER run measured is dropped, one only ONE measured stays (`7,4 h` vs `–` is itself informative), and a null `context` contributes nulls, never zeros. Domain-type touch: `Mesocycle` gained the optional **`hasReport`** the contract already served (`data/types.ts`) — `toMesocycle` spread it through all along, it just was not typed — and mock `mockClose` now flips it to `true` alongside the status, since it seeds a report in the same breath. Mock parity needed a SECOND report: `mesoReportHyp03Mock` (`data/train/train.ts`) + the archived run `meso-hyp-03`, deliberately unlike `meso-rec-03` (6 vs 8 weeks, worse adherence, higher-volume arc with `triceps` but no `biceps`/`ham`, worse lifestyle averages, `energyAvg: null` in the totals to exercise the one-sided `–`) and sharing exactly **three** of its five exercises, so both the overlap and the non-overlap paths are live in the offline app; `mesoReportHooks`' `mockReportFor` resolves against a small `MOCK_REPORTS` table now instead of one id. The MSW handlers were left alone on purpose — see the CPU-flake note above.

### Workout execution
- **Tables** (`202606120900_mezo-tod_t2_workout_execution.sql`): adds `workout_session.template_session_id` (self-FK SET NULL), `exercise_set.workout_session_id` (FK→instance CASCADE), renames `voice_note`→`note`, creates `exercise_feedback` (UNIQUE `(workout_session_id, exercise_id)`; CHECKs pump 1-4 / joint_pain 1-3 / workload 1-3). `202606171200_mezo-an1_exercise_set_skipped.sql` adds `exercise_set.skipped` (F3 whole-exercise skip marker). `202606171210_mezo-an1_exercise_note.sql` adds `exercise.note` (F4 durable per-exercise note). **`202607072000_mezo-dhdr_prescribed_sets.sql` turns the template `exercise` row into a *recipe*** (hypertrophy drive): it replaces the single `sets`/`target_reps` pair with `warmup_sets` (default 0), `working_sets`, `rep_min`, `rep_max`, and adds a nullable `anchor_weight_kg NUMERIC(6,2)` (first-session load seed); `target_rir` is kept. Same migration adds `exercise_set.kind` (`warmup`|`working`, default `working`, `ck_exercise_set_kind` CHECK) so a logged set records which half of the recipe it belongs to.
- **Entities:** `ExerciseSetEntity` (`weight_kg NUMERIC(6,2)`, `side` CHECK L|B|R, `done_at`, `skipped`, **`kind` default `working`** — `ExerciseSetEntity.java:72`), `ExerciseEntity` (the template exercise — carries the durable `note` **and the recipe fields `warmupSets`/`workingSets`/`repMin`/`repMax`/`targetRir`/`anchorWeightKg`**, `ExerciseEntity.java:49-70`), `ExerciseFeedbackEntity`.
- **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/workouts/today` (workout context — **since the cross-day start (`mezo-p7rp`) it takes an optional `?templateSessionId=` and day resolution is `open instance > param > today's weekday label`**: the user's open instance always wins (a deep link to another day while one runs resumes the running one), the param pins any owned template day (else 404), and only the param-less no-open case falls back to the weekday-label match; **`completedWorkout` is now the resolved day's most recent completed instance of the current Mon–Sun week**, not just today's — a day done any day this week reviews instead of restarting (D5). **Each `TodayExercise` also carries `prescribedSets: PrescribedSet[]` + a `rationale` string when `mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled` is on, null when off** so the FE falls back to ad-hoc logging; **since Progressive Overload Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`) each `TodayExercise` additionally carries a structured `progression: ProgressionSignal` (null on a first session / anchor-only exercise with no real history — the RIR-aware engine has nothing to compare against yet), and `WorkoutTodayResponse` carries a day-level `overloadSummary` (counts of exercises moving via each lever, null when the switch is off)**; see the recommendation-engine subsection below), **`GET /api/train/workouts?from&to`** (date-range summary list — see below; **summaries carry `templateSessionId`** since `mezo-p7rp` so the FE can map template day → completed instance this week), **`GET /api/train/workouts/{id}`** (single-instance detail for the done-day review — see the done-state subsection below), `POST /api/train/workouts` (resumes the open instance, never duplicates; **cross-day guards `mezo-p7rp`:** another day's workout open → **409 `TRAIN_WORKOUT_OPEN_ELSEWHERE`** (D6), template day already completed this Mon–Sun week → **409 `TRAIN_DAY_DONE_THIS_WEEK`** (D5) — the same-template resume branch runs BEFORE the guards so resuming never conflicts), `POST .../{id}/sets`, **`PUT /api/train/workouts/{id}/sets/{setId}`** (`mezo-l3on` — egy logolt szett teljesítmény-mezőinek felülírása; teljes csere, nem patch: `kind`/`setIndex`/`exerciseId`/`target*` nem változik, warmup soron a `rir` mindig `null`; újraderivált `medals`-szal a válaszban) + **`DELETE /api/train/workouts/{id}/sets/{setId}`** (`mezo-l3on` — soft delete, majd az adott gyakorlat megmaradt szettjeinek `setIndex`-e 0..n-1-re rendeződik, mert a FE pozicionális kurzora `logged.length`-ként hézagmentességet feltételez; mindkét új végpont csak `active` instance-on él — **409 `TRAIN_WORKOUT_NOT_ACTIVE`** máskülönben —, egy skip-marker sorra vagy idegen/ismeretlen `setId`-re **404**), `POST .../{id}/skip` (F3 — records a skip-marker set for a whole exercise), `POST .../{id}/feedback` (upsert per exercise), `POST .../{id}/finish`, `PUT /api/train/exercises/{exerciseId}/note` (F4 — owner-scoped durable note write, ≤500 chars). Cross-day ITs: `CrossDayWorkoutIT`.
- **`GET /api/train/workouts?from&to` (date-range summaries — `mezo-t16y.1`, added for the Insights-Weekly composition):** returns owner-scoped **completed** workout instances (`status = 'completed'` — the done-semantics shift of `mezo-cd8s`, see the done-state subsection below; **was** ≥1 non-skipped set) whose date falls in the **inclusive** `[from, to]` range, **date-ascending**. `WorkoutService.listWorkouts` (a pure read, no `@Transactional`) validates `from ≤ to` — `from>to` → **400 `TRAIN_INVALID_DATE_RANGE`** — and **reuses the existing `WorkoutSessionRepository.findDoneInstancesBetween`** query (the same entities finder the companion `get_training_log` tool uses under `scope=gym`, §5), so **no new repository query** was added. FE client: `trainApi.listWorkouts(from, to)` (`GET /api/train/workouts?from=&to=`); its original consumer, `useWeekly` (`data/insights/weeklyHooks.ts`), was **retired with the Insights Weekly tab (`mezo-p2tr`)** — the op itself survives, now consumed instead by `useWeekWorkouts` (`data/train/workoutDetailHooks.ts`) for the current week's range. See [`insights.md`](insights.md) §2.2 for the retirement. **`.../{id}/finish` now returns an optional `levelUp`** (the gamified-progression payload): `WorkoutService.finishWorkout` computes + attaches it inside its `@Transactional`, but **only when the `mezo.feature.progression.enabled` switch is on** (gated by the `ProgressionGate` `@ConditionalOnProperty` bean, injected via `ObjectProvider`). Start/resume responses never carry it — the shared `toInstanceResponse` is untouched. See the progression backend doc (`_platform-api-backend.md` §4e); the FE now **captures** this `levelUp` and presents it via the shared `LevelUpProvider` host + `LevelUpScreen` overlay (P5, §2 active-workout COMPLETE phase + the sport/run log flows).
- **DTOs:** `WorkoutTodayResponse` (now also carries **`completedWorkout`**, a nullable `WorkoutInstanceResponse` = the most recent `completed` instance of today's template day dated **today**; drives the Kész/Megnézem hero, the day-sheet gating and the session-route redirect without a second fetch — `mezo-cd8s`), `TodayExercise` (now carries the recipe fields `warmupSets`/`workingSets`/`repMin`/`repMax`/`targetRIR`/`anchorWeightKg` instead of `sets`/`targetReps`, plus optional `prescribedSets`/`rationale`), **new `PrescribedSet` (`{kind: warmup|working, targetWeightKg?, targetReps, targetRIR?}`)**, **new `ProgressionSignal` (`{lever: weight|rep|hold|deload, deltaKg?, deltaReps?, targetWeightKg?, targetReps, rationale}` — Progressive Overload Plan 1, `mezo-5pfe`)** + **new `OverloadSummary` (`{weightUp, repUp, hold}`, day-level)**, `LastWeekRef`, `WorkoutStartRequest`, `WorkoutInstanceResponse`, `WorkoutSummaryResponse` (`{id, date, status}` — the `listWorkouts` range summary; `status` enum `planned|active|completed|skipped`), **`WorkoutDetailResponse` (`{id, templateSessionId, date, status, title, dayLabel, durationEst, exercises: WorkoutDetailExercise[]}`) + `WorkoutDetailExercise` (`{exerciseId, name, muscle, type, warmupSets, workingSets, repMin, repMax, targetRIR, skipped, sets: ExerciseSetResponse[]}`) — the done-day review payload (`mezo-cd8s`)**, `ExerciseSetResponse` (now `+ kind`), `SetLogRequest` (now `+ kind`, default `working`; **`rir` is optional since `mezo-eerq`** — the FE omits it on warmup sets, persisting a NULL instead of a fake RIR 0), **new `SetUpdateRequest` (`{weightKg, reps, rir?, side?, note?}` — the `updateSet`/`deleteSet` full-replace body, `mezo-l3on`)**, `WorkoutSkipRequest`, `WorkoutFeedbackInput`, `ExerciseNoteRequest` (`note` ≤500, nullable to clear). The meso-day `GymExercise`/`GymExerciseInput` DTOs likewise carry the recipe fields (replacing `sets`/`targetReps`), so the planner/builder round-trips the recipe. `TodayExercise.note` surfaces the durable note on read; `saveExerciseNote` is owner-scoped (foreign/missing exercise → **404**, over-length note → **400**). `logSet` validation: setIndex 0-49, weightKg 0-999, reps 1-100, rir 0-5 (optional), side `^[LBR]$`, note ≤500. `logSet`/`skipExercise` on a completed instance → **409** (`TRAIN_WORKOUT_NOT_ACTIVE`); a foreign exercise → **404**. `skipExercise` persists an `ExerciseSetEntity` with `skipped=true`, the next free `setIndex` for that exercise, and null performance fields — a skip marker is **not** a logged set. **Since `mezo-cd8s` the gym done-state is `status = 'completed'`**, so neither a skip marker nor any number of logged sets flips a day to done on its own — only the explicit finish (or the lazy auto-close of a stale instance carrying ≥1 non-skipped set) does (see the done-state subsection below).
- "Last week" comparison: `WorkoutService.lastWeekRefs` picks the top working set of the most-recent completed session in which the exercise's **identity** (catalog id, else exact name — `ExerciseHistoryResolver`, soft-deleted day-edit rows included) was trained — **since `mezo-eq4w` this is identity-resolved, not row/template-day-scoped**, so a day edit's soft-delete+reinsert row swap never blanks the comparison. **Warmup sets (`kind='warmup'`) are excluded** from this pick (working-set history only).

#### Set recommendation engine — hypertrophy drive + progressive overload (`mezo-dhdr` + `mezo-5pfe`)
`SetRecommendationService.prescribe(createdBy, ex, deloadWeek)` (`feature/train/service/SetRecommendationService.java`) computes the per-set targets that `getToday` attaches as `prescribedSets`, and — since Progressive Overload Plan 1 (`mezo-5pfe`) — the structured `progression: ProgressionSignal` per exercise. It resolves the exercise's **top working set** of the most recent completed session where the exercise's **identity** was trained (catalog id, else exact name — the shared `ExerciseHistoryResolver`, `mezo-eq4w`; formerly template-day + row-scoped, which a day edit's row swap silently reset), then **delegates the lever/weight/rep decision to a new pure `ProgressionDecider`** (`service/ProgressionDecider.java` — no Spring, no DB; unit-tested directly via `ProgressionDeciderTest`). The **RIR-aware, deload-aware double-progression matrix** (SUPERSEDES the earlier reps-only rule of "reps < rep_min → drop the increment; reps in range → hold"): `slack = loggedRIR − targetRIR` (a **null logged RIR is treated as on-target**, `slack = 0`, neutral — an un-rated set never triggers a false grind-hold or a false pass).
  - **Deload week** (see below) → **regress** regardless of reps/RIR: ~0.9× the last working weight (plate-snapped), reps reset to `rep_min`.
  - `reps ≥ rep_max` → **+weight**: bump by the type increment (plate-snapped), reps reset to `rep_min`.
  - `rep_min ≤ reps < rep_max` → **+rep** (build toward `rep_max`) — **unless** the last set was a **grind** (`slack < 0`, i.e. the logged RIR came in under the target), in which case the engine **holds/consolidates** the same weight instead of adding a rep.
  - `reps < rep_min` → **−weight** (drop by the increment, plate-snapped) when the athlete was **at or over target effort** (`slack ≤ 0`); otherwise **hold** — the load was too heavy but the effort wasn't there either, so the engine waits rather than moving the wrong way.

  On the **first session** (no working-set history) it still seeds from `anchor_weight_kg` (or leaves the target **null** with no anchor) exactly as before, and `progression` is **null** — there is nothing to compare against yet. **Weightless/bodyweight history** (a logged set with no weight) still progresses on reps only (`progression.lever = 'rep'`, unchanged behavior). **Plyometric / bodyweight exercises are weightless** — the engine returns null target weights (reps/RIR only), which the FE renders as a reps-only stepper. Warmup sets are generated by ramping the working target down through the configured `warmupRamp` — and are **emitted even when the base weight is unknown** (first session, no anchor; `mezo-eerq`): their target weight is simply null, keeping the prescribed list aligned with the recipe's set count so the FE never loses the `B{n}` labels / RIR suppression on a first session. The method returns a `Prescription(sets, rationale, progression)` record (`service/Prescription.java`, now 3 fields) — the set list, the human-readable `rationale` shown in the active workout, and the structured signal the FE renders as `ProgressionBanner`/the prep chips (§2). `WorkoutService.getToday` tallies each exercise's `progression.lever` into the day-level `overloadSummary` (`weightUp`/`repUp`/`hold`, `deload` folded into `hold`). It reads **completed-instance history only**, so a live (open) session never feeds back into its own prescription → the recommendation is **stable across a mid-workout resume** (§9).
- **Deload-week detection** (`WorkoutService.getToday`, `mezo-5pfe`; index fixed by `mezo-hi9m` DA1): `deloadWeek` is derived from `activeMeso.getPhaseCurve().get(activeMeso.getCurrentWeek() - 1)` equalling `"Deload"` (case-insensitive) — **`currentWeek` is 1-based, `phaseCurve` is 0-indexed**, so the lookup is `currentWeek - 1` (`WorkoutService.java:172-177`; a pre-existing off-by-one against a 0-based index was corrected here). **Bound-checked and null-safe** — no active meso, no phase curve, or `currentWeek - 1` outside `[0, phaseCurve.size())` all resolve to **not deload** rather than throwing, so a meso whose `currentWeek` has drifted past its own curve length degrades gracefully instead of 500ing. `VolumeProgressionService.isDeloadPhase` applies the same corrected 1-based lookup independently (see below).
- **Config** (`mezo.hypertrophy.*` → `HypertrophyProperties`, `feature/train/config/HypertrophyProperties.java`): `plateStep` (2.5 — rounding granularity for computed kg, also used by the deload 0.9× snap), `defaultIncrement` (2.5 — fallback), a per-type `increment` map (compound 5.0 / isolation 2.5), `defaultWarmupSets` (2 — a backend-side fallback, independent of the FE picker's own type-seeded default in `libraryToGymExercise`, below), and **`warmupLadders`** (`Map<Integer, List<Ramp(pct, reps)>>`, budget wave-2 `mezo-dnln` — SUPERSEDES the earlier single flat `warmupRamp` list): **count-keyed, absolute-rep** ladders, one per `warmupSets` count — `1: [70%×4]`, `2: [50%×8, 75%×3]`, `3: [50%×8, 70%×4, 90%×2]` (`application.yml` `mezo.hypertrophy.warmup-ladders`). `SetRecommendationService.warmupLadder(n)` looks up `warmupLadders.get(n)` for `n ≤ 3`; for `n > 3` it **prepends `(n-3)` repeats of the 3-ladder's first rung** (50%×8) ahead of the full 3-ladder, so a 4th/5th warmup set never invents a new percentage — it just repeats the lightest rung. `@Validated` record, wired in `application.yml` under `mezo.hypertrophy:` — never `@Value`.
- **FE adaptive warmup-count suggestion (client-only, `mezo-dnln`):** `suggestedWarmupSets(day, exId)` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/warmupSuggest.ts`) is a pure heuristic — no server call — that picks a sane `warmupSets` count for an exercise from its type/muscle-group and its position among the day's earlier non-plyo exercises of the same **budget group** (`setBudget.ts`'s `budgetGroup`, see `#### Weekly bands` below): `type === 'plyo'` or **bodyweight-ish** (`anchorWeightKg == null && repMin ≥ 15` — the gate is the range's floor, not its ceiling, so a weighted-feel 12-15 isolation under a cut-prep/recovery preset scheme, which has no anchor yet on first add, still gets ramp-up; only a range that's high-rep **throughout** [e.g. 15-20] is bodyweight-ish enough to skip warmups, `mezo-szsi`) → 0; unknown budget group (e.g. a sport row) → 0; the first non-plyo `compound` opening its group → 3, but 2 when `anchorWeightKg != null && anchorWeightKg < 60` (a lighter compound needs less ramp-up); a later `compound` of an already-hit group → 1; an `isolation` opening its group → 1; an `isolation` after the group was hit → 0 ("group hit" only counts non-plyo exercises earlier in the day's order — a plyo row never makes a later exercise "not first"). Consumed two ways in `MesoEditor.tsx`: (1) passed as `suggestedWarmup` to every `ExerciseAccordionRow`, which shows a `↺ javaslat: {n}` chip in the expanded Bemelegítő tile when it differs from the stored count (tap → `onChange({ warmupSets: n })`); (2) the **add-path override** — `MesoEditor`'s existing new-exercise-detection `useEffect` (the one that auto-expands a freshly added row) ALSO fires `onChange(day.day, newId, { warmupSets: suggestion })` once, when the suggestion differs from `libraryToGymExercise`'s type-seeded default (compound 2 / isolation 1 / plyo 0, `mezo-dq60` — no longer a flat `warmupSets: 2` for every pick) — so a newly picked exercise lands on its adaptive count without the athlete tapping the chip. Because the picker already seeds a type-aware value close to what `suggestedWarmupSets` would land on anyway, this override is usually a no-op in practice — it's a safety net for the cases where position/anchor-weight context (which `libraryToGymExercise` doesn't see) pulls the count the other way. In real mode this is a **second PUT** right after the add's own persist (`MesoExercises.persistDay` fires on every `onChange`, `MesoExercises.test.tsx`).
- **Switch:** the whole engine is gated by `mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled` via the `HypertrophyDriveGate` `@ConditionalOnProperty` marker bean (`feature/train/HypertrophyDriveGate.java`; key constant `FeaturesConfiguration.HYPERTROPHY_DRIVE_SWITCH`). Off → `WorkoutService` skips the engine and returns null `prescribedSets`/`rationale`/`progression`, and `overloadSummary` is null, so the FE logs ad-hoc with no progression UI at all.
- **Downstream consumer (Progressive Overload Plan 3, `mezo-gj42`):** the deterministic
  `OverloadChallengeGenerator` (proactive-owned) reads this same per-exercise `progression` off
  `getToday` to pick the day's biggest jump for a proactive `overload` challenge — no new engine logic
  here; see [proactive.md §HBWI](proactive.md).

#### Volume progression — weekly per-muscle set ramp (Progressive Overload Plan 2, `mezo-hi9m`)
`VolumeProgressionService.rolloverIfDue(createdBy, activeMeso)` (`feature/train/service/VolumeProgressionService.java`) is called as one of the first steps of `WorkoutService.getToday` (right after the active meso is resolved, before the deload check and the effective-set distribution below — `WorkoutService.java:119-121`), gated by an `ObjectProvider<VolumeProgressionGate>` (present only when `mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled=true`, **default on**, mirroring `HypertrophyDriveGate`/`ClosingBlockGate`). It performs the **weekly rollover**: a real write path for what used to be seed-only `currentSets`/`volumeRecompute`.
- **Priority tiers resolve the ramp ceiling per muscle group (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD4):** `PriorityTier.of(meso.getMusclePriorities(), muscle)` (`feature/train/service/PriorityTier.java`) reads the meso's sparse `muscle_priorities` map — a `null` map, an absent key, or an unrecognized value all resolve to **Grow**, the default — and `tier.ceiling(mev, mav, mrv)` picks which volume landmark is the weekly ramp's "100%":

  | Tier | Ceiling | Ramps toward it? |
  |---|---|---|
  | **Emphasize** | MRV | yes — same ceiling as every muscle had before `mezo-3m5m` |
  | **Grow** (default/absent key) | MAV | yes — **was MRV before `mezo-3m5m`** |
  | **Maintain** | MEV | only up to MEV — below it (e.g. right after a deload) it RAMPs back up like any other tier; at/above MEV it HOLDs |

  **This is an intentional behaviour change, not a regression:** before `mezo-3m5m` every tracked muscle ramped all the way to MRV; now a group left on the default Grow tier ramps only to MAV, and only a group explicitly marked **Emphasize** (FE-capped at `EMPHASIZE_CAP` = 2 groups at once) keeps the old MRV ceiling. **Early-deload detection and the deload floor are UNCHANGED by tier** — `VolumeDecider`'s `earlyDeload = prevSets >= mrv && grind` and the deload floor `⌈mev/2⌉` both read the row's RAW `mrv`/`mev`, never the tier ceiling, so a Grow group capped at MAV never early-deloads off its (lower) ceiling — it still has to reach the raw MRV first. **A mid-cycle tier switch never cuts current sets:** switching a group to Maintain only lowers its ceiling to MEV — since `currentSets` is normally already at/above MEV at the point of a mid-cycle switch, the next rollover HOLDs there rather than dropping it to MEV; only an actual deload-phase week reduces sets, and that check is tier-independent too. **There is no separate ramp-disable flag for Maintain** — the ceiling alone encodes the tier: `VolumeDecider`'s RAMP condition is simply `prevSets < rampCeiling` (plus the shared productive/no-grind checks), so a Maintain muscle deloaded below MEV recovers back up to MEV over subsequent weeks exactly like a Grow/Emphasize muscle ramps toward its own ceiling — it just never ramps PAST MEV.
- **Idempotent per calendar week:** `MesoWeeks.clampWeek(startDate, weeks)` (a shared, package-visible `startDate`-anchored 1-based week helper, also used by the arc endpoint below) computes the week containing today; if it hasn't advanced past `meso.volumeRecompute.lastRun` ("W{n}"), the call is a no-op — safe to invoke on every `getToday`.
- **Otherwise, per `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity` row** (one per muscle group on the meso): it reads the **previous** calendar week's COMPLETED instances, collapses each logged exercise's 21-token zone to its coarse **`MuscleGroup`** (`service/MuscleGroup.java` — pure, `chest`/`back`/`shoulder`/`biceps`/`triceps` are dash-prefix-collapsed from the finer zones; `quad`/`ham`/`glute`/`calf`/`core` and legacy coarse tokens pass through unchanged), and derives per-group `loggedLastWeek` (working-set count) + `grind` (any exercise whose most-recent logged working set landed ≥ `grindRirGap` RIR below its `targetRir` — the recovery-proxy signal, since the engine has no direct cross-load input yet); both signals are computed only over exercises with `countsTowardVolume = true` (`VolumeProgressionService.lastWeekSignals`) — the fix-zárás closing block and plyometrics never move the rollover. It then resolves each row's `PriorityTier` (see the bullet above) and runs **`VolumeDecider.decide`** (`service/VolumeDecider.java` — pure, Spring-free, unit-tested via a dedicated test): week 1 → `MEV`; deload week or already-at-raw-`MRV`-and-grinding → `round(prevSets × deloadFraction)` floored at `⌈mev/2⌉`; productive AND below the tier's ceiling (logged ≥ target, not grinding, `prevSets < rampCeiling`) → `+step`; else → **HOLD** (for a Maintain group this HOLDs at/above MEV, but still RAMPs back up toward MEV first if a deload left it below). The result is persisted onto `currentSets` + appended as a `ProvenanceEnvelope.Adjustment` (`kind: "pattern"`) on the row's existing `source` — the baseline MEV/MAV/MRV is untouched, only a new adjustment is appended.
- The mesocycle's `currentWeek` advances to the new calendar week and `volumeRecompute` gets a fresh `{lastRun: "W{n}", nextRun: "W{n+1}", trigger: "weekly-rollover", changes: [...]}` — `changes` is a terse per-muscle `{muscle, change, reason, deload}` list (e.g. `"+2 sets"` / `"tartás"` / `"deload"`), NOT the richer free-text prose the mock/seed fixtures carry (be honest about that gap when describing this to a reader expecting mock-parity prose).
- A muscle group with no volume-log row on the meso is simply skipped, never fabricated.
- **Baseline seeding — where the rows are born (`mezo-xlmp`, tier-aware since the mesocycle wizard redesign):** volume-log rows are no longer demo-seed-only. `VolumeProgressionService.seedBaselines(createdBy, mesoId, priorities)` inserts one row per coarse `MuscleGroup` the meso's TEMPLATE days actually train — counting only exercises with `countsTowardVolume = true`, so a template whose only chest work is an exempt exercise seeds no chest row — from the fixed **`mezo.volume.baselines`** RP table (chest 8/14/20 · back 10/16/22 · shoulder 8/12/18 · biceps 6/10/14 · triceps 6/10/14 · quad 8/12/18 · ham 6/10/14 · glute 8/12/18 · calf 6/10/16; core/traps intentionally absent — RP treats their MEV as ~0), with **`currentSets = PriorityTier.of(priorities, group).weekOneStart(mev, mav, mrv)`** — MEV+2 for an EMPHASIZE group (capped at MRV), plain MEV otherwise, so the freshly seeded row already agrees with what `MesoPlanSkeleton`/`MesoPlanGeneratorService` put in week 1 of a generated plan (the W1 rollover stamps its own START audit on top regardless) — and a provenance of an `RP guidelines · intermediate` baseline, confidence 0.5 (contract-required on `VolumeSource`), no adjustments. Called from `TrainService.createMesocycle` (**ACTIVE-status creates only** — a planned meso stays profile-less, so `MesoVolume`'s "csak aktív mesocikluson" guard stays true, `src.musclePriorities()` passed through) and **unconditionally from `TrainService.activateMesocycle`** (idempotent — an existing row's group is skipped untouched — so tapping activate doubles as the backfill path for pre-`mezo-xlmp` mesos created without rows; `target.getMusclePriorities()` passed through). Gated by the same `VolumeProgressionGate` as the rollover (switch off ⇒ byte-identical pre-seed behavior). A group present in the template but absent from the config table (core, the `lats`/`rear-delt` legacy pass-throughs, sport rows) is never fabricated — DA5 holds on the create path too. ITs: `VolumeBaselineSeedIT` (5 tests incl. the create→`getToday` W1-MEV-distribution chain) + `VolumeBaselineSeedSwitchOffIT` + **`MesoStartTierSeedIT`** (the tier-aware MEV+2 start on an EMPHASIZE group, mesocycle wizard redesign).
- **Effective per-exercise working sets (same `getToday` call, DA6):** once the rollover has run (or on a week where it was already up to date), and only when the switch is on and the meso has volume-log rows, `WorkoutService.effectiveWorkingSets` distributes each muscle group's `currentSets` across **the meso template week's** same-group exercises that carry `countsTowardVolume` — proportional to each exercise's template `workingSets`, remainder to the largest, never below 1 — and this **derived** count overrides `TodayExercise.workingSets` (the template row itself is untouched). Distributing per DAY (the pre-`mezo-gbo7` behaviour) multiplied weekly volume by training frequency and broke the spec's `[MEV, MRV]` bound. Exempt exercises — the fix-zárás closing block and plyometrics — keep their template `workingSets` and are excluded from the rollover signals, the arc's actuals and baseline seeding. This effective count is what's threaded into the hypertrophy-drive engine: `SetRecommendationService.prescribe(createdBy, ex, deloadWeek, effectiveWorkingSets)` — the 4-arg overload, the only caller being `getToday`. Switch off, or no volume-log rows on the meso → every exercise keeps its template `workingSets` unchanged (byte-identical to pre-Plan-2 behavior).
- **Config** (`mezo.volume.*` → `VolumeProperties`, `feature/train/config/VolumeProperties.java`, `@Validated` record): `step` (2 — sets added on a productive week), `deloadFraction` (0.5 — deload/at-MRV-grind target as a fraction of the previous week's sets), `grindRirGap` (2 — RIR-below-target gap counted as a grind), and **`baselines`** (`Map<String, Baseline(mev, mav, mrv)>` keyed by coarse muscle group — the RP landmark table the create/activate seeding reads, `mezo-xlmp`; see the baseline-seeding bullet above).
- **Switch:** `mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled` (`FeaturesConfiguration.VOLUME_PROGRESSION_SWITCH`, default **true**) via the `VolumeProgressionGate` marker bean (`feature/train/VolumeProgressionGate.java`).
- **Read-only companion — the volume-arc endpoint:** `GET /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/volume-arc` (`operationId getMesocycleVolumeArc`, `TrainController.getMesocycleVolumeArc`) → `VolumeArcService.arc` (`feature/train/service/VolumeArcService.java`) returns `MesocycleVolumeArcResponse { mesocycleId, title, currentWeek, weeks, startDate, endDate, status, phaseCurve[], muscles: MuscleVolumeArc[] }`; each `MuscleVolumeArc { muscle, region, mrv, weeks: VolumeArcWeek[] }` (`region` = a FE color-family key — coral/sky/lav/rose/sage/amber — resolved from a fixed `MuscleGroup → region` table); each `VolumeArcWeek { week, phase, planned, actual, isCurrent }` where `planned` is a scaffold projection (week 1 = MEV, deload weeks = `round(ceiling × deloadFraction)`, else ramps by `step` up to the muscle's **tier-resolved ceiling** — same `PriorityTier` resolution as the rollover above (Emphasize MRV / Grow MAV / Maintain MEV, `mezo-3m5m`) — independent of what `VolumeDecider` actually decided that week; the response's own `MuscleVolumeArc.mrv` field always reports the row's RAW `mrv` untouched by tier — only this internal scaffold ceiling shifts) and `actual` is the aggregated logged **working**-set count per `MuscleGroup` per `startDate`-anchored meso-week (a new `ExerciseSetRepository.aggregateWorkingSetsByMuscleAndDate` query, bucketed via `MesoWeeks.weekOf`, and joined on `countsTowardVolume = true` so exempt exercises never inflate the actuals) — **`null` for any week beyond `currentWeek`** (nothing logged yet). This endpoint is **read-only, ownership-filtered via `OwnershipGuard`, and deliberately NOT gated** by the volume-progression switch (a harmless read that never mutates state) — it renders even if the rollover itself is switched off. FE consumption: §2 `Mezociklus áttekintő`.

#### Plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign, `mezo-d20.7.9`)
`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate` (`MesoPlanGenerateRequest {daysOfWeek[2..6 of Hét..Vas], weeks 4..8, priorities?{group: emphasize|grow|maintain}, goalText?}` → `MesoPlanGenerateResponse {template: MesoTemplateUpsertRequest, rationale, llmUsed}`) is the single-model Hypertrophy generator; nothing is persisted — the FE posts `template` back to `createMesoTemplate` unchanged. Pipeline, all in `service/`: **`MesoPlanSkeleton`** (pure) derives the split from the day COUNT (2–3 Full · 4 Upper/Lower · 5 Upper/Lower/Push/Pull/Legs · 6 PPL×2 — every coarse group ≥2×/week by construction), week-1 sets from `PriorityTier.weekOneStart` (EMPHASIZE MEV+2, else MEV) and ceilings from `PriorityTier.ceiling`, spreads the weekly amount over the group's days (remainder earliest), and emits the phase curve (`MEV…MAV…MRV, Deload`); **`MesoPlanFiller`** (pure) fills each (day, group) frame with 1–2 catalog exercises (compound-first, stim-desc, rotating on the second weekly occurrence) — the fallback that always exists; the train-owned **`MesoPlanLlm`** port (companion's `MesoPlanLlmAdapter`, `[meso-plan]` marker, SMART tier, gated by `mezo.feature.meso-plan-ai.enabled` + the companion switch) lets Gemini pick exercises INTO the frames from the candidate list; **`MesoPlanMerger`** (pure) validates every pick (known id, right group, real day), caps accepted picks per (day, group) the same way `MesoPlanFiller` does (2 when the frame has ≥6 sets else 1, further bounded by the tunable + valid-pick count), and renormalizes sets to the frame — the model never changes volume. `MesoPlanMerger.merge` returns a `MergeResult{days, appliedPicks}`; `MesoPlanGeneratorService` reports **`llmUsed = true` only when at least one LLM pick was accepted** (`appliedPicks > 0`) — an answer that changed nothing (e.g. the fake's default `{"days":[]}`) is treated exactly like no answer: the deterministic days AND the deterministic rationale are kept. Tunables: `mezo.meso-plan.*` (`MesoPlanProperties`: 2 exercises/group/day, compound 8–10 / isolation 12–15 reps, RIR 1) — every group ≥2×/week and ≤8 sets/session hold by construction of the split table (asserted in `MesoPlanSkeletonTest`), not by a tunable. **Seeding is tier-aware since this slice:** `seedPlanBaselines`/`seedBaselines` start an EMPHASIZE group's `currentSets` at MEV+2 so the volume log and the generated program agree in week 1.

Two contract quirks worth knowing when reading `MesoPlanGenerateRequest`/`-Response`: the `pattern: '^(emphasize|grow|maintain)$'` on `priorities` map values is **not enforced by the generated DTO** (a plain `Map<String, String>` — OpenAPI map-value patterns don't reach MapStruct/Jackson validation) — an invalid tier is instead caught server-side by `PriorityTier.normalize` inside `MesoPlanGeneratorService.generate`, 400ing with `TRAIN_MUSCLE_PRIORITY_TIER_INVALID`; and `daysOfWeek`'s `uniqueItems: true` generates as a **`Set<String>`**, not a `List`, so `MesoPlanGeneratorService` re-sorts it into `MesoPlanSkeleton.DAY_ORDER` order itself rather than trusting request order.

#### Weekly bands — planning-time weekly set-band layer (client-only, `mezo-7rdg` + `mezo-d20.14`)
A **separate, FE-only layer from the volume-progression engine above** (D4 in the original design spec, `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-01-set-budget-unified-editor-design.md`): it warns the athlete **while planning** a mesocycle, on every render, with no server call and no touch of `muscle_group_volume_log`/MEV/MAV/MRV data. Source: Jeremy Ethier / Built With Science ("What is the fastest way to gain 20 lb of muscle naturally?", yt `ehQ_5TThkRI`), drawing on Dr. Mike Zourdos' set-volume + proximity-to-failure meta-analyses and Jake Remmert's per-session set-cap finding. **The wizard v2 redesign (`mezo-d20.14`) retired the old percent-pill `SetBudgetCard` entirely** — `frontend/src/features/train/logic/weeklyBands.ts`'s `weeklyBands(days, priorities, landmarks?)` and `components/WeeklyBandsCard.tsx` are the sole surviving planning-time UI everywhere `SetBudgetCard` used to render (the wizard's `Program` step, the builder's `MesoEditor`, and `MuscleWeekSheet`'s read-only Gym mirror) — no percentages shown as text anywhere, no MEV-floor "under" signal, no optimal-zone underlay: each row is just `{group, label, tier, planned, start, ceiling, pct, step}`, rendered as a **`start → ceiling`** number pair (or `{planned} szett · tart` for Maintain) with a tier chip and a `▲ +2 / hét` / `plafonon` step chip; `pct` (`planned / ceiling`) only ever drives a bar's fill width, never rendered as text.
- **Style-from-RIR — no new field (D1):** `setStyle(targetRIR)` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/setBudget.ts`) classifies every exercise straight from the existing `targetRIR` — `≤1` → **failure 🔥** (sets to failure), `≥2` → **volume 🌿** (2-3 reps short). The `MesoEditor`/`ExerciseAccordionRow` segmented toggle just writes RIR **0** or **2**; manual RIR fine-tuning re-classifies automatically.
- **`weeklyBands` itself:** one `BandRow` per coarse **budget group** (`budgetGroup(muscle)` — `chest/back/traps/shoulder/biceps/triceps/quad/ham/glute/calf/core`; off-day keys `''`/`'sport'` excluded) that carries `countsForVolume` working sets this week (`ex.workingSets` summed, exempt sets simply not counted — `weeklyBands` tracks no separate `exemptSets` field, unlike `daySessionBreakdown` below). `tier = tierOf(priorities, group)` (`logic/musclePriorities.ts`; Maintain/Grow/Emphasize); `start = weekOneSets(tier, lm)` and `ceiling = ceilingSets(tier, lm)` (both `logic/mesoPlan.ts` — Emphasize starts `MEV+2` and ramps to `MRV`, Grow `MEV → MAV`, Maintain holds at `MEV`); the landmark `lm` source is, in order, an explicit `volumePerMuscle[group]` (the mesocycle's own live `{mev,mav,mrv}`, when the caller has it) → else the static **`GROUP_LANDMARKS`** table (`setBudget.ts` — a FE mirror of the backend's `mezo.volume.baselines` RP table: chest 8/14/20 · back 10/16/22 · shoulder 8/12/18 · biceps 6/10/14 · triceps 6/10/14 · quad 8/12/18 · ham 6/10/14 · glute 8/12/18 · calf 6/10/16); a group with **no** landmark at all (`traps`/`core`) is simply **omitted** from the rows — there is no sets-only fallback row any more. Rows sort ceiling-desc (Emphasize/high-ceiling groups read first), then label.
- **The original fatigue-cap formula is retired from the UI but the pure function survives, for one caller only.** `budgetOf(failureSets, volumeSets) = failureSets/FAILURE_WEEKLY_CAP(12) + volumeSets/VOLUME_WEEKLY_CAP(20)` and its supporting table `GROUP_MEV`/`muscleBudgets`/`BudgetLevel` (incl. the retired `'under'` level and the `zoneStart` MEV-floor projection) still live in `setBudget.ts` and are still exported, but **nothing in the planning UI reads them any more** — `weeklyBands` does not call `muscleBudgets` at all. The **sole remaining consumer is `logic/weekZone.ts`** (the live done-vs-plan layer, below), which still projects `WeekZoneRow.doneBudget`/`todayBudget`/`planBudget` on the old fatigue scale and still keys its `below`/`entering`/`in`/`over` status off `GROUP_MEV`. **This is a known, deliberate gap, not an oversight:** re-basing `WeekZoneCard`/`ZoneMiniGrid`/`ZoneTrack` onto `weeklyBands`-style current→ceiling bands (and deleting `budgetOf`/`GROUP_MEV`/`FAILURE_WEEKLY_CAP`/`VOLUME_WEEKLY_CAP` once nothing calls them) is tracked as a follow-up, `mezo-d20.16`.
- **Volume exemption (budget wave-2, `mezo-0znc`; generalized from a plyo-only rule to the per-exercise flag by `mezo-gbo7`):** the shared predicate `countsForVolume(ex)` (`setBudget.ts`) — `ex.countsTowardVolume ?? ex.type !== 'plyo'`, so fixtures/plans with no flag keep the old plyo-only behaviour — decides what's **never counted toward a band or the session cap**: plyometrics (a weightless neuromuscular seed, Box/Depth Jump) and, since `mezo-gbo7`, any exercise explicitly flagged `countsTowardVolume: false` (e.g. the fix-zárás closing block). `weeklyBands` simply skips exempt sets when summing `planned`, so an **exempt-only** group never surfaces in a `WeeklyBandsCard` row at all — the per-day `daySessionBreakdown`/`DayBreakdownCard` (below) is the only surface that still reports exempt sets explicitly (`DayGroupRow.exemptSets`, kept at `sets: 0` for an exempt-only group).
- **Session cap (D3):** `sessionCapWarnings(days)` flags any day × budget-group combination whose exempt-excluded working sets (`countsForVolume`) exceed **`SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` (8** — tightened from 11 by `mezo-d20.14`) in a single session — the fix is frequency (split the same weekly volume across ≥2 days), not shown as a number here, just the warning. `wizard/StepProgram.tsx`'s `DayTile`s also render this same `SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` as their per-muscle-bar cap line.
- **Daily breakdown card (budget wave-2, `mezo-smhn`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-daily-session-breakdown-design.md`, variant A):** `daySessionBreakdown(day)` (`setBudget.ts`) is the **per-day** companion to the weekly bands above — same coarse budget-group tally but scoped to the ACTIVE day only, exempt sets split out the same way via `countsForVolume` (`DayGroupRow { group, label, colorMuscle, sets, exemptSets, over }`, `over = sets > SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP`). `leastLoadedDayFor(days, group, excludeDay)` picks the best day to move sets to when a group is over the session cap — the non-off training day (excluding `excludeDay`) with the fewest counted sets of that group, ties broken by fewest total counted sets then original day order; `null` when no other training day exists. **`DayBreakdownCard`** (`components/DayBreakdownCard.tsx`) is the presentational "Ma · izmonként" card — one bar per group (fill vs. the `SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` line, `⚠` + amber warning strip when over, suggesting `leastLoadedDayFor`'s day) — rendered nothing (`rows.length === 0`) on an empty/off day. `MesoEditor` mounts it between `MesoEditorHero` and the weekly `WeeklyBandsCard` (both levels stay visible at once — the design spec's variant B Ma/Hét switcher was rejected) and also feeds `daySessionBreakdown`'s over-groups into each `ExerciseAccordionRow`'s `highlight` prop (a red-tinted card border, `type !== 'plyo' && overGroups.has(budgetGroup(e.muscle))`).
- **Traverses `days` itself — `muscleWeek.ts` is untouched.** `setBudget.ts`/`weeklyBands.ts` run their own pass over `MesoDay[]`; neither extends `MuscleWeekRow`/`muscleWeekFromMeso` (see the design spec's §7 reality note — the logic modules stay independent, each doing its own traversal).
- **Consumers:** the unified `MesoEditor` (builder `Gyakorlatok` view via `MesoExercises` + the wizard's `Program`-step day pages via `ProgramDayView`, live while planning, §2 — hero, `DayBreakdownCard`, `WeeklyBandsCard`, and the accordion's over-row highlight + adaptive warmup chip, all fed from this module), the wizard's `Program` step itself (`StepProgram.tsx` mounts a second, week-level `WeeklyBandsCard` directly, outside `MesoEditor`), and a **read-only mirror** — `MuscleWeekSheet`'s Gym-page detail sheet (`WeeklyBandsCard` with no `defaultOpen`/collapse concept — it always shows every row, §2 `Gym`). `GymPage`'s meta card no longer mirrors this planning-time module directly — the live zone unification below renders live done-vs-plan data instead.
- **Live zone unification (mid-cycle, live, `mezo-oyhy.7`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-04-live-zone-unification-design.md`) — still on the fatigue scale, follow-up `mezo-d20.16`:** a companion layer, separate from the planning-time bands above, that projects this week's ACTUAL LOGGED sets alongside the plan. `logic/weekZone.ts`'s `weekZoneRows({ plannedDays, completed, todayPlan? })` re-derives a per-budget-group `WeekZoneRow` (`doneSets`/`todaySets`/`plannedSets` + their `budgetOf` scale equivalents `doneBudget`/`todayBudget`/`planBudget`, a `zoneStart` MEV projection via `muscleBudgets`/`GROUP_MEV`, and a `status: 'below' | 'entering' | 'in' | 'over'`) from `completed: WorkoutDetailResponse[]`. Two week-outlook fields sit beside the status (`mezo-zr6p`): **`remainingPlanSets`** (`plannedSets - doneSets - todaySets`, floored at 0 — what the plan still owes) and **`setsToZone`** (`mev - max(plannedSets, doneSets + todaySets)`, floored at 0 — what's missing once the WHOLE week's plan is counted), so a `below` row can distinguish "not there **yet**" from "the week as planned never gets there" (this week's completed instances, meso + custom) — sets price by their own logged RIR (fallback: the exercise's `targetRIR`), skip-marker/warmup rows and `plyo` exercises are excluded, mirroring `setBudget.ts`'s own exclusion rules. `data/train/weekMuscleLogHooks.ts`'s **`useWeekMuscleLog()`** (barrel: `@/data/hooks`) supplies `completed`: it takes `useWeekWorkouts()`'s this-week summaries, filters `status === 'completed'`, and fires one `getWorkout` detail query per instance (`useQueries`; disabled in mock mode, so mock is always an empty week and `pending` is forced `false` there). The **bar language is single-sourced in `ZoneTrack`** (`components/ZoneTrack.tsx` — track + `zoneStart`-to-100% sage optimal-zone underlay + ordered `solid`/`today`/`ghost`/`overflow` segments); two surfaces render through it — **`WeekZoneCard`** (prep screen, §2 Active workout — `prepSegments`: done → today → remaining-plan, `selectPrepRows` filters to groups trained today) and **`ZoneMiniGrid`** (`components/ZoneMiniGrid.tsx`, GymPage meta card, §2 `Gym` — `gymSegments`: done → remaining-plan, no today slice; `selectGymRows` filters to groups with `plannedSets > 0 || doneSets > 0`) — `ZoneTrack` is **no longer used by any weekly planning card** (`SetBudgetCard`, its only other consumer, is deleted). Marks on `ZoneMiniGrid`'s cells (`⚠`/`↓`) describe the **weekly PLAN**, not the live done state — `planBudget > 1` / `plannedSets < mev` — the same semantics the old per-head pill grid carried, one level coarser (budget group, not per-head).
- **Out of scope (D4) / independence:** no integration with `VolumeProgressionService`/the volume rollover engine above — `GROUP_MEV` is a fixed client-side table, not read from the backend's per-mesocycle MEV/MAV/MRV, so `weekZone.ts`'s optimal-zone floor and the performance-driven weekly volume ramp stay two intentionally independent layers. **Partial exception, landmarks only (`mezo-3m5m`):** `weeklyBands`/`peakWeekFit` CAN read a mesocycle's own `volumePerMuscle` MEV/MAV/MRV when the caller supplies it, falling back to the static `GROUP_LANDMARKS` mirror otherwise — but neither reads `currentSets`/the live ramp state, so the independence claim holds for the RAMP itself; only the static landmark numbers have an optional live source now. Volume counting is **direct-only** by decision (ADR `docs/decisions/0021-volume-counting-direct-only.md`) — a set counts fully toward its one `budgetGroup`, never split fractionally across overlapping muscles — and fractional cross-muscle counting stays deferred behind a synergist-catalog prerequisite (bd `mezo-oyhy.5a`, backlog). Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-03-optimal-zone-bands-design.md`.
- **Structural lint — soft structural checks (`mezo-oyhy.2`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-06-structure-lint-design.md`):** `structureLint(days)` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/structureLint.ts`) runs the RP/Helms/Nippard/Ethier structural consensus over the meso week: exercises/muscle/session (≤3, ham/traps ≤2), sets/exercise (compound 2–4, isolation 2–3, `countsForVolume`-exempt excluded), single-day frequency (≥4 weekly sets landing on one day), weekly variety (2–5 distinct movements per group), session size (5–9 slots, plyo counts), push:pull (silence band 0.6–1.6), ham:quad (<0.4 with quad ≥6) and rep-zone mono-diet (`mezo-oyhy.4`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-07-rep-zone-hint-design.md`: a group's dominant rep zone ≥80% of its ≥6 weekly sets — zone from the rep range, `repZoneOf` — heavy repMax ≤10 / light repMin ≥20 / else moderate; deliberate skews stay silent via `REP_ZONE_SKEW_OK` — shoulder→light, ham/glute→heavy) — thresholds live in exported constant tables (one place to tune), muscle→side map `PUSH_PULL_SIDE`. Pure derivation, findings are soft observations only — never blocking. `StructureLintCard` (`components/StructureLintCard.tsx`) renders the findings in `MesoEditor` below `WeeklyBandsCard`: collapsible, neutral `surface-2` explanation rows (label + why-detail per finding), a `{n} észrevétel` (amber wash) / `✓ rendben` (sage wash) header pill; never red, never force-opens (`useState(false)`, no `defaultOpen`), never blocks saving. The push:pull/ham:quad balance-rule copy explicitly flags its structural-balance-literature provenance (distinct from the RP-derived rules above). **Since `mezo-3m5m` (spec GD5) `structureLint` takes an optional `priorities` argument and goes silent for two rules on a Maintain group:** **R3** (single-day frequency) and **R4** (weekly variety) both `continue` past any budget group whose `tierOf(priorities, group) === 'maintain'` — a maintained group training once a week on one movement is the whole point of the tier, not a structural defect to flag. Every other rule (R1/R2/R5–R9, including the budget-adjacent `session-length`/`sets-per-exercise`) still evaluates Maintain groups exactly as before.
- **Session-length estimator — the app's single duration source (`mezo-oyhy.3`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-06-session-length-design.md`):** `estimateSessionMinutes(exercises)` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/sessionLength.ts`) is a pure recipe-based duration estimate — working-rep execution (`SESSION_TIME.repSeconds` 3.5s, `plyoRepSeconds` 2s for `type==='plyo'`) × `workingSets` × avg reps, inter-set rest `restSecondsFor(type)` (compound 150s / else 90s, the live rest-timer engine's own numbers) × `(workingSets−1)`, a warm-up-set block (`warmupSetSeconds` 20s + `warmupRestSeconds` 45s) × `warmupSets`, a fixed 90s `transitionSeconds` per exercise, summed over all exercises, then **one** `Math.round` on the total plus a fixed 8-minute session warm-up block (`warmupBlockMinutes`) — 0 for an empty exercise list. It is the single formula behind every duration surface: `MesoEditor`'s hero `~X perc` stat (`dayMinutes`), `structureLint`'s `session-length` rule (band `SESSION_LENGTH_BAND` 45–90 min, § above), the prep-briefing pill (`prepBriefing.ts`'s `prepStats().durationEst`), the `TrainTodayPage` gym-hero chip (`workoutMinutes`) and the `TodayPage` gym facts row (`gymMinutes`, hidden when 0 rather than showing `~0 perc`). The data layer's `WorkoutPlan.durationEst`/`WorkoutTodayResponse.durationEst` field **stays in the API contract** but as of this task **no UI reads it** — a conscious decision so every surface agrees with one formula instead of drifting between a stored estimate and a live recompute.
- **Peak-week session-time fit signal (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD6) — a DISTINCT surface from R8 above:** `peakWeekFit(days, priorities?, volumePerMuscle?)` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/peakWeekFit.ts`) projects each budget group's counted exercises onto its **CURRENT tier's landmark target** (same `tierTargetOf`/`GROUP_LANDMARKS` resolution as `muscleBudgets` above — i.e. what the week would look like once every group actually reaches its tier's weekly set target, not what it looks like today) via `projectGroup`, a pure FRONTEND MIRROR of the backend's `WorkoutService.effectiveWorkingSets` (`backend/.../WorkoutService.java:380-441`: a floor of 1 counted set per exercise, the remainder handed out proportional to each exercise's template `workingSets`, largest-fractional-remainder tie-break), then re-estimates each day's session length (`estimateSessionMinutes`, § above) against that PROJECTED plan and returns only the training days that fall outside `SESSION_LENGTH_BAND` (45–90 min) — a plan that fits its own peak week everywhere yields `[]`. **`PeakFitCard`** (`components/PeakFitCard.tsx`, "Csúcshét · időbecslés") renders those days ("Kedd: csúcshéten ~112 perc — vegyél el, vagy tedd át." / "…csúcshéten is csak ~30 perc — férne még bele inger."), modeled on `StructureLintCard`'s soft-grey/never-red/never-force-opens register, mounted in `MesoEditor` above `StructureLintCard`; renders `null` when there is nothing to flag. **R8 reads the template's OWN minutes today; `PeakFitCard` reads the projected PEAK week's** — neither merges into nor silences the other, and a plan can be R8-clean today while still flagging here (a group ramping toward Emphasize's MRV will add sets it doesn't have yet).
- **Generator fitter — RETIRED (`mezo-oyhy.6`'s `generateProgram`/`fitProgram`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-07-generator-fitter-design.md`, deleted whole by `mezo-d20.14`).** The client-side generator `frontend/src/features/train/logic/planner.ts` (goal-preset `SCHEMES`, curated `SPLIT_TEMPLATES`) and its three-phase rep-zone/volume/session-length fitter `frontend/src/features/train/logic/programFit.ts` (`fitProgram`), together with their calibrated invariant suite `planner.test.ts`, no longer exist — the wizard's `Program` step sources its plan exclusively from the backend-mirrored **plan generator** (`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`, §4 `#### Plan generator` above), whose own `MesoPlanSkeleton`/`MesoPlanFiller`/`MesoPlanMerger` pipeline (backend) and `logic/mesoPlan.ts`/`data/train/mesoPlanMock.ts` (FE mock twin) took over split-derivation, week-1/ceiling set math and exercise fill; there is no client-side fit-after-generate pass any more — the generator's split table guarantees ≥2×/week per group by construction (asserted server-side in `MesoPlanSkeletonTest`), so no removal/trim engine is needed. **`SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` is NOT such a guarantee — it is a display/lint threshold, not an invariant:** `dayFrames`/`MesoPlanSkeleton` spread a group's week-1 sets over its ≥2 training days, which keeps the DEFAULT `GROUP_LANDMARKS` week-1 numbers under the cap on every 2–6-day split, but a high per-mesocycle `volumePerMuscle` override (or any hand edit) can push a single session over it — and is then **flagged** by `sessionCapWarnings`/the red tab dot/the day tiles' cap badge, never prevented. `structureLint`/`peakWeekFit`/`estimateSessionMinutes` (below) are unaffected — they still evaluate whatever program is currently in the wizard's/builder's draft, generated or hand-edited.

#### Closing block — fix zárás (`mezo-z2ul`)
`ClosingBlockService` (`feature/train/service/ClosingBlockService.java`) **lazily ensures the configured closing exercises at the END of every non-empty template day of the active meso** — by default **Dead Hang** (2 × 45–60 mp; weightless `plyo` type → reps-are-seconds, kg-stepper hidden on the FE) and **45° Back Extension** (2 × 12–15). Triggered from `WorkoutService.getToday` behind the `ClosingBlockGate` `@ConditionalOnProperty` marker bean (`mezo.feature.closing-block.enabled`; key constant `FeaturesConfiguration.CLOSING_BLOCK_SWITCH`; mirrors `HypertrophyDriveGate`). **Idempotent** by catalog id OR case-insensitive name (a manually added copy counts, nothing duplicates); **empty (rest) template days are skipped** so the block never turns a rest day into a workout; a day-edit that strips the rows self-heals on the next `getToday`. Config: `mezo.closing-block.exercises` — `ClosingBlockProperties` (`feature/train/config/`), a `@Validated` record list of `{slug, workingSets, repMin, repMax, targetRir}` where `slug` references a **master catalog row** (a missing slug logs a warning and is skipped, never a 500). The appended rows are **ordinary catalog-linked template exercises** (`warmupSets 0`, server-side `createdBy`), so logging, records, history and prescriptions work unchanged — **no API contract change, no DDL** (the new `dead-hang` master entry ships via `content/exercise-catalog.json`; `back-extension-45` already existed). ITs: `ClosingBlockIT` (append/idempotency/multi-day/rest-day/manual-duplicate) + `ClosingBlockSwitchOffIT`.

#### Lazy auto-close of stale instances (`mezo-cd8s`)
`WorkoutAutoCloseService` (`feature/train/service/WorkoutAutoCloseService.java`) **settles abandoned workout instances** so a session left open on a past day never lingers as `active`. Invoked as the **first statement** of `WorkoutService.getToday` (before the closing-block ensure and the meso lookup, so stale instances settle even when there is no active meso). A SEPARATE `@Transactional` bean because `getToday` is a plain read and a same-class `@Transactional` self-invocation would bypass the proxy (mirrors `ClosingBlockService`). For each of the owner's `active` instances dated **strictly before today** (`WorkoutSessionRepository.findByCreatedByAndStatusAndDateBeforeAndTemplateSessionIdIsNotNull`): it becomes **`completed`** when it carries **≥1 non-skipped logged set** (the work counts — feeds `findDoneInstanceDates`, `lastWeekRefs` and the prescription engine), else **`skipped`** (a started-but-empty session never counts as done). **Idempotent** (a settled instance is no longer `active`, so a re-run is a no-op) and **always on** — no feature gate, **no DDL/contract change**. After it runs, only a **today-dated** instance can still be `active`, so the open-instance resume lookup below can never resurrect last week's abandoned session. IT: `WorkoutAutoCloseIT` (past-active-with-set → completed / past-empty → skipped / today-active → untouched).

#### Done-state = completed status + workout-detail review (`mezo-cd8s`)
The gym "done that day" signal changed from **≥1 non-skipped logged set** to **`status = 'completed'`** (an explicitly finished — or lazily auto-closed — instance): `WorkoutSessionRepository.findDoneInstanceDates` (→ `weekDoneDates`) and `findDoneInstancesBetween` (→ Insights `listWorkouts` + the companion `get_training_log` tool's `scope=gym`, §5) both switched their predicate to `s.status = 'completed'`, so a started-but-unclosed instance with logged sets is **no longer** counted (the auto-close settles stale ones, above). `lastWeekRefs` and `SetRecommendationService` already read completed-only history — unchanged. The same shift flows to every consumer of `findDoneInstanceDates`/`findMesoDoneInstanceDates` (QuestEvaluator, `TrainingCommitmentCalculator`/discipline trait, Insights weekly counts, companion tools, habit, proactive) — the agreed semantic root of the four fixes; the origin-agnostic-vs-meso-only split between those two finders is a later addition (`mezo-ws2x`, below).
- **`completedWorkout` on `getToday`** — after the auto-close, `WorkoutService.getToday` resolves the most recent `completed` instance of today's template day **dated today** (`findFirstByCreatedByAndTemplateSessionIdAndStatusAndDateOrderByCreatedAtDesc`) onto `WorkoutTodayResponse.completedWorkout` (null when none). One extra read, no second endpoint — drives the Mai Kész/Megnézem hero, `GymPage`'s day-card tap resolution (§2, direct-start flow) and the `/train/session` → review redirect (§2).
- **`GET /api/train/workouts/{id}` → `WorkoutDetailResponse`** (`WorkoutService.getWorkoutDetail`, a pure read, no `@Transactional`): the done-day review data source (weekly rows + Mai "Megnézem"). It looks up the owned **instance** (`ownedInstanceOrThrow`, instance-only) → joins the template day's exercises (`ExerciseRepository.findByCreatedByAndWorkoutSessionIdInOrderByOrderIndexAsc` on the instance's `templateSessionId`) with this instance's logged sets grouped per exercise; a skip-marker set flips the exercise's `skipped` flag and is excluded from `sets` (non-skipped, `setIndex` order). A foreign/missing id **or a template row** (`templateSessionId == null`) → **404 `TRAIN_WORKOUT_NOT_FOUND`**. ITs: `WorkoutDoneSemanticsIT` (completed counts, active-with-sets does not), `WorkoutDetailContractIT` (happy / 404 foreign / 404 template / skipped flag).

#### Saját edzés — custom workout templates (`mezo-ws2x`)
A custom (meso-less) workout template is an ordinary `workout_session` TEMPLATE row (`template_session_id IS NULL`) with **`origin='custom'`**, `mesocycleId` null, empty `dayLabel` (not weekday-bound), and its title in `type` — so the whole instance machinery (start/log/finish/records/prescriptions/"last week") works on it unchanged.
- **`workout_session.origin`** (`202607231811_mezo-ws2x_workout_session_origin.sql`) — TEXT `NOT NULL DEFAULT 'meso'`, CHECK `meso|custom` (`WorkoutSessionEntity.origin`). `WorkoutService.startWorkout` copies the template's origin onto the new instance (`instance.setOrigin(template.getOrigin())`), so every instance carries its template's provenance.
- **CRUD lives in `TrainService`** (`TrainService.java` — **supersedes the design spec's dedicated `CustomWorkoutService` naming**; a deliberate deviation, helper reuse — `toExerciseEntity`/`toDay`/`videosByCatalog` already live there). `GET /api/train/custom-workouts` (`listCustomWorkouts`, oldest-first), `POST /api/train/custom-workouts` (`createCustomWorkout` — new template, `origin='custom'`), `PUT /api/train/custom-workouts/{id}` (`updateCustomWorkout` — rename + full soft-delete+reinsert exercise replace, same pattern as `replaceDayExercises`), `DELETE /api/train/custom-workouts/{id}` (`deleteCustomWorkout` — soft delete only; completed instances + their sets survive, feeding records/history exactly like a soft-deleted meso day). All four are owner-scoped via `ownedCustomTemplateOrThrow` (missing/foreign/meso-origin/instance row → 404).
- **DTOs:** `CustomWorkoutResponse {id, name, exercises: GymExercise[]}`, `CustomWorkoutUpsertRequest {name, exercises: GymExerciseInput[]}` — the exercise shape is the same recipe DTO the meso day editor uses.
- **D5 guard exception:** `WorkoutService.startWorkout`'s "once per week" completion guard (`TRAIN_DAY_DONE_THIS_WEEK`) is **skipped for `origin='custom'` templates** — a custom day is repeatable any number of times per week. **D6 (one open workout at a time, `TRAIN_WORKOUT_OPEN_ELSEWHERE`) still applies**, unchanged and origin-agnostic.
- **`getToday` is meso-independent for custom days:** the open-instance and `?templateSessionId=` day-resolution branches (`WorkoutService.getToday`) now resolve **without** an active meso — only the weekday-label fallback (no open instance, no param) still requires one, and the closing-block ensure only runs when a meso exists. This is what lets a custom workout start and resume with zero meso in the account.
- **`completedWorkout` suppression:** `getToday` sets `completedWorkout` (→ the FE review-redirect gate) to **null for `origin='custom'` days**, however many times the day was finished this week — a custom day never gates back into a forced review, staying freely repeatable.
- **`WorkoutSessionRepository` splits into two finders (final-review fix, `mezo-ws2x`)**: `findDoneInstanceDates` stays **origin-agnostic** (its original, pre-`ws2x` semantics — any completed instance, meso or custom) and a NEW `findMesoDoneInstanceDates` adds `AND s.origin = 'meso'`. Only **plan-adherence** signals — the weekly ✓ marks (`WorkoutService.doneDatesThisWeek` → `weekDoneDates`), `QuestEvaluator` (`gym_session_done`), and the discipline trait (`TrainingCommitmentCalculator`) — read the meso-only finder, so **custom completions never tick these three**. Everywhere else that reads "was a gym session done that day" as a **real-load** signal — `HabitEvaluator` (`training_done_today`), `ContextSnapshotAssembler` (the companion `[Edzés]` block), `MetricWindowEvaluator` (the proactive volume-trend window) — stays on the origin-agnostic finder, so a custom session counts there exactly like a meso one. (An interim over-reach briefly put the meso-only filter on all six call sites; the split above is the corrected, spec-adjudicated state.) **`findDoneInstancesBetween` stays origin-agnostic** (unchanged) — Insights `listWorkouts` and the companion `get_training_log` tool's `scope=gym` count custom sessions like any other (§5).
