- **`WorkoutSummaryResponse` carries a required `origin` (`meso|custom`) + `title`** (`TrainMapper.toWorkoutSummary` — `origin` via an `OriginEnum.fromValue(...)` expression mapping, the same idiom as `status`; `title` sourced straight from `type`), so the FE's weekly agenda and `useWeekWorkouts` can distinguish + label custom rows without a second fetch.
- ITs: `CustomWorkoutIT` (13 tests — CRUD, ownership, D5-skip, D6-kept, meso-independent start/getToday, origin propagation, the `findDoneInstanceDates`/`findMesoDoneInstanceDates` split).

### Exercise catalog + records
- `exercise_catalog` (`202606121400_mezo-7ot_*`) is now **WRITABLE** (`202607082000_mezo-52zg_catalog_write.sql`, `mezo-52zg`): it gains **`created_by` UUID** (FK→`app_user` ON DELETE CASCADE — **null = master JSON content**, loader-owned; **set = user-authored**), **`is_deleted`** (soft delete for user rows only), and **`video_url` TEXT** (a demo video settable on ANY row incl. master). Master rows stay `created_by null` and are read-only.
- `ExerciseCatalogLoader` (`@Order(50)`, **every profile incl. prod**) upserts by `slug` from `backend/src/main/resources/content/exercise-catalog.json` and is now **non-clobbering**: it only writes rows whose slug appears in the JSON (so **user rows are never touched** — their slugs are unique, `ExerciseCatalogService.uniqueSlug`), and it **only seeds a `video_url`/`image_start_url`/`image_end_url` when the row has none** (`ExerciseCatalogLoader.java:84` — never overwrites a user-set demo or image). An invalid muscle/type token still fails startup fast.
- **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/exercises` (catalog, `staleTime 1h` on FE — each item now carries `editable` + `videoUrl`), **`POST /api/train/exercises`** (create a user exercise, 201), **`PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}`** / **`DELETE /api/train/exercises/{id}`** (edit / soft-delete a USER exercise — a foreign/missing row → **404**, editing/deleting a **master** row → **409 `CATALOG_MASTER_READONLY`**), **`PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}/video`** (attach/clear the demo video on ANY row incl. master; clearing = null `videoUrl`), **`PUT /api/train/exercises/{id}/images`** (`mezo-8xdl.1` — attach/clear the demo stills on ANY row incl. master; both frames written unconditionally, so a null clears that frame), `GET /api/train/exercise-records` (computed on the fly — no materialized table).
- **Service** (`ExerciseCatalogService`): `create`/`update`/`delete` are owner-scoped via `ownedOrThrow` (master `created_by null` → 409, non-owner → 404); `setVideo` and `setImages` (`mezo-8xdl.1`) find by id and write the media on any row — ownership-free, because attaching media is not authoring content; `list` maps each row's `editable = created_by == currentUser`. Method-level `@Transactional` on the writes.
- `ExerciseRecordService.list` resolves an exercise identity by `exercise.catalog_id` else name over **ALL rows incl. soft-deleted** (`ExerciseRepository.findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted` native SQL — day-edits soft-delete template rows but must not erase history), computing `bestSet`, `bestE1rm` (Epley `w×(30+reps)/30`), `bestSessionVolume`, totals, `sessionCount`, `repRecords`, `recentTopSets`.
- **Medals (`mezo-wp6n`) — derived, not materialized (ADR 0016).** `MedalService.list` (mirrors `ExerciseRecordService.list` right above — same identity resolution, same `findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted` read) replays the owner's working sets chronologically per identity through the pure `MedalEvaluator` (`feature/train/service/MedalEvaluator.java`, no Spring, no DB — the `ProgressionDecider` idiom) to derive the four `RECORD`-tier medals (`WEIGHT`/`REPS_AT_WEIGHT`/`E1RM`/`SESSION_VOLUME`, strict `>`, a baseline earns nothing) plus the history-independent `TARGET_HIT` (weight/reps both ≥ the row's own stored target) on every read — no `set_medal` table exists. `MedalService` also serves `forSet(createdBy, setId)` (keyed on the set id, since two exercises can share `setIndex 0` in one instance) and `forSession(createdBy, workoutSessionId)` (adds `SESSION_VOLUME`, which is exercise-scoped-per-session and never appears on a set row). **`GET /api/train/medals`** (`TrainController` → `MedalService.list`) → `MedalListResponse { medals: [Medal] }` serves the `Medálok` cabinet (§2); `WorkoutService.logSet` attaches a set's `medals[]` onto `ExerciseSetResponse`, and `finishWorkout` attaches the session's onto `WorkoutInstanceResponse`.
- **`exercise_set` target snapshot (`mezo-wp6n`).** Migration `202607301900_mezo-wp6n_exercise_set_target.sql` adds `target_weight_kg NUMERIC(6,2) NULL` and `target_reps INTEGER NULL`, written server-side from the client-supplied prescription snapshot at log time (`SetLogRequest.targetWeightKg`/`targetReps`). Null means no prescription was in force (first session, engine off, or a pre-existing row) — such a set can never earn a `TARGET_HIT` and isn't a miss either. This is the one fact the derived medal model needs persisted, because `ProgressionSignal` is recomputed from the *latest* history on every read and a past set's prescription is otherwise unreconstructable — see ADR 0016. Consequence: the four RECORD types backfill for free over all existing history; `TARGET_HIT` accrues only from this migration forward.
- **Media resolution onto exercises:** `GymExercise` (meso day) and `TodayExercise` (workout) carry a nullable **`videoUrl`** plus (since `mezo-8xdl.1`) **`imageStartUrl`/`imageEndUrl`**, all resolved server-side from the linked `exercise_catalog` row (via `catalog_id`) in **ONE batched fetch** by `CatalogMediaResolver` (`feature/train/service/`, renamed from `CatalogVideoResolver`; returns `Map<UUID, CatalogMedia>` where `CatalogMedia(videoUrl, imageStartUrl, imageEndUrl)` — a row with no media at all is absent from the map, and the `catalogId != null` guard stays at the two call sites, `WorkoutService.getToday` + `TrainService.toDay`). The FE therefore renders the `▶ Demo` and the stills without a second fetch (§2 active workout, §5 catalog seam).
- **Demo stills (`mezo-8xdl.1`) — the two-frame image pair.** `exercise_catalog` gains **`image_start_url` + `image_end_url`** (`202608052135_mezo-8xdl.1_exercise_catalog_images.sql`, both TEXT NULL, no constraint). The shape mirrors the source data: the public-domain [free-exercise-db](https://github.com/yuhonas/free-exercise-db) ships **exactly 2 photos per exercise** (start + end position, same camera), and **alternating them is what conveys the movement** — a single still often does not. **`image_start_url` is the presence flag**: no start frame ⇒ the exercise has no image, and a half-populated pair resolves as-is rather than being dropped. Values are same-origin vendored paths (`/exercises/{slug}-a.jpg`) or an absolute URL — a free URL field, so a user-attached absolute link needs no schema change (exactly how `video_url` behaves). The loader seeds each frame **only when the row has none**, so a redeploy never clobbers a user-attached image. Design spec: [`2026-08-05-exercise-demo-images-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-05-exercise-demo-images-design.md); source scan: [`queries/2026-08-05-exercise-media-apis.md`](../research/queries/2026-08-05-exercise-media-apis.md). **Assets (`mezo-8xdl.2`, shipped):** **124 of the 161 rows** carry vendored frames under `frontend/public/exercises/{slug}-a.jpg|-b.jpg` (248 files, ~15 MB, public domain). The slug → dataset-name map is **hand-curated** in `scripts/data/exercise-image-map.json` and imported by `node scripts/import-exercise-images.mjs [--write-catalog] [--force]` (dependency-free, run by hand, never in CI; it validates the whole map before downloading anything, stamps the relative paths onto the content JSON, and prints the unmapped list). **Fuzzy matching is disqualified, not merely imperfect** — on this catalog it paired `Pull-Up → Scapular Pull-Up` and `Cable Fly → Cable Rear Delt Fly`; a wrong image teaches the wrong movement. **The remaining 37 rows render imageless** (incl. `kb-swing`, `kb-halo`, both carries) — a specced state. **The images are excluded from the workbox precache** (`globIgnores: ['**/exercises/**']` + no `jpg` in `globPatterns`, `vite.config.ts`) and served through a `CacheFirst` `exercise-images` runtime cache instead: the precache is the install cost and demo photos are not install-critical (precache stays 25 entries / 1.8 MB). Rationale + accepted costs: [ADR 0020](../decisions/0020-vendor-public-domain-exercise-imagery.md). **FE (`mezo-8xdl.3`, shipped):** `features/train/components/ExerciseImage.tsx` — `hero` (3:2, crossfades the two frames every 1.2 s) and `thumb` (44 px, start frame only). **`start` is the presence flag**: `hero` renders **nothing** without it (no empty placeholder), while `thumb` falls back to a muscle-wash tile with the exercise's initial so list rows keep a straight left edge. **`prefers-reduced-motion` does not simply drop the alternation** — which frame you are looking at is *information*, so it becomes a manual `⇄` toggle. The photos' tonal clash with the DS is reconciled **inside the component** (`.exdemo*` in `prototype.css`: `saturate(.88)`, `brightness(.9)` in dark, DS radius, and the muscle rail continuing along the leading edge — the `mezo-kaui` card language), not per call site. Wired into **`ExerciseRecordSheet`** as the proof surface: the image sits **above** the unchanged `▶ Demo` chip — the still is the cheap always-on layer, the video stays the opt-in deep one. `ExerciseLibraryItem` carries `imageStartUrl`/`imageEndUrl` (mapped in `toLibraryItem`), resolved on the page by the same `catalogId`-then-name fallback as `videoUrl`. **The other three surfaces (picker thumb, active workout, prep + catalog cards) are deliberately deferred into the DS migration** (`mezo-8xdl.4` → `mezo-setx.6.7`/`.6.14`/`.6.17`) so that UI is written once, on the new component language. **Now wired (`mezo-8xdl.4`, shipped):** the `thumb` variant renders on the `Gyakorlatok` catalog cards (`ExercisesPage` — the rank plaque collapsed to a `#n` prefix to make room for it), the mesocycle builder's `ExercisePickerSheet` rows, and `PrepExerciseCard`; the active-workout `.excard` instead gets a tap-to-reveal **`⛶ Kép`** chip (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`, beside the existing `▶ Demo` chip) that mounts the `hero` crossfade only on tap, so the still never steals the live logging surface's initial paint.
- **DTOs:** `ExerciseCatalogItem` (adds `slug` vs the FE `ExerciseLibraryItem`, plus **`editable` + `videoUrl` + `imageStartUrl`/`imageEndUrl`**), **`CatalogExerciseCreateRequest`** (`{name, muscle (21-token enum, mezo-wu1s), type (compound|isolation|plyo), stim 0–1, fatigue 0–1, videoUrl?, imageStartUrl?, imageEndUrl?}` — on CREATE the media fields are set-only-when-present, on UPDATE they are written unconditionally so a null clears), **`CatalogVideoRequest`** (`{videoUrl?}` — null clears) and **`CatalogImagesRequest`** (`{imageStartUrl?, imageEndUrl?}` — nulls clear, `mezo-8xdl.1`), `ExerciseRecordResponse`, `RecordSetRef`, `E1rmRecord`, `SessionVolumeRecord`.
- **Content:** 2 new plyometric master entries seeded via `content/exercise-catalog.json` — `db-jump-squat` (DB Jump Squat, quad) and `single-leg-plate-hop` (Single-Leg Plate Hop, calf) — feeding the new `Láb+Plyo / Felső` split; plus `dead-hang` (Dead Hang, lats, `plyo` = weightless) backing the fix-zárás closing block (`mezo-z2ul`).
- **Kettlebell block (`mezo-18g3`) — 22 master entries, catalog 139 → 161.** A `kb-*` slug family appended to `content/exercise-catalog.json` covering the hardstyle canon: ballistics/hinge (`kb-swing`, `kb-single-arm-swing`, `kb-deadlift`, `kb-single-leg-rdl`, `kb-clean`, `kb-high-pull`, `kb-snatch`), presses (`kb-military-press`, `kb-push-press`, `kb-clean-and-press`, `kb-bottoms-up-press`, `kb-halo`), squat/lunge (`kb-goblet-squat`, `kb-front-squat`, `kb-thruster`, `kb-racked-lunge`), pull (`kb-row`, `kb-renegade-row`) and core/carry (`kb-turkish-get-up`, `kb-windmill`, `kb-suitcase-carry`, `kb-front-rack-carry`). **Every name is prefixed `Kettlebell …`** — there is no equipment column, and the `Gyakorlatok` browser's search matches on `name` only (`ExercisesPage.tsx:268`), so the prefix IS the equipment filter. **Type is `compound`/`isolation`, never `plyo`** even for the ballistic swings/snatches: a `plyo` row hides the kg stepper on the FE and logs `weightKg 0` (reps-only), which would throw away the bell weight. `videoUrl` is left null — attach demos per row from the UI (`VideoUrlSheet`) or seed them in the JSON later. Because the loader runs in **every profile**, a rollout to prod upserts these into the live DB with no migration and no manual SQL. The two catalog-size assertions (`ExerciseCatalogLoaderIT`, `ExerciseCatalogContractIT`) moved 139 → 161.

### Sport / volleyball · cross · TRX
- **Tables:** `sport_session` — a **3-kind modality** since `mezo-lmox` (`202606271000_mezo-lmox_generalize_sport_session.sql`): the existing `sport` column is the typed discriminator (`ck_sport_session_sport` CHECK `volleyball|cross|trx`) and a `rounds` INT effort column was added for cross/TRX; the original table (`202606111400_mezo-n5q_create_train.sql`) carries intensity/shoulder_strain CHECK 1-10 + `jump_count`. `sport_schedule_slot` (added in `202606121000_mezo-0ae_t3_sport_schedule.sql`; `day_of_week` SMALLINT 0=Hét..6=Vas CHECK, `kind` training|match CHECK) **gains the same discriminator as `sport_session`** (`mezo-05v6`, `202607141200_mezo-05v6_sport_schedule_slot_sport.sql`): `sport TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'volleyball'` + `ck_sport_schedule_slot_sport` CHECK `volleyball|cross|trx` — the migration back-fills every existing row to `volleyball` via the column default, so no data migration script was needed.
- **Endpoints:** `GET/POST /api/train/sport-sessions`, `GET/PUT /api/train/sport-schedule` (`replaceSchedule` = full soft-delete + re-insert).
- **Range read (`mezo-d20.7.1`, Design 2.0 F6.1):** `GET /api/train/sport-sessions` takes optional inclusive `from`/`to` query dates — the Sport `Napló` 4-week idő+RPE trend needs a bounded window instead of the whole log. **No migration** (pure read over `sport_session.date`, which is already indexed by owner). Both bounds are optional and the old call is byte-identical: neither given → the historical whole-log, date-descending read; a single bound left out stays unbounded on that side (`findBy…DateGreaterThanEqual…` / `…DateLessThanEqual…`). With **both** present the range is validated in `TrainService.listSportSessions` — `from > to` → 400 `TRAIN_INVALID_DATE_RANGE`, span wider than `mezo.train.sport-session-max-span-days` (400 days, `TrainProperties.sportSessionMaxSpanDays`) → 400 `TRAIN_DATE_RANGE_TOO_WIDE`. The open-ended forms are deliberately unguarded: they ARE the pre-existing behaviour. Every branch goes through an ownership-scoped finder. Focused IT: `SportSessionRangeIT` (default / multi-week window / single-day / open-ended / both 400s / cross-user isolation) + two HTTP cases in `SportContractIT`.
- **DTOs:** `SportSessionResponse` (now `+ rounds?`, `+ levelUp?`; `setsPlayed`/`shoulderStrain` are volleyball-only → optional), `SportSessionCreateRequest {sport? (pattern volleyball|cross|trx), duration, rpe, setsPlayed?, shoulderStrain?, rounds?, notes?}` — only `duration`+`rpe` are required; `sport` defaults to `"volleyball"` server-side. **`SportScheduleSlotInput` gains optional `sport?`** (pattern `volleyball|cross|trx`; omitted → `SportService.replaceSchedule` defaults the entity to `"volleyball"`, `SportService.java:93`) and **`SportScheduleSlotResponse` gains required `sport`** (`SportScheduleSlotEntity.sport`, entity-level default `"volleyball"`) — the round-trip mirrors `SportSessionCreateRequest`/`SportSessionResponse`'s existing discriminator pattern. Server defaults session date/time to **now** (the sheet has no date picker).
- **Gap closed (`mezo-05v6`):** ✅ the weekly sport schedule previously assumed **max one slot per day, always volleyball** — a recurring non-volleyball rhythm (e.g. Tue/Thu 12:00–13:00 TRX) or a mixed day (TRX + volleyball on the same day) simply **could not be scheduled** and never appeared in `Heti terv`/`Mai`. The `sport` discriminator + the FE per-day slot-list editor (`SportScheduleSheet`, above) + the multi-slot agenda model (`WeeklyAgendaDay.sport: VolleyballSession[]`, §2 `Mai`) **close this gap**: any weekday can now hold any number of slots of any sport, matched date+sport for done-state.
- **One-off events (`mezo-e1sp`):** dated, non-recurring sessions/matches outside the weekly rhythm. **Table `sport_event`** (`202608010400_mezo-e1sp_create_sport_event.sql`) mirrors `sport_schedule_slot` with a concrete `date DATE NOT NULL` instead of `day_of_week`; `kind` (default `'training'`) / `sport` (default `'volleyball'`) reuse the same CHECK vocabularies; `idx_sport_event_created_by_date`. Unlike the slots it is **long-lived** (individually created/soft-deleted, never full-replaced). **Endpoints:** `GET /api/train/sport-events` (optional `from`/`to` range), `POST` (`SportEventCreateRequest {date!, time!, durationMin!, kind?, sport?, location?, intensityLabel?}` → 201 `SportEventResponse`), `DELETE /api/train/sport-events/{id}` (204; foreign/absent → 404 via `OwnershipGuard`). `SportService.listEvents/createEvent/deleteEvent`, `SportEventEntity/Repository`, `TrainMapper.toEventResponse`. **FE:** `trainApi.sportEvents/createSportEvent/deleteSportEvent`; `useTrain` loads the full list (`['train','sportEvents']`), exposes it as `sportEvents` + `addSportEvent`/`deleteSportEvent`, and **merges the current Mon–Sun week's events into `sport.schedule`** (`mergeEventsIntoSchedule` — `VolleyballSession` gained optional `date`/`oneOff` fields; `buildWeekAgenda` date-guards dated slots so a one-off never repeats on other weeks' same weekday). `deriveBlocks` (fuel) now emits **one sport block per today-session** (`.filter`, was `.find`) so a stacked day (recurring training + one-off match) counts fully in the daily calorie budget/meal windows. **`WorkoutWindowQueryService.addSportWindows` pools the date's one-off events with the weekday's recurring slots** (shared `PlannedSport` record) for the meal scorer's pre/post-workout windows. NOT merged into `WeeklyScheduledActivityService`'s weekly average by design — a one-off doesn't belong in the steady-state TDEE bootstrap.
- **Progression (`mezo-lmox`):** **`POST /api/train/sport-sessions` now returns an optional `levelUp`** (mirrors the gym finish + run log): `SportService.logSportSession` runs `SportSignalCalculator.compute` (train-side, `feature/train/signal/`) → `ProgressionService.applySport` → `LevelUpResultMapper.toDto` inside its `@Transactional`, **only when `mezo.feature.progression.enabled` is on** (the shared `ProgressionGate` bean, injected via `ObjectProvider`); idempotent on the saved session id; the GET list never carries it. Per-kind athletic XP (volleyball→vertical_jump/agility/coordination/explosiveness/aerobic_capacity from `setsPlayed`+rpe+min; cross→anaerobic_capacity/strength_endurance/explosiveness/core_stability from `rounds`+rpe; trx→core_stability/strength_endurance/anaerobic_capacity/mobility from `rounds`+rpe+min) is config-driven (`mezo.progression.sport`). See the progression backend doc (`_platform-api-backend.md` §4e); the FE now **captures** this `levelUp` and presents it via the shared `LevelUpProvider` host + `LevelUpScreen` overlay (P5, §2 active-workout COMPLETE phase + the sport/run log flows).
- 🟣 `jumpCount` exists in the DB/DTO but the log sheet never captures it (volleyball scores off `setsPlayed`, not jumpCount); `intensity` is never written by the log path; `team`/`season`/`weeklyHours` have no DB home. **The cross/TRX log-sheet UI shipped in P5** (`mezo-te8k`): `SportLogSheet` now has a `Röpi | Cross | TRX` kind selector (de-hardcoded from volleyball) — volleyball sends `{sport:'volleyball', setsPlayed, shoulderStrain}`, cross/TRX send `{sport:'cross'|'trx', rounds}` (only `duration`+`rpe` are always required); the volleyball-only shoulder field + Mezo-observation copy hide for cross/TRX.

### Gym schedule (`GymScheduleSlot` — weekday → time)
A **standalone** owner-scoped weekly schedule answering only *when* the user trains on each weekday; it **persists across mesocycles** (the active meso supplies *what*, this supplies *when*). Mirrors `sport_schedule_slot`, minus the volleyball-specific columns. This is the Option-Y choice (standalone schedule, not time-on-the-meso-day) — see the X-vs-Y decision in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-15-gym-schedule-times-design.md`. **Edited from two places (both write the `PUT`): the mesocycle planner Step 2 (a per-day time picker, prefilled from the standing slots — the primary editor, set when you plan a block) and the Gym-page "Időpontok" chip (mid-cycle tweaks). Fuel only *reads* it. Y+ refinement: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-gym-times-in-planner-design.md` (`mezo-4t43`).**
- **Table:** `gym_schedule_slot` (`202606151500_mezo-auk_create_gym_schedule_slot.sql`) — `day_of_week` SMALLINT (0=Hét..6=Vas, `ck_gym_schedule_slot_day_of_week` CHECK 0–6), `time` VARCHAR(5), plus the `OwnedEntity` columns; `idx_gym_schedule_slot_created_by_day_of_week`. Lean by design: **no** duration/kind/location.
- **Entity / repository:** `GymScheduleSlotEntity extends OwnedEntity` (`dayOfWeek`, `time`); `GymScheduleSlotRepository.findByCreatedByAndDeletedFalseOrderByDayOfWeekAscTimeAsc`.
- **Service:** `GymScheduleService` — `getSchedule` + a **replace-all** `replaceSchedule` (soft-delete the owner's current week via `@SQLDelete`, then re-insert; method-level `@Transactional` on the write only), exactly mirroring `SportService`'s schedule methods. Ownership stamped server-side, never from the client.
- **Endpoints:** `GET/PUT /api/train/gym-schedule` (`TrainController.getGymSchedule`/`putGymSchedule` delegate to `GymScheduleService`); PUT body is the full slot list and returns the new list.
- **DTOs / mapper:** `GymScheduleSlotResponse {id, dayOfWeek 0–6, time '^\d{2}:\d{2}$'}`, `GymScheduleSlotInput {dayOfWeek, time}` (`api/feature/train/train.yml`); `TrainMapper.toGymSlotResponse` (entity→DTO). The FE joins these onto the active meso's gym days via `deriveGymSchedule` (§3).

### Running (`Futás`)
- **Tables** (`202606141200`/`202606141210_mezo-b4n_*`): `running_block` (status planned|active|archived CHECK, kind interval CHECK, `structure jsonb`), `run_session_log` (FK→block CASCADE, `rpe_actual` CHECK 1-10).
- **The plan is typed jsonb:** `RunningBlockStructure` (`entity/RunningBlockStructure.java`) = `weeks[ {weekNumber, phaseLabel, sessions[ {key, dayOfWeek 0=Hét..6=Vas, timeOfDay "HH:mm" (nullable), label, kind sprint|pyramid|steady, rpeTarget{min,max}, rounds?, segments[ {type warmup|work|rest|cooldown, durationSec, label} ]} ]} ]`, persisted via `@JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)`; `RunningMapper` round-trips it field-for-field to `RunningBlockStructureDto`.
- **`timeOfDay` rides the `structure` jsonb — NO Liquibase migration.** It's a nullable `HH:mm` field added to `RunPrescribedSession` (`RunningBlockStructure.java:17`, OpenAPI `train.yml` `RunPrescribedSession.timeOfDay` `pattern ^\d{2}:\d{2}$`); MapStruct + Hibernate `@JdbcTypeCode(JSON)` round-trip it with no DDL change. The pre-existing `dayOfWeek` is now **user-editable** in the builder (`WeekdayGrid`). Both are **plan-level**: the FE `setSessionDay`/`setSessionTime` editors (`runningDraft.ts:59-64`) apply one edit to the same-`key` session in **every** week, since weekday/time are constant across weeks.
- **Endpoints:** `GET/POST /api/train/running-blocks`, `PUT/DELETE /api/train/running-blocks/{id}`, `POST .../{id}/activate`, `POST .../{id}/close`, `GET/POST /api/train/run-sessions`. `RunningService` enforces single-active on activate; `deleteBlock` is soft delete. **`POST /api/train/run-sessions` now returns an optional `levelUp`** (the gamified-progression payload, just like the gym `.../{id}/finish`): `RunningService.logSession` computes + attaches it inside its `@Transactional`, but **only when the `mezo.feature.progression.enabled` switch is on** (the same `ProgressionGate` `@ConditionalOnProperty` bean, injected via `ObjectProvider`). When on, it runs `RunSignalCalculator.compute` (train-side, `feature/train/signal/`) → `ProgressionService.applyRun` → `LevelUpResultMapper.toDto`, idempotent on the saved log id; the GET list path never carries it. See the progression backend doc (`_platform-api-backend.md` §4e); the FE now **captures** this `levelUp` and presents it via the shared `LevelUpProvider` host + `LevelUpScreen` overlay (P5, §2 active-workout COMPLETE phase + the sport/run log flows).
- **`currentWeek` is server-derived, not client-owned (mezo-478).** `applyUpsert` sets it to `clampWeek(startDate, weeks)` — the 1-based week containing today, clamped to `[1, weeks]` (week 1 before the start) — ignoring the request's `currentWeek` (mirrors `TrainService` for mesocycles). Reads additionally **heal** a stale/invalid stored value (null, `<1`, or `>weeks`) via `RunningService.toResponse`, so a block written before this (e.g. the legacy `currentWeek 0` that rendered "az aktuális hét (0) nincs a tervben" for a plan starting today) self-corrects on the next fetch. Weeks are 1-indexed; the `RunWeekView` `weeks.find(w => w.weekNumber === currentWeek)` therefore always resolves for an in-range plan. Mock mode mirrors the derivation via `currentWeekOf` (`shared/lib/dates.ts`). _Known limitation:_ like mesocycles, the stored value only re-derives on write/activate; the read-time heal only fires for out-of-range values, so a valid block does not auto-advance week-to-week without a write.
- **DTOs:** `RunningBlockResponse`/`RunningBlockUpsertRequest`, `RunningBlockStructureDto`, `RunWeek`, `RunPrescribedSession`, `RunSegment`, `RpeTarget`, `RunSessionLogResponse` (now also carries an optional `levelUp` → `LevelUpResult`, the same schema as the gym finish)/`RunSessionLogRequest`.
- **Seed:** `RunningSeedData` is `@Profile("demofixtures")` (same as `TrainSeedData`, since `mezo-uuv`) — a plain `demodata` (prod) app starts **clean** with no running blocks; the 3 demo blocks (active/planned/archived) require the opt-in `demodata,demofixtures` profile combo.

---

## 5. Integrations

This is the highest-value section: Train both consumes and exposes a number of seams. The contract (the type/shape that crosses) is named at each.

| Seam | Direction | Where | Type crossing |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Single data boundary** | Train ↔ app | `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts:202-203` re-exports `useTrain`, `useRunning` | hook return types `TrainData`, `RunningData` |
| **Day-type seam → Growth quests** (`mezo-df7q`) | Train → Quest | `WorkoutService.findPlannedTemplateForDate` (extracted from `getToday` — planned template for a date via active meso + HU day label); `QuestEvaluator` reads the meso-only `findMesoDoneInstanceDates` (plan-adherence, `mezo-ws2x`) | `Optional<WorkoutSessionEntity>` (present → GYM day, absent → REST); see [`growth.md`](growth.md). `LevelUpResult.source`/`Gain.kind` contract enums extended (`QUEST`/`LIFE`) in `train.yml` |
| **Discipline-trait port** (Growth E2, `mezo-jzca`) | Train → Progression | `feature/train/signal/TrainingCommitmentCalculator` implements the progression `TrainingCommitmentSource` port — planned (active-meso template weekdays in a window) vs done (distinct done-instance dates via the meso-only `findMesoDoneInstanceDates`, plan-adherence, `mezo-ws2x`) | `TrainingCommitmentSource.Stats{planned, done}`; feeds the growth **discipline** trait (`TraitCalculator`, [`growth.md`](growth.md) §3). One-directional (train → progression), same pattern as `RobustnessSource`. `LevelUpResult.source` enum also gained **`ACTIVITY`** in `train.yml` |
| **`Mai` aggregation** | GYM/Sport ↔ Running | `pages/TrainTodayPage.tsx` composes BOTH hooks | `WeeklyAgendaDay`, `RunPrescribedSession`, `WorkoutPlan`, `SportSchedule` |
| **Sleep goal → Train** (read, `mezo-67rb`) | Sleep goal → Train | `TrainTodayPage` reads `useSleepGoal().goal.wakeTime` → `features/train/logic/morningWindow.ts` derives the [wake+60′, wake+6h] window that gates `MorningTrainingCard` (§2 Mai) | `SleepGoal.wakeTime` → `MorningWindow{start,end}` — a Train-only circadian window; **since `mezo-u6jx`** no longer a backend mirror (habit M5 `training_done_today` is date-presence, [habit.md](habit.md) §5). One-way (Train never writes the goal) |
| **Sport → all systems (cross-load)** | Sport → Train/Fuel/Sleep/Weight/Insights | `data/train/train.ts` `sport.crossLoad` + `SYSTEM_LABELS`; rendered by `CrossLoadRow` | `CrossLoadRow {target,impact,why,system,warning}` — 🟣 **mock-only; engine Phase 3** (real mode = `crossLoad: null` → view ghosts) |
| **Running → GYM (cross-load)** | Running → Train leg volume | `components/RunCrossLoadCard.tsx` | static presentational text — 🟣 **Phase-3 engine** |
| **Volume recompute provenance** | `VolumeProgressionService` (weekly performance ramp) → meso volume | `MesoMusclePage`'s `DerivationSteps` (was `MesoVolume`/`MesoOverviewPage`, deleted in mesocycle pages v2), `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity.source` (`ProvenanceEnvelope`), `VolumeRecomputeJson` | `VolumeSource`/`VolumeAdjustment` — **the weekly performance ramp is LIVE** (`mezo-hi9m`, behind `volume-progression`); 🟣 the **cross-load** contribution (Sport/Running load feeding the same recompute) is **still Phase-3** |
| **Catalog ↔ planner/editor** | Exercises → meso days | `ExercisePickerSheet` writes `GymExerciseInput.catalogId` | `ExerciseLibraryItem.catalogId` → `exercise.catalog_id` FK |
| **Records ← sets** | Workout execution → Exercises | `ExerciseRecordService` aggregates `exercise_set` (incl. soft-deleted) | `ExerciseSetEntity` → `ExerciseRecordResponse` |
| **Auth / ownership** | Train ↔ techcore | every service takes `UUID createdBy` first; entities extend `OwnedEntity` | `CurrentUserId`, `created_by` |
| **Date formatting** | Train ↔ lib | `huMonthDay`/`huMonthDayDow` (`@/shared/lib/dates`) | ISO date strings → HU display |
| **Error contract** | Train ↔ techcore | `SystemRuntimeErrorException` + `SystemMessage` | `SystemMessageList` (401/404/409 etc.) |
| **Design system** | Train ↔ shared UI | `Sheet`, `GhostState`, ui primitives; tokens in `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css` | **Since Design 2.0 (`mezo-d20.3.x`)** the re-faced pages (hub, Heti, Medálok, Gyakorlatok, Mezociklus + its full-screen siblings, the whole session flow) additionally consume the **Mozaik primitives** `@/shared/ui/mozaik` (`Tile`/`Mosaic`/`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`/`StatStrip`/`StatCell`/`CollapsibleStrip` + `motion.tsx`'s `EntranceGroup`), the **clay SVG sprites** `@/shared/ui/clay` (`ClayIcon`/`ClaySpot` — no emoji in the new faces), and the `--mz-*` token family in `styles/prototype.css`. Older layers still stand underneath: category palette `--cat-*`; **since Napiv S4 (`mezo-8141`)** `Mai`/`Sport`/`Futás` consume the session-type tokens `--tag-gym/-sport/-run` + `--wash-gym/-sport/-run` (coral/rose/sky) instead — superseding the pre-S4 blue `--info` (Futás) / pink `--cat-tendency` (Sport) accents, which since the S8 cleanup (`mezo-mifi`) are **fully drained from `features/train`** (migrated to `--rose`/`--sky`); the un-re-skinned Mesociklusok/Builder/Planner body content now runs on `--coral`, not the legacy accents (§9); Gyakorlatok cards run on the muscle-family tokens via `logic/muscleColors.ts` since `mezo-kaui` |
| **Context snapshot** | Companion → Train (read-only, ✅ V0.3) | `ContextSnapshotAssembler` ([`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.5) reads active meso, `GymScheduleService.getSchedule`, `SportService.getSchedule`, the origin-agnostic `findDoneInstanceDates` (real-load signal, `mezo-ws2x` — a custom instance counts here) + the V0.3 since-date finders on `SportSessionRepository`/`RunSessionLogRepository` | rendered `[Edzés]` block in the chat system prompt; strictly one-way (Train never imports companion) |
| **Chat tools** | Companion → Train (read-only, ✅ V0.5) | `TrainTools` (`get_training_log(scope, days)`, mezo-xixu — merged from `get_recent_workouts`+`get_sport_sessions`) reuse the same reads + the V0.5 plain finder `WorkoutSessionRepository.findDoneInstancesBetween` (the entities variant of `findDoneInstanceDates`, **completed-only semantics since `mezo-cd8s`** — was ≥1-logged-set) + per-instance sets via `ExerciseSetRepository` | tool results in chat answers + `Workout`/`Sport`/`Run` ref chips; still strictly one-way |
| **Meso AI review** (`mezo-meyc.3`, S3) | Train → Companion (event) → Train (write-back) | Train publishes `MesocycleClosed(userId, mesocycleId)` in the close/regenerate transaction (`MesocycleReportService`, §4); the companion's `MesoReviewListener` (`feature/companion/service/`, `@Async @TransactionalEventListener(AFTER_COMMIT)`) hands it to `MesoReviewGenerator`, which assembles the lifestyle context (`MesoContextAssembler` over `MetricSeriesService`) and writes `mesocycle_report.context` + `ai_eval`/`ai_eval_status`/`ai_eval_generated_at` back through Train's own `MesocycleReportRepository`. Gated by the **train-owned** `MesoReviewGate` (`mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled`) which the generator reads via `ObjectProvider`: switch off ⇒ the context IS still written, only the narrative is skipped and the row stays `pending` (`aiEvalEnabled: false`, so the FE hides the section instead of polling). | `MesocycleClosed` record out; `MesoContextJson` (train-owned, `entity/json/`) + narrative text back in. Train never imports companion — the direction is one-way both ways round (event out, repository write in), which is what keeps `feature_slices_are_cycle_free` satisfied. Full description in [`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.6 |
| **Workout challenges** | Proactive → Train (FE consume + write; BE read of Train repos) | `ActiveWorkoutPage` prep carousel consumes `useChallenges`/`useChallengeActions` (`data/train/challengeHooks.ts`) over `/api/proactive/challenge`; the backend `ChallengeOutcomeEvaluator` reads `exercise_set` rows FK'd to the template exercise. Strictly one-way (Train never imports proactive; challenges are NOT in `WorkoutPlan`). | FE `Challenge` (`@/data/types`); `ChallengeResponse`/`ChallengeDecisionRequest` on the wire — full lifecycle in [proactive.md](proactive.md) §HBWI/§5.10 |
| **Full-bleed session route** (Napiv S5, `mezo-8141`) | Train → App shell | `AppLayout.tsx` hides the bottom `TabBar` when `location.pathname === '/train/session'` (the session's own `.wk-top` header owns the back affordance) | boolean `hideChrome` (`hideTabBar` until `mezo-atry`, which extended it to the shell header and the FAB), a route-string match only — no route metadata/config involved. (The old **rest Live-Activity shell seam** — `LiveActivityProvider` + `DynamicIsland` — was deleted by `mezo-xt65`: the rest timer is feature-local now, §2's Rest timer paragraph.) |
| **Level-up overlay** | Train → Progression | all 3 finish flows (gym `finishWorkout`, sport/run log sheets) call `useLevelUp().showLevelUp(r?.levelUp)`; the single `LevelUpProvider` host (`features/progression/`, mounted in `AppLayout`) portals `LevelUpScreen` over the chrome. The same 3 mutations also **invalidate `['progressionProfile']`** (real mode) so the P6 Me/Profile radar + muscle cards refresh with the just-earned XP (`docs/features/me.md` §3). | `LevelUpResult` (`@/data/train/trainApi`); sheets expose `onSave?: (input, done) => void` (deferred close — the parent shows the overlay in the mutation's `onSuccess` and calls `done()` in `onSettled`, so the sheet closes on both success **and** failure — no stuck-disabled CTA) |
| **Account XP/coins/streak** (`mezo-k7rn`, mock-mode only) | Train → Gamification | mock-mode `finishWorkout` (`trainHooks.ts:387`, `type: 'GYM'`) and `logSportSession` (`trainHooks.ts:417`, `type: 'SPORT'`) call `awardGamificationEvent`; `useRunning`'s mock `logRunSession` (`runningHooks.ts:95`, `type: 'RUN'`) does the same — each is a **separate, additive** call beside the existing skill-XP/level-up path above, feeding the account-wide coins/streak ledger, not `WorkoutPlan`/`LevelUpResult`. (The `AppHero` header that used to *display* that ledger on every section is deleted with the shells — the profile now surfaces on the Én hub, see [growth.md](growth.md)/[me.md](me.md); the awards themselves are unchanged.) Real mode never calls it (no-op) — see [growth.md](growth.md) for the account-progression domain. | `XpEventType` (`@/data/gamification/gamificationTypes`); no return value consumed here (the award emits its own toast) |

**Counting semantics for custom (`Saját edzés`) workouts (`mezo-ws2x`):** a completed custom instance is real training load, so it counts everywhere a completed instance is used as a **real-load** signal — `ExerciseRecordService` (records ← sets, above), `SetRecommendationService` (prescriptions read completed-instance history regardless of origin), Insights `listWorkouts`/weekly counts and the companion `get_training_log` tool's `scope=gym` (both via the origin-agnostic `findDoneInstancesBetween`, chat-tools seam above), gym XP/progression (`finishWorkout`'s `levelUp`, unaffected by origin), and — via the origin-agnostic `findDoneInstanceDates` — the habit `training_done_today` metric (`HabitEvaluator`), the companion `[Edzés]` context block (`ContextSnapshotAssembler`, above), and the proactive volume-trend window (`MetricWindowEvaluator`). It is **excluded** only from **plan-adherence** signals, which read the meso-only `findMesoDoneInstanceDates`: the `Mai`/`Heti` weekly **✓** done-marks (`WorkoutService.doneDatesThisWeek`), the day-type→Quest seam (`QuestEvaluator`), and the discipline-trait port (`TrainingCommitmentCalculator`) — a custom session is real work, but not evidence of following the plan.

**The canonical internal integration** is `TrainTodayPage`: it merges `gymSchedule` (from `useTrain`, itself the meso-days × gym-slots join of `deriveGymSchedule`), `sport.schedule` (volleyball), and `runSessionsForDay(activeRunningBlock, dayIdx)` (from `useRunning`, via `data/train/runningAgenda.ts`) into one `WeeklyAgendaDay[]`, then orders each day by time-of-day through the shared `daySessions` helper (`features/train/logic/agenda.ts`). Logging from here reuses the **shared sheets** `SportLogSheet`/`RunLogSheet`. Note the cross-load seams (Sport→everything, Running→gym volume) are the documented bidirectional contracts but their **live engines are Phase 3** — today they render static rows.

**Inbound seams (consuming side):** the Nap hub (`/nap`, formerly `/today` — `NapHubPage`) links here with an **`Edzés` tile** whose line is `today.workoutType` and whose tap navigates to `/train`; that is navigation only — Nap renders its own day read, not this backend. (`WorkoutTeaser`, the card that used to carry that link, went with the TodayPage view layer in the F8 cleanup.) **Fuel's Terv weekly view is reconciled to Train (Fuel P4, `mezo-kpo`):** in real mode `useFuelWeek` (`data/fuel/fuelWeekHooks.ts`) renders `useTrain().gymSchedule.weeklyTimes` (the `deriveGymSchedule` join) + `sport.schedule.volleyball.sessions`, and Fuel's `GymScheduleSheet` **writes back** through `useFuelWeekActions().saveGymSchedule` → `useTrain().saveGymSchedule` (`PUT /api/train/gym-schedule`, replace-all) — Fuel is a *secondary editor* of the Train-owned schedule per the P0a ADR (only day+time cross; `type`/`active` stay meso-owned, gym `duration` stays out of scope and Fuel renders a presentational default). **Fuel's Mai day-planner timeline (P5, `mezo-9ys`) also consumes Train's REAL schedule** — `useFuelTimeline` reads `useTrain().gymSchedule`/`sport.schedule` + `useRunning().activeRunningBlock` (via `runSessionsForDay`) as today's gym/sport/run `PlannerBlock`s. All documented from the consuming side in `today.md` §5 / `fuel.md` §5.

## 6. How to use it (consume)

Import from the single boundary `@/data/hooks`; never reach into `trainHooks.ts`/`runningHooks.ts` directly.

```tsx
import { useTrain, useRunning } from '@/data/hooks'

function MyWidget() {
  const {
    mesocycles, activeMeso, workout, gymSchedule, gymSlots, sport,
    exerciseLibrary, exerciseRecords, workoutPending, todaySession,
    // imperative writes:
    createMesocycle, activateMesocycle, closeMesocycle, saveDayExercises,
    startWorkout, logSet, saveWorkoutFeedback, finishWorkout,
    logSportSession, saveSportSchedule, saveGymSchedule,
    createCatalogExercise, updateCatalogExercise, deleteCatalogExercise, setExerciseVideo,
  } = useTrain()

  const {
    runningBlocks, activeRunningBlock, runSessions, runningPending,
    saveRunningBlock, activateRunningBlock, closeRunningBlock,
    deleteRunningBlock, logRunSession,
  } = useRunning()

  // ALWAYS ghost-guard real mode (no static fallback) and check *Pending before redirecting:
  if (workoutPending) return null
  if (!activeMeso) return <GhostState />
  // ...
}
```

- Reads return `null`/`[]` in real mode when the backend is empty — render a ghost, never assume data.
- Writes are imperative callbacks that `mutate` + invalidate the relevant query key; most accept an optional `{ onSuccess }`. In mock mode `useTrain` writes **no-op** while `useRunning` writes **emulate the server** via `queryClient.setQueryData` (so the `Futás` flows are interactive without a backend).
- **Backend consumer:** inject the relevant `*Service`; all methods take `UUID createdBy` first (from `CurrentUserId`). Use generated `api.dto` types only.

---

## 7. How to extend it

Adding a Train endpoint/field/surface is **contract-first + dual-mode + both-test-modes**. The obligations are non-negotiable.

**The wizard's `Program` step now sources its proposal from the plan generator** (`POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`, §4 `#### Plan generator`) — `mezo-d20.14` wired it up and deleted the client-side `planner.ts generateProgram`/`fitProgram` pipeline it used to fall back to: `data/train/mesoPlanHooks.ts`'s `useMesoPlanGenerate()` calls the real endpoint in real mode and `data/train/mesoPlanMock.ts`'s `mockMesoPlan` (a FE-only mirror using the same `logic/mesoPlan.ts` frames) in mock mode, so both modes exercise the same contract shape end to end.

**Add or change an endpoint/field:**
1. Edit `api/feature/train/train.yml` (path + schema) FIRST. Follow `docs/references/api_contract_conventions.md`.
2. Merge + regenerate: `cd api/generate && npm run generate:api`; then `cd frontend && pnpm generate:api`. Backend types regenerate during `./mvnw generate-sources`.
3. Backend: implement the method on `TrainController` (delegate to a `*Service`). Follow `docs/references/java_package_structure.md` (`feature/train/{controller,service,repository,entity,dto,mapper}`) and `spring_patterns.md` (constructor DI via `@RequiredArgsConstructor`, method-level `@Transactional` on writes only, derived→JPQL→native repositories, MapStruct).
4. New table → Liquibase changeset `{YYYYMMDDHHMM}_{bd-id}_{desc}.sql` per `docs/references/liquibase_conventions.md` (UUID PK `gen_random_uuid()`, explicit `pk_/fk_/uq_/ck_/idx_` names, soft-delete columns). Seed data in Java `@Profile("demodata"|"demofixtures")` — never SQL. Add the table to `ResetDatabase`'s TRUNCATE list and add/extend a `*Populator` (`docs/references/integration_test_framework.md`).
5. New configurable value → `application.yml` under the `mezo:` root (`docs/references/configuration_conventions.md`), never `@Value`.
6. Errors → `SystemRuntimeErrorException` + a `SystemMessage` code (`docs/references/error_handling.md`).
7. Frontend: add the client method to `data/train/trainApi.ts`/`data/train/runningApi.ts`, wire a query/mutation in `trainHooks.ts`/`runningHooks.ts` **with both branches** — mock (no-op for `useTrain`, or `setQueryData` for `useRunning`) and real. Keep the boundary cast idiom (`as Mesocycle` style) consistent and use `satisfies` on request bodies. Ghost-guard the new read in every view.

**Add a Train surface (there is no sub-tab to add any more):** decide first whether it is a **hub tile → full-page sibling** or a **tile page inside an existing route**.
- A *sibling page* gets a route in `app/router.tsx` under `/train/...`, a `pages/<X>Page.tsx` built on `MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`, and a `<Tile>` on `EdzesHubPage`'s `Mosaic` whose `line` comes from that page's OWN hook — **and vanishes when the hook has nothing to say** (never a fabricated count). Its data goes through `useTrain`/`useRunning` or a new hook re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`.
- A *tile page inside a route* (the session-prep idiom) is a local component under `pages/prep/`-style, swapped by page state, so the URL and the mounted session survive.
- Do **not** reintroduce a section shell or a sub-nav ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md)), and take clay sprites over emoji in any new face ([ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md)).

**Swap a mock-only feature to real** (e.g. a future cross-load engine): produce the data server-side, replace the static fixture branch in the hook with the API client call, and drop the `crossLoad: null` ghost — keeping the mock branch byte-identical to Phase 1.

---

- **Visual baselines (S8, `mezo-mifi`):** `train` (now the hub), `train-heti`, `train-gym` (the alias, so it renders Heti) and `train-session` are among the self-baselined goldens in `frontend/tests/visual/visual.spec.ts` (both themes; `pnpm test:visual`) — harness mechanics in [_platform-design-system.md](_platform-design-system.md) §8. **`pnpm build` is its own gate**, and `shared/ui/mozaik/prototypeCssStructure.test.ts` (added by the F8 cleanup, `mezo-d20.9.1`) is a CSS-aware structural scanner over `styles/prototype.css` — balanced braces, no conflict markers, terminated comments with no stray comment-close, no runaway string, each failure naming the line. It exists because a multi-way union merge swallowed a `@media (prefers-reduced-motion)` closing brace four times during the redesign, and once a comment body containing `*/` terminated its own comment and broke the build with an unrelated-looking "Unterminated string" — vitest caught none of it.
- **Backend (integration-first, `@SpringBootTest` + Postgres, AssertJ, populators — `docs/references/testing_standards.md`, `integration_test_framework.md`):** service ITs `TrainServiceIT`, `WorkoutServiceIT`, `SportServiceIT`, `ExerciseRecordServiceIT`, `ExerciseCatalogLoaderIT`, `ProvenanceRoundTripIT` (the typed-jsonb risk item), `TrainSeedDataIT`, `SetRecommendationServiceIT` (the prescription engine over real history, extended for RIR/deload in `mezo-5pfe`), `WorkoutTodayProgressionIT` (RIR-aware lever matrix + deload + `overloadSummary` over `getToday`, `mezo-5pfe`); a **pure unit test** `ProgressionDeciderTest` (`feature/train/service/`, no Spring — every lever branch incl. grind-hold, null-RIR neutrality, deload); HTTP contract ITs `TrainContractIT`, `WorkoutContractIT`, `SportContractIT`, `GymScheduleContractIT` (gym-schedule GET/PUT round-trip, replace-all, ownership isolation, 401), `RunningContractIT`, `ExerciseCatalogContractIT`, `ExerciseRecordContractIT`. Populators: `support/populator/TrainPopulator.java`, `RunningPopulator.java`. **Plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign, §4 `#### Plan generator`):** pure unit tests `MesoPlanSkeletonTest` (split-per-day-count, ≥2×/week by construction, tier week-1/ceiling wiring, phase curve), `PriorityTierTest` (`service/`, no Spring — tier normalization, week-1-start and ceiling math per tier), `MesoPlanFillerTest` (compound-first/stim-desc ordering, 1-vs-2-exercise threshold, rotation on the second weekly occurrence, empty-pool never fabricates), `MesoPlanMergerTest` (id/group/day validation drops a bad pick and falls back to the deterministic fill, set renormalization to the frame, the `MesoPlanFiller`-parity exercise-count cap, `MergeResult.appliedPicks`); `MesoPlanPropertiesBindingIT` (`config/`, `mezo.meso-plan.*` binds); `MesoStartTierSeedIT` (the tier-aware MEV+2 seed start, above); `MesoPlanGenerateContractIT` (the AI switch off via `@TestPropertySource`, deterministic-only response shape); `MesoPlanGenerateAiIT` (`companion-fake` profile — the fake's default `{"days":[]}` answer changes nothing so `llmUsed` stays false and the deterministic rationale is kept, a scripted `[fake-meso-plan:{…}]` sentinel planted in `goalText` drives an exact accepted pick and marks `llmUsed` true, `[fake-fail]` falls back to the deterministic fill).
  ```bash
  cd backend
  docker compose up -d
  ./mvnw clean test                  # ITs against fixed mezo_test DB (always use clean)
  ```
