## 9. Decisions, gotchas & deferred

**Key decisions** (see linked specs): mock parity vs clean-slate real mode (`2026-06-11-train-write-clean-slate-design.md`); running as a mesocycle-mirroring slice with typed-jsonb plan structure (`2026-06-14-train-running-slice-design.md`); on-the-fly records aggregation (no materialized table) over soft-deleted rows (`2026-06-12-exercise-records-design.md`); catalog as master content loaded in every profile (`2026-06-12-exercise-catalog-design.md`).

**Design 2.0 decisions** ([ADR 0032](../decisions/0032-five-tab-ia-dissolved-section-shells.md) for the IA, [ADR 0033](../decisions/0033-mozaik-2-tile-language.md) for the visual language — the latter supersedes ADR 0026): the nine-tab shell dissolved into one hub + full-page siblings; a tile opens a *page*, never a nested shell; clay SVG sprites replaced emoji; the entrance choreography plays once and then the page is still. **The redesign was recompose, not reinvent** — the axiom that let seven pages be re-faced in one wave without a single contract change.

**Gotchas:**
- **Three prep components are dead but not deleted (`mezo-d20.3.8` leftovers).** `components/PrepHero.tsx`, `components/PrepExerciseCard.tsx` and `components/ChallengesCarousel.tsx` have **no production import** — the prep mosaic replaced them with the `pages/prep/*` tile pages + `PrepExerciseTile`. Their test files still exist and still pass, which is exactly why the F8 sweep (which deleted `GymDayCard`/`GymStat`/`PhaseDots`, all three test-free) did not catch them. `components/LastWeekStat.tsx` is a fourth, older instance of the same thing. Deleting them is a cleanup follow-up, not a behavior change — but do not read them as documentation of the live screen.
- **A source comment on `MesoTemplatesPage` still says it "keeps the Train sub-nav around it".** There is no sub-nav; what the sentence actually pins (and what `train.nav.test.tsx` tests) is that the page stays on the plain DS page-header shell rather than the `MozaikPage` scaffold. Stale prose, correct behavior.
- **`GymPage` renders, it does not redirect.** `/train/gym` returns `<TrainWeekPage />` directly, so the URL stays `/train/gym` while the content is Heti's — deliberate, so bookmarks and the three in-app callers are not bounced through a history entry. Anything keying off `location.pathname` (the `train-gym` visual golden, for one) sees the Gym path with Heti's content.
- **Mock parity contract is the single most important rule:** mock returns byte-identical Phase-1 statics (synchronous `initialData`); real has no fallback → views MUST ghost-guard.
- **`useTrain` mocks no-op; `useRunning` mocks emulate** (`setQueryData`). Asymmetric on purpose.
- **Real-mode run-block create inserts synchronously into the cache** (`runningHooks.ts:52-63`, **mezo-11m**): on a `create` success the new block is written into `['running','blocks']` directly (no async invalidate that would race the read-after-write), so navigating straight to the builder no longer crashes on an `undefined` structure. Updates + mock mode still invalidate. The builder also guards `!draft.structure` with a `Betöltés…` placeholder (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:97-99`).
- **Run-builder edits auto-save (no Save button):** a 600 ms debounce (`RunningBlockBuilderPage.tsx:65-69`) plus a flush-on-back (`backToList`, `:71-74`); `dirty` is a `JSON.stringify` diff of the draft vs the loaded block (`:57-60`). The re-seed effect keys on `block?.id` only so a refetch never clobbers in-progress edits.
- **Template vs instance** both live in `workout_session`: template = `template_session_id IS NULL`; instance carries date/status/back-link. `listMesocycles`/`getToday` filter to templates; `logSet`/`finish` operate on instances.
- **`meso_template` (the plan) vs `mesocycle` (a run) is a DIFFERENT, higher-level split** (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)) — don't conflate the two: a template's `days` is a jsonb plan document with no `workout_session` rows at all; only `start`ing it stamps real `workout_session`/`exercise` rows for a run. **Known deferred edges, accepted for S1:** `POST .../rerun` on a run whose materialized template was later soft-deleted still returns that (now-invisible) `templateId`, and the follow-up `start` call 404s (`TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND`) — there is no re-materialize-on-404 retry yet; a full template `PUT` regenerates every exercise's server id, so a client must not assume ids are stable across saves. **Close now freezes a report (`mezo-meyc.2`, superseding the S1 bare-flip state above):** see the `MesocycleReportService` bullet in §4 for the computation + idempotency contract. **S3 (`mezo-meyc.3`) filled in the AI half:** the `mezo.feature.meso-review.enabled` switch (`MesoReviewGate`), the `MesocycleClosed` event, and the companion's listener/assembler/generator that write `context` + `ai_eval` back (§5 *Meso AI review*, [`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.6). **Known deferred edges, accepted for S3:** a `failed` narrative is never retried automatically — the only re-trigger is `POST .../report/regenerate` (which resets the status to `pending` and republishes the event), so a transient provider outage needs one manual tap; the generator is idempotent purely **by status**, so two closes racing the same run could in principle both pass the `pending` check and burn two smart-tier calls (single-user app, one close per run — not worth a DB lock); the LLM sees the context field names as-is, so the **metric legend** prepended to the payload ([`companion.md`](companion.md) §5.6) is the only thing stopping the narrative from overstating `gymRpeAvg` (sport+run RPE, no gym data) and the weight deltas (consecutive-measured-day sums) — if the assembler's metric→field mapping changes, that legend must change with it or the model will confidently overstate. A run auto-archived by starting the next one (the single-active invariant) has no report until someone visits it and hits „Riport generálása" — nothing backfills it proactively; mock mode's `mockRegenerate` can "generate" a report for a still-active run (no archived-only guard in the offline demo, real mode enforces the 409). **Three context fields are computed but never rendered:** `MesoContextJson.Week.sleepQualityAvg`/`waterAvgMl`/`mealCoverageDays` (and the totals-level `mealCoverageDays`) round-trip through `getReport` and ride the AI payload (so the narrative can reference sleep quality or fuel coverage), but `MesoReportPage.tsx`'s context block only ever reads `sleepAvgH`/`kcalAvg`/`energyAvg`/`stressAvg`/`weightDeltaKg`/`sportMinutes`/`sportSessions`/`runSessions`/`gymRpeAvg` — a FE-only gap, not a data gap, and remained open through S4 (the compare view reads only the context TOTALS, never the weekly rows, so it never needed these three fields either). **S4 (`mezo-meyc.4`) shipped the compare view and closed out the epic — see below for its own deferred edges.**
- **Compare view aligns volume by week NUMBER, not phase (`mezo-meyc.4`):** `alignVolumeWeeks` (`frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts:72-97`) faces W1 against W1, W2 against W2, … regardless of each run's own phase curve — a 4-week MEV block and a 6-week block with its deload in a different slot still line up by raw index, so "week 3 of run A" and "week 3 of run B" can land in different phases. Phase-aware alignment (MEV-to-MEV, deload-to-deload) is a real design question the spec left open, not solved here — a candidate for a future compare-view revision if two differently-phased runs turn out to be the common case. `betterSide` (now exported from `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts`, moved there from the page in the mezo-meyc.4 fix wave) and the library's max-two selection cap (`frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesocycleLibraryPage.tsx:62-65`) both carry dedicated unit tests as of that wave (`mesoCompare.test.ts`'s `betterSide` block; `MesocycleLibraryPage.test.tsx`'s third-tap-refused test) — the one-sided-null branch was in fact a real bug (a regression on the only-measured side used to win by default) fixed in the same wave.
- **S4 history cards ship a presence chip, not the spec's „key results" (scope narrowed from spec §4/§8, mezo-meyc.4 fix wave):** the design spec's `Történet` section promised cards carrying "key results — adherence %, top strength jump" per closed run; what actually shipped is `ArchivedMesoCard`'s `hasReport`-driven footer chip (`riport` / `nincs riport`) plus a `closedAt`-based eyebrow date — narrating a run's outcome on the card would mean batch-fetching every archived run's report just to render the list (the compare view deliberately fetches one report per request instead, §4 above), so it was YAGNI-cut for this wave rather than solved. A bd follow-up is filed to revisit a lightweight batched summary if the card ever needs to show adherence/strength at a glance.
- **Single-active invariant** for both mesocycles and running blocks (enforced server-side on activate AND on meso create-as-active).
- **Records resolve over soft-deleted exercises** via native query — day-edits soft-delete template rows but must not erase history.
- **Warmup sets (`kind='warmup'`) are excluded from every "real work" reader** (hypertrophy drive, `mezo-dhdr`): `ExerciseRecordService` (e1RM / best-set / session-volume), `WorkoutService.lastWeekRefs`, and the `GymSignalCalculator` progression XP all count **working sets only**, so a warmup ramp never inflates a PR, a "Múlt hét" reference, or earned XP. Correspondingly the recommendation engine reads **completed-instance history only** — the open live session is invisible to its own prescription, which is what makes the prescription **stable across a mid-workout hard-reload resume** (§4 engine subsection).
- **`ProgressionDecider` is a pure, Spring-free static method** (`decide(RefSet ref, repMin, repMax, targetRir, inc, plateStep, deloadWeek)`, `feature/train/service/ProgressionDecider.java`, `mezo-5pfe`) — no DB, no Spring wiring, unit-tested directly (`ProgressionDeciderTest`) rather than through an integration test; `SetRecommendationService` is the only caller, and the decider's precondition (`ref.weightKg() != null`) is enforced by the caller — the weightless/first-session/anchor-only branches never reach it. **A grind beats reps-in-range in the lever priority**: an athlete who hit `rep_min..rep_max` reps but logged a harder-than-target RIR (`slack = loggedRIR − targetRIR < 0`) gets **HOLD**, not the naive reps-only rule's **+rep** — RIR silently overrides the rep count in exactly this one branch. **A null logged RIR is neutral (`slack = 0`), never a grind** — an un-rated set can still earn a `+rep` or `+weight` lever exactly as if it had hit the target RIR.
- **Ownership errors:** foreign/missing → **404** (indistinguishable); `logSet` on a completed instance → **409**.
- **`phase_curve text[]` is `List<String>`** (not `String[]`) for Hibernate dirty-checking.
- Day-edit save (`MesoExercises.tsx`) fires `saveDayExercises` only when the day carries a real row `id`; mock fixtures have no id → local-only. **Exercise reorder is real** (the `SortableList` primitive, drag + ▲▼): the builder remaps the day's exercises and persists via the same `saveDayExercises` full-list PUT (array order = the contract); the planner (`MesocyclePlannerPage`'s terminal `Program` step) reorders its local draft `program` only (saved wholesale at the end).
- **Wizard v2's draft lives in one reducer, generation is explicit and once-per-visit (`mezo-d20.14`, superseding the old signature-`useEffect` regeneration guard `mezo-n46i`/`mezo-7rdg` described in prior revisions of this doc).** `wizard/wizardState.ts`'s `wizardReducer` owns everything — `step`, `daysOfWeek`, `weeks`, `priorities`, `goalText`, `name`, `proposal`, the editable `program: MesoDay[]`, a `dirty` flag, and `activeDay`. `MesocyclePlannerPage.tsx` is a pure dispatcher: no local program/priorities state of its own. The generator is called from `goToStep`, not a `useEffect` signature — it fires **only** when entering step 2 (`Program`) with no existing `proposal` (`step === 2 && !state.proposal`), so a step round-trip (back to `Fókusz` and forward again) always keeps whatever is already generated, edited or not. **Every `editProgram` dispatch sets `dirty: true`**; `dirty` is the sole regeneration guard — `StepProgram`'s `↺ Újragenerálás` checks it and opens an inline confirm strip instead of calling the generator directly when true (§2 above). Changing `priorities` (Fókusz step) or `weeks`/`name` (Program step's own inputs) never triggers a background regeneration and never marks `dirty` — only `editProgram` (day/exercise edits) does; a `weeks` change is deliberately visible-but-inert until the athlete taps `↺ Újragenerálás` themselves (a `coachsub` note says so). Added-exercise ids use `crypto.randomUUID()` (never `Date.now()`), minted once per proposal by `mesoPlanHooks.ts`'s `toProposal`/`seedDays` so a fresh generation is immediately editable.
- **Gym time is a *standalone* schedule, not a meso-day field** (Option Y, `2026-06-15-gym-schedule-times-design.md`): `GymScheduleSlot` persists across mesocycles; the meso decides *whether* you train that weekday, the slot decides *when*. `deriveGymSchedule` joins them by weekday — a slot with no meso gym day shows nothing; a meso gym day with no slot renders with `time: null` (graceful). PUT is **replace-all** (delete-then-insert the whole week).
- **`Mai` orders by time-of-day, not modality** — `daySessions` (`features/train/logic/agenda.ts`) is the single ordering helper shared by `WeeklyDayRow` and `TrainTodayPage` heroes; untimed (null/`''`) sort last (`'99:99'` sentinel), ties stable by modality. `TrainTodayPage` builds today's hero from a `daySessions` pass, with run-only-today handled via a **synthetic** agenda day (no gym/sport but a running session) so the run still gets a hero; a fully empty today shows "Ma pihenőnap".
- **The "Időpontok" entry is always visible** (since `mezo-4t43`; on Heti's `PageHead` since the Gym fold): in mock mode `saveGymSchedule` no-ops, so a local optimistic `gymOverride` keeps the edit visible in-session (not persisted across reload); real mode persists via `PUT` + `['train','gymSchedule']` invalidation. Mock mode renders gym times from the static `gymScheduleMock` slots (`data/train/train.ts`). The **mesocycle planner Step 2** is the primary editor of the same standing schedule.
- **`Mai` done-state matching is asymmetric** (`TrainTodayPage.tsx`) — there is **no schedule↔log link** for sport, so "logged" is matched by **the shown day's date AND sport, per slot** (`mezo-05v6` generalized this from a volleyball-hardcoded match; `mezo-9bbc` then generalized "today's date" to "the shown day's date" so the same match works on any past day): `loggedSportOn(iso, k) = sport.sessions.find(s => s.sport === k && s.date === huMonthDayDow(iso))` for the hero (called with `shownIso`, today's date when today is shown), `sportDoneOn(date, k)` for the weekly rows (the mapped `SportSession.date` is the HU display string, so we compare against the target date formatted the same way) — a mixed day (e.g. a TRX slot + a volleyball slot) flips each slot independently since each carries its own `k`. Running **does** carry the prescribed tuple back, so it matches on `blockId + weekNumber + sessionKey` (`runLoggedFor(key)`), ignoring date. **Gym** has no client-readable per-day completion, so the backend computes it: `GET /api/train/workouts/today` returns `weekDoneDates` — the ISO dates this Mon–Sun week whose gym instance is **`status = 'completed'`** (`WorkoutService.doneDatesThisWeek` → `WorkoutSessionRepository.findDoneInstanceDates`; **completed-only since `mezo-cd8s`**, not the old ≥1-logged-set — a started-but-unclosed session is settled by the lazy auto-close, §4). `TrainTodayPage` derives `loggedGym = weekDoneDates.includes(today)` for the hero, and per-row `gymLogged`/`isSportLogged(slot)` (matched against each weekly row's computed calendar `date`, the latter also against the slot's sport) so **past** completed days are marked, not just today. **The done-state stores nothing new — it is a pure read-derivation over already-persisted sessions/sets.** In real mode the log itself is persisted to Postgres (`POST /api/train/sport-sessions` → `SportService.create` → `sport_session` table, §4); the flip then happens after `logSportMutation`/`logRunSession` **invalidate→refetch** the session query from the backend (the DB is the source of truth — the same data the Sport `Napló` reads). The `setQueryData` cache-append is **mock-only** (the `mock ? … : …` arm in `trainHooks.ts`): it exists so the UI flips without a backend in tests/prototype, was a silent no-op before, and **never** substitutes for the DB in real mode — mock `sport.week` stats are also not recomputed on append (real mode re-derives them). A timezone edge exists: the backend stamps the log date server-side, so the flip relies on the server "today" matching the browser's local date.
- **Retroactive logging (`mezo-9bbc`, Task 9) is client-date-only.** A past-day "Pótold" log changes exactly one thing over a normal today-log: the sheet sends the selected day's ISO `date` in the request body instead of omitting it (`SportLogSheet`)/instead of today's `localDateString()` (`RunLogSheet`) — see the date-wiring paragraph above. Nothing else about the write path is date-aware: ownership (`created_by`) is still stamped server-side from the security principal exactly as on any other write, and XP/level-up resolution runs through the same `onSuccess: r => showLevelUp(r?.levelUp)` handler regardless of which day the log is dated to. There is no backend distinction between "logged live" and "logged retroactively" — the DB just stores whatever `date` the client sent.
- **The sub-nav lineage is over — three layers, all retired.** Generic `.subnav-item` underline → the coral `.np-pills`/`.np-pill` pill nav (Napiv S4, `mezo-8141`; Train first, Fuel/`--sage` and Me/`--lav` after) → a `SubNavDropdown` popover fed by `TRAIN_TABS` off `AppHero`'s `utilities` prop (compact-header redesign, `mezo-ugqb`) → **nothing**: `TrainSection`, `TrainSubNav`, `tabs.ts`/`TRAIN_TABS` and `AppHero` are all deleted (`mezo-d20.3.1` + `mezo-d20.9.1`). Navigation between Train surfaces is the hub's tiles and each page's back chip. The S4 `GYM` → `Gym` copy rename outlived its own tab: the label survives only in prose.
- **`weeklyLoad` (`logic/weeklyLoad.ts`) headlines the Futás load tile with the week's dominant running style by intensity** — `sprint` > `pyramid` (`piramis`) > `steady` (`tempó`). Each `RunPrescribedSession.kind` (`sprint|pyramid|steady` per `train.yml`) maps to its own short lowercase Hungarian label; a `steady`-only week now reads `tempó` rather than collapsing into `piramis` (fixed in S8, `mezo-mifi`; the raw `.value` string is rendered verbatim by `LoadTiles`).
- **`WeeklyDayRow`'s gym block titles itself off `gym.type`** — `GymScheduleDay` has no separate workout-name field, so the type string (e.g. `Push`) is the only label available. It used to be printed twice (title **and** meta line) to disambiguate same-time gym days; since the stacked restructure (`mezo-lruy`) the pills carry only the schedule facts (time, duration) and the type appears once.
- **The Napiv S4 accent migration is partial, not domain-wide** (and Mozaik 2.0 layered `--mz-*` on top of it rather than replacing it — the re-faced pages speak both). `TrainTodayPage`, the then-`GymPage`, `SportPage`, `RunningPage`, `WeeklyDayRow`, and `LoadTiles` were re-skinned onto `--tag-*`/`--wash-*` (`TrainSubNav` was S4's pill-nav consumer; it's since deleted, compact-header redesign `mezo-ugqb`); `MesocycleLibraryPage`/`MesocycleBuilderPage`/`MesocyclePlannerPage`/`RunningBlockBuilderPage` only picked up the `.pghead-np` page-head (body content still runs on `--coral`); `ExercisesPage` since moved onto the muscle-family tokens (`logic/muscleColors.ts`, `mezo-kaui`). **Fixed in the final-review wave (`mezo-8141`):** `TrainTodayPage`'s own hero "MA"/"Kész" status chips (volleyball + run heroes, both the corner chip and the log CTA) now consume `--tag-sport`/`--tag-run` instead of the pre-S4 `--cat-tendency`/`--info`. The final legacy-token holdouts — `SportLogSheet`/`SportScheduleSheet`/`RunLogSheet`, `ChallengeCard`, and the run builder/editor (`RunningBlockBuilderPage`/`WeekdayGrid`/`RunWeekEditor`) — were migrated off `--cat-tendency`/`--info` onto `--rose`/`--sky` in the S8 cleanup (`mezo-mifi`), leaving `features/train` free of the legacy accents (the un-re-skinned Builder/Planner body content still runs on `--coral`, not the legacy tokens). **Napiv S5 separately finished the `--brand-glow` → coral migration for the whole active-workout surface** — the warmup rows, exercise list, prescribed-list accent, note pill, challenge tint, the `Kezdjük el` CTA (now `.np-cta`, was `CtaPrimary`), and the `FeedbackModal`/`ExerciseActionSheet`/add-set-prompt/note-editor eyebrows, plus `WorkoutSummary`/`PRToast` (both coral; `WorkoutSummary` replaced the deleted `WorkoutComplete` in `mezo-cd8s`; `PRToast` itself is since deleted too, replaced by `MedalToast`/`MedalChip`, `mezo-wp6n`) — these are no longer part of the "unmigrated" set above. Candidate for an S8 cleanup pass (see the S4 plan's out-of-scope note). Residual source-comment prose naming retired tokens was reworded at the vocabulary-sweep close (`mezo-x3x0`, comments only; two of the three files it touched — `ChallengesCarousel.tsx` and `PhaseDots.tsx` — are now dead or deleted).
- **Rest duration is hardcoded, not data-driven** (`restSecondsFor`, `features/train/logic/restTimer.ts`, Napiv S5): 150s for `compound`, 90s otherwise — no rest-duration field exists anywhere in the model or DB. A configurable per-exercise rest is a Phase 2 backlog candidate (needs a new data-model field), not a Phase 3 item.
- **`⊘ Kihagyás` clears a live rest first** (`handleSkip` calls `rest.skip()` before the model transition — the guard originates in the `mezo-8141` final-review fix) — abandoning the current exercise mid-rest must not leave the rest bar counting down toward it.
- **The active-workout `90s`/Voice tool chips were removed outright, not left inert** (Napiv S5, `mezo-8141`): the old always-present-but-disabled chip row is gone from the UI entirely (superseded by the real rest countdown for timing). Voice logging itself is still Phase 3 — it just no longer shows even a disabled chip.
- **Per-set note and per-exercise note are two different data paths — don't conflate them.** The transient `.setnote` input (`SetLogRequest.note`, ≤500 chars, cleared after every logged set) rides the `POST .../{id}/sets` body (`ActiveWorkoutPage.tsx`); the durable per-exercise note (F4, above) is a separate `PUT /api/train/exercises/{exerciseId}/note` call that persists on the exercise template, survives across sessions, and pre-loads next time. Both inputs coexist on the same `.excard`.
- **`components/LastWeekStat.tsx` is orphaned dead code** (Napiv S5) — no import anywhere in `src`; the "múlt hét" comparison it used to render a column of now lives once, inside the `⚡ Progresszió` strip's `ProgressionBanner` (`mezo-d20.3.9` removed the `.wkx-subrow` that briefly carried it). Left in place, not deleted — see the dead-prep-components gotcha above.
- **`TrainMapper.toSetResponse` was silently dropping `setIndex` from every `ExerciseSetResponse`** (found + fixed while building set edit/delete, `mezo-l3on`): the entity property is `setIndex` but the generated DTO's builder method is `index(Integer)`, so MapStruct's implicit by-name matching never wired it — an explicit `@Mapping(target = "index", source = "setIndex")` (`TrainMapper.java:77`) was needed. Every real-mode `logSet`/`updateSet`/workout-detail response had been shipping with no `setIndex` until this fix; nothing client-side depended on it (the FE tracks its own positional cursor), so the bug was latent, not user-visible.
- **Set edit/delete follow-ups still open (`mezo-l3on`):** editing or deleting a logged set only works on the **active** instance — a finished workout (`/train/review/:id`) stays read-only, with no retroactive correction path. The slot count (`extra`/`removed`) is still **client-only** state, exactly like the pre-existing `＋ Szett`: a mid-workout hard reload restores the template's planned count, even though the logged sets' own edits/deletes are persistent. A server-side slot count is a candidate follow-up, not yet built.

**Deferred / Phase 3 (🟣 do NOT assume live):** `niggleWarning`; the live **cross-load → volume recompute engine** (Sport AND Running cross-load are still static; Heti's `MuscleWeekSheet`'s static `sportMuscleLoad.ts` heuristic is the FE interim, `mezo-ly27` — this is distinct from the weekly **performance** ramp, which shipped, see §4); `shoulderLoadTrend` (constant `'stabil'`); sport `jumpCount`/`intensity`/`team`/`season`/`weeklyHours` capture; gym-schedule **`duration`** only (gym **time** is now live via `GymScheduleSlot` — §4); copy-week ("Heti terv másolása" inert); the active-workout **Voice** tool (no UI, ever since Napiv S5 removed the old inert chip — §9 gotcha above); Progressive Overload **Plan 3** (the daily-challenge overload tie-in, `mezo-gj42` — Plan 1 and Plan 2 have both shipped, see §1/§2/§4). The rest-timer **duration** stays out of scope too (hardcoded 150/90s, no data field) but the countdown itself is real, not deferred.

**Deferred by the Design 2.0 wave itself (real gaps, honestly open):**
- **Four pages never got a Mozaik face** — `Mai` (`TrainTodayPage`), `Sport`, `Futás`, `Sablonok` — plus the planner, the two builders and the template editor. They work, and their `mezo-d20.3.4`/`.3.5` slices fixed what was load-bearing, but the tab reads as two visual generations at once. The editors' deep design round is **F7.2/F7.4**, which has not run: design first, dev issue after.
- **The `Történet` mosaic tile has no route.** It scrolls the already-rendered history section into view instead of navigating — the only tile in the app that does, and a placeholder until a dedicated history route exists (F2.7 scope).
- **Heti shows no weekly RPE average.** The prototype has the stat; no cross-domain aggregate (gym RIR + sport/run RPE) exists to compute it, so the cell was dropped rather than fabricated. Same call as `Gyakorlatok`' dropped `PR e héten` cell (no dated week-boundary contract).
- **The three dead prep components + `LastWeekStat`** (gotcha above) still ship in the bundle.
- **NOT deferred, for the avoidance of doubt:** the pyramid `completedRounds` scoring fix landed on both sides — `mezo-d20.3.5` made the FE capture and send the value honestly for every run kind, and the backend half (F6.3) shipped with the F6 backend slice.

---

## 10. Key files

**FE — data / hooks / api**
- `frontend/src/data/hooks.ts` — single boundary, re-exports `useTrain`/`useRunning` (`:202-203`)
- `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.ts` — Train queries + mutations, mode branching
- `frontend/src/data/train/runningHooks.ts` — Running queries + server-emulating mock mutations
- `frontend/src/data/train/train.ts` — Train mock fixtures, label maps, presets
- `frontend/src/data/train/running.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningDraft.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningAgenda.ts` — running fixtures + pure structure editors + agenda helpers
- `frontend/src/data/train/trainApi.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/runningApi.ts` — REST clients + re-exported gen types
- `frontend/src/data/train/challengeApi.ts`, `frontend/src/data/train/challengeHooks.ts` — **workout challenges (`mezo-hbwi`, proactive-owned surface consumed here)**: `useChallenges`/`useChallengeActions` over `/api/proactive/challenge` (dual-mode; re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`); backend lives in [proactive.md](proactive.md)
- `frontend/src/data/train/workoutDetailHooks.ts` — **done-day review data (`mezo-cd8s`)**: `useWorkoutDetail(id)` (`GET /api/train/workouts/{id}`, mock serves a static `workoutDetailMock` fixture) + `useWeekWorkouts()` (this Mon–Sun week's `listWorkouts` summaries → the weekly-row date→instance-id map); both re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoArcHooks.ts` — **volume-arc data (Progressive Overload Plan 2 Phase B, `mezo-hi9m`)**: `useMesocycleVolumeArc(id)` (`GET /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/volume-arc`, dual-mode; mock derives `mesoVolumeArcMock` from `data/train/train.ts`); re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`; domain types `MesoVolumeArc`/`MuscleVolumeArc`/`VolumeArcWeek` live in `data/types.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoTemplateHooks.ts` — **template/run split (`mezo-meyc.1`)**: `useMesoTemplates()` — dual-mode list (`useDualQuery`, mock `mesoTemplatesMock` from `data/train/train.ts`) + `createTemplate`/`updateTemplate`/`deleteTemplate`/`startTemplate`/`rerun` mutations; real mode invalidates both `['train','mesoTemplates']` and `['train','mesocycles']` on success, mock mutates both caches directly via `setQueryData` (a started/rerun template touches both); re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/train/mesoReportHooks.ts` — **run report + close flow (`mezo-meyc.2`)**: `useMesoReport(id)` (exported `mesoReportQueryKey`, `GET /api/train/mesocycles/{id}/report` resolving a 404 to `notFound`, `regenerate()`/`regenerating` over `POST .../report/regenerate`, a 3s poll gated on `aiEvalStatus==='pending' && aiEvalEnabled`); `useTrain().closeMesocycle` (in `trainHooks.ts`) posts the extended `{selfEval?}` close body; both re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`
- `frontend/src/data/types.ts` — Train domain types
- `frontend/src/data/train/{medalTypes,medalApi,medalHooks,medalMock,medalEvaluator}.ts` — **medal collection (`mezo-wp6n`)**: FE-side `Medal`/`MedalType`/`MedalTier` types, `medalApi.list()` (`GET /api/train/medals`), `useMedals()` (`useDualQuery`, key `['train','medals']`, re-exported from `data/hooks.ts`), a seeded mock cabinet, and `medalEvaluator.ts` — the §6-rules port for mock mode, seeded from the mock plan's `lastWeek` per exercise (`SESSION_VOLUME` deliberately not evaluated in mock mode)

**FE — progression level-up (P5, `mezo-te8k`; consumed by the 3 finish flows)**
- `frontend/src/features/progression/{LevelUpScreen,LevelUpProvider,levelUpMeta}.{tsx,ts}` — the full-bleed overlay, the single `useLevelUp()` host (mounted in `AppLayout`), and the skillKey → HU name+emoji map (muscle names reuse `MUSCLE_LABELS`)
- `frontend/src/data/progression/progressionMock.ts` — deterministic seeded `gymLevelUpMock`/`sportLevelUpMock`/`runLevelUpMock` fixtures (mock-mode finish/sport/run return these)
- `frontend/src/shared/hooks/useReducedMotion.ts` — shared reduced-motion gate; `.levelup*` styles + keyframes in `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css`

**FE — in-card rest timer (`mezo-xt65`, replaced the S5 Live-Activity island — feature-local, no shell seam)**
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/useRestTimer.ts` — page-local countdown state hook (`idle | running | paused`; `start/pause/resume/skip`, 500ms self-tick, self-clears at 0)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/RestTimerBar.tsx` — the `.restbar` CTA-morph bar (draining fill, ⏸/▶ + ⏭ buttons, inert body); swaps in for `.donebtn` in the excard
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/restTimer.ts` — pure `restSecondsFor(type)` (150s compound / 90s else, hardcoded) + `fmtMMSS` — the only place the rest duration is decided

**FE — screens / views**
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/EdzesHubPage.tsx` — **the Edzés tab's Mozaik hub at `/train`** (`mezo-d20.3.1`): the shell's `AppHeader` (own header before `mezo-atry`) → today's-session hero (TrainTodayPage's honest states, one CTA) → the six-tile mosaic with per-page live lines. **`TrainSection.tsx`, `tabs.ts`/`TRAIN_TABS` and `TrainSubNav.tsx` are deleted** with the shell.
- `frontend/src/shared/ui/mozaik/` + `frontend/src/shared/ui/clay/` — the shared Mozaik primitives (`Tile`/`Mosaic`/`MozaikPage`/`PageHead`/`PageHero`/`PageBody`/`StatStrip`/`StatCell`/`MCells`/`CollapsibleStrip`, `motion.tsx`'s `EntranceGroup`+`useCountUp`) and the clay SVG sprites (`ClayIcon`/`ClaySpot`) every re-faced Train page consumes; tokens are the `--mz-*` family in `frontend/src/styles/prototype.css`. Cross-domain — see [`_platform-design-system.md`](_platform-design-system.md).
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/{TrainTodayPage,TrainWeekPage,SportPage,RunningPage,ExercisesPage,MesocycleLibraryPage,WorkoutReviewPage}.tsx` — the full-page siblings (`TrainTodayPage` = `Mai` at `/train/mai`; `TrainWeekPage` = `Heti` at `/train/week`, which absorbed the Gym view in `mezo-d20.3.2`; `WorkoutReviewPage` = the done-day read-only review at `/train/review/:workoutId`, `mezo-cd8s`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/GymPage.tsx` — the **thin `/train/gym` alias** (`return <TrainWeekPage />`), kept for `MesoStartSheet`/`MesocyclePlannerPage`/`CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`'s `useBackNav` + PWA bookmarks (`mezo-d20.9.1`, §2). **`GymDayCard`/`GymStat`/`PhaseDots`/`GymSkeleton` are deleted.**
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/prep/{PrepGyakorlatokPage,PrepFejlodesPage,PrepHetiZonaPage,PrepKuldetesekPage,PrepBemelegitesPage,PrepNigglePage}.tsx` — the session-prep mosaic's six tile pages (`mezo-d20.3.8`), swapped by `ActiveWorkoutPage`'s `prepTile` state rather than by routing, so `/train/session` never remounts; `components/PrepExerciseTile.tsx` is `PrepGyakorlatokPage`'s row
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesoDayPage.tsx` — the per-day editor at `/train/mesocycles/:id/days/:day` (full-screen sibling; hosts `MesoExercises` scoped to one day; mesocycle pages v2 Task 3, `mezo-d20.15`; see §2)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/{MesoWeekPage,MesoMusclePage}.tsx` — the volume week + per-muscle drill-in at `/train/mesocycles/:id/week` and `.../week/:muscle` (full-screen siblings; `MesoOverviewPage`'s successor, absorbs its whole provenance surface; mesocycle pages v2 Task 4, `mezo-d20.15`; see §2). **`pages/MesoOverviewPage.tsx`, `components/{MesoOverview,MesoVolume}.tsx` and `sheets/DayDetailSheet.tsx` are deleted** (+ their tests); the old `/train/mesocycles/:id/overview` route is now `RedirectToWeek` (`app/router.tsx`) → `.../week`.
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.tsx` — the template day-plan editor at `/train/mesocycles/templates/:id` (full-screen sibling; `MesoEditor` + background full-template `updateTemplate` PUT, `mezo-meyc.1`; see §2)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesoReportPage.tsx` — the frozen end-of-mesocycle report at `/train/mesocycles/:id/report` (full-screen sibling, the Mozaik page idiom; `mezo-meyc.2`; the `Ezt akartad` quote + izmonként peak card, mesocycle pages v2 Task 5; see §2)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/MesoComparePage.tsx` — the two-run compare view at `/train/mesocycles/compare?a=&b=` (full-screen sibling; two `useMesoReport` reads composed client-side, no endpoint; `mezo-meyc.4`; `Fókusz-különbség` + `Csúcs-volumen`, mesocycle pages v2 Task 5; see §2)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoCompare.ts` — the compare view's pure helpers: `alignVolumeWeeks` (muscle union, weeks aligned W1..max) · `sharedStrengthDeltas` (catalogId-else-exact-name identity, sorted by the louder |e1RM %|) · `contextDiff` (the six run-level lifestyle totals) — `mezo-meyc.4` — plus, since mesocycle pages v2 Task 5, `peakVolumeRows(a, b)` (per-muscle `aPeak`/`aCeiling`/`bPeak`, the max planned value across each run's own weeks) and `focusDiff(run)` (the run's non-`grow` `musclePriorities` chips + `isLegacyPlan`); see §4
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoBands.ts` — the hub hero + run page's shared derivations off a live `Mesocycle` (mesocycle pages v2, `mezo-d20.15`): `RunBand`/`runBands(meso)` (per-muscle current→ceiling + `step: 'up'|'hold'|'cap'`), `phaseChip(meso)`, `WeekDot`/`weekDots(meso)`, `deciderSentence(meso)` (maps the backend `VolumeChange.reason` vocabulary to Hungarian), `nextRolloverChips(meso)`; see §2
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoWeek.ts` — the `Heti vizsgálat` + izom-részlet pages' derivations (mesocycle pages v2 Task 4): `WeekSummary`/`weekSummary(arc, tiles)`, `MuscleWeekTile`/`muscleTiles(arc, meso)`, `WeekWorkDay`/`whereItWorks(meso, group)`, `PreviousBlock`/`previousBlock(archived, group)` (reads the latest archived run's raw `volumePerMuscle` snapshot, not a fetched arc); see §2
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoDates.ts` — `todayDayToken(date?)`, the pure Monday-first weekday token (`ActiveMesoCard`'s "Ma · {day}" line), plus `addWeeks`/`getSeason` (unchanged, §4 `#### Plan generator`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/MesoCloseSheet.tsx` — the builder's close confirm sheet (summary + optional self-eval → `closeMesocycle` → navigates to the report, `mezo-meyc.2`; see §2)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/MuscleArcSwitch.tsx` — the muscle-pill + `VolumeArcChart` switch, originally extracted out of the now-deleted `MesoOverviewPage` (`mezo-meyc.2`) so the frozen report and the live overview shared one arc component. **`MesoOverviewPage` is deleted** (mesocycle pages v2 — its provenance surface moved to `MesoWeekPage`/`MesoMusclePage`, which render their own `VolumeBand`/spark chart, not `MuscleArcSwitch`); `MuscleArcSwitch`'s remaining live consumer is `MesoReportPage`'s frozen arc, and `MesoComparePage` re-styles its own local copy of the pill row (it cannot reuse the chart, which draws one run's arc)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/{MesoTemplatesPage,MesoTemplatesSkeleton}.tsx` — the dedicated **`Sablonok` page** at `/train/templates`, reached from the Mezociklus hub's tile (`mezo-tlwa`, §2): DS head + `+ Új` → planner, the `MesoTemplateCard` list, the dashed `+ Új sablon tervezése` CTA, the `Még nincs sablonod.` ghost, and the `role="status"` loading skeleton; owns the duplicate (`createTemplate` of the template's own document, `(másolat)` title → the copy's editor) and delete flows plus the `MesoStartSheet` wiring for `Indítás`
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/MesoTemplateCard.tsx` + `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/MesoStartSheet.tsx` — the `Sablonok` tab's card (four actions since `mezo-tlwa`: `Szerkesztés`/`Indítás` over `Duplikálás`/two-tap-confirm `Törlés`) and the single start-a-run sheet (`startTemplate`, active → `/train/gym`), both `mezo-meyc.1`
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/runToTemplate.ts` — pure `runToTemplate(meso) → MesoTemplateUpsertRequest` (`mezo-tlwa`): the run→template fork behind `MesoReportPage`'s `Sablon mentése ebből a futamból` CTA and `ArchivedMesoCard`'s `Sablonná` chip — `— sablon` title suffix, `''`→`null` on the optional plan fields, days via `toDayInputs`, `volumePerMuscle` collapsed onto each profile's `source.baseline` (unresolvable muscles skipped, none ⇒ `null`); see §2 `Sablonok`
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoDays.ts` — `seedDays` (deep-ish clone for editable local day state) + `toDayInputs` (domain days → the contract's day-input list, rest days included, exercise ids dropped); shared by `MesoExercises`, `MesoTemplateEditorPage`, the planner's terminal save, and (since `mezo-tlwa`) the duplicate + `runToTemplate` paths
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/MedalsPage.tsx` — the medal cabinet at `/train/medals`, reached from the hub's `Medálok` tile (`mezo-wp6n`; Mozaik re-face `mezo-d20.3.2`, §2 `Medálok`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/pages/{TrainTodaySkeleton,TrainWeekSkeleton}.tsx` — layout-matched real-mode loading skeletons (`workoutPending || runningPending`) for `TrainTodayPage`/`TrainWeekPage` respectively, so the swap to real data never reflows the page (`mezo-9bbc`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/VolumeArcChart.tsx` — the per-muscle volume-arc chart (per-week bars: actual solid muscle-color, planned dashed, deload amber, current-week glow, MRV caption), consumed via `MuscleArcSwitch` by `MesoReportPage` (frozen, `mezo-meyc.2`) — its other pre-mesocycle-pages-v2 consumer, `MesoOverviewPage`, is deleted
- `frontend/src/features/train/{ActiveWorkoutPage,MesocyclePlannerPage,MesocycleBuilderPage,RunningBlockBuilderPage}.tsx` — full-screen routes
- `frontend/src/features/train/wizard/` — **wizard v2's own module (`mezo-d20.14`, retires `planner.ts`/`programFit.ts` and the old 5-step step components)**: `wizardState.ts` (the reducer — `WizardState`/`WizardAction`, `initialWizardState`, `generateInput` (the `MesoPlanGenerateRequest` the generator is asked), `toUpsert` (the `MesoTemplateUpsertRequest` the save writes, deriving `phaseCurve` from the CURRENT `weeks`), `inputChanged` (the pure stale-proposal predicate over `proposalInput`)), `StepWhen.tsx` (+ its own `canLeaveStepWhen` gate), `StepFocus.tsx`, `StepProgram.tsx` (hero/mosaic/bands/fit-strip/save footer, the generate-wait state, BOTH failure states — full-body retry with no proposal, inline strip over a standing one — the dirty-regenerate confirm strip and the stale-input note), `DayTile.tsx` + `dayTiles.ts` (`dayTileData`/`dayTone` — the day-mosaic tile's shared derivation, consumed by both the mosaic and `ProgramDayView` so the two never drift), `ProgramDayView.tsx` (the page-state, non-route single-day editor opened from a tile — `days={[day]}` to edit, `weekDays={program}` so the week-level cards judge the real week)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoPlan.ts` — the FE mirror of the backend `MesoPlanSkeleton` (§4 `#### Plan generator`): `dayFrames`/`weekTotals`/`splitLine`/`recommendedDays` (split-from-day-count, `SPLIT_TYPES`/`SPLIT_LABELS`), `phaseCurve(weeks)` (ramp/MEV/MAV/MRV/Deload derivation — mirror both sides), `weekOneSets`/`ceilingSets` (tier → landmark math, shared by `weeklyBands.ts`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/weeklyBands.ts` — `weeklyBands(days, priorities, landmarks?)` → `BandRow[]` (planned/start/ceiling/tier/step, no percent text), §4 `#### Weekly bands`; `frontend/src/features/train/logic/mesoDates.ts` (`addWeeks`/`getSeason` HU month helpers moved verbatim off the retired `planner.ts`, plus `todayDayToken` — see the dedicated entry above); `frontend/src/data/train/mesoPlanHooks.ts` — `useMesoPlanGenerate()` (dual-mode mutation over `POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate`) + `toProposal` (mints client ids via `seedDays` so a fresh generation is immediately editable); `frontend/src/data/train/mesoPlanMock.ts` — `mockMesoPlan`, the mock-mode twin of the backend generator (same `dayFrames`/`phaseCurve`, exercises picked from the mock library, compound-first/stim-desc/rotating — always returns `llmUsed: false`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/muscleFilters.ts` — exercise filters
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/agenda.ts` — pure `daySessions(day)` time-ordering helper (shared by `WeeklyDayRow` + `TrainTodayPage`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/weekAgenda.ts` — pure `buildWeekAgenda({gymTimes,sportSlots,runningBlock,weekWorkouts,today?})` Mon–Sun agenda builder, lifted out of `TrainTodayPage` unchanged and shared with `TrainWeekPage` (`mezo-9bbc`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/dayStripItems.ts` — pure `dayStripItems(agenda, isDone)` → per-weekday chip data (dots/doneCount/sessionCount) for `DayStrip`; `toneOf` maps gym **and completed custom (saját)** items to the `gym` tone (`mezo-9bbc`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/sessionState.ts` — pure `sessionState({dayIso,todayIso,timeOfDay,now?})` → `now|today|missed|planned`, `SESSION_STATE_LABEL` → `MOST|MA|ELMARADT|TERVEZETT` (a `TodaySessionCard`'s `stateLabel`; since Task 9 the SAME read also gates the sport/running card's CTA — `planned`⇒none, `missed`⇒`Pótold`, else the today copy, `mezo-9bbc`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/weeklyLoad.ts` — pure `weeklyLoad(agenda)` weekly-load-tile summarizer for `Heti`'s `LoadTiles` (Napiv S4, `mezo-8141`; moved off `Mai` onto `Heti` in `mezo-9bbc`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/workoutState.ts` — active-session model; `prescribedAt(session, id, idx)` resolves a set's prescribed target (warmup then working), fed by the recipe on `TodayExercise` (`mezo-dhdr`); the cursor is **exerciseId-keyed** (`nextSetIdx`/`completeSet(s,id,set)`/`nextUnfinishedAfter`, no scalar `setIdx` — `mezo-cd8s` free navigation). **Set edit/delete (`mezo-l3on`)** added a `removed` counter (the `extra` mirror) + `canRemoveSet`/`removeSet` (drops one slot, floored at one, splicing `prescribed` in lock-step with the removed index so row `i` never re-pairs with the wrong target) and `updateLoggedSet`/`attachSetId` — the latter addresses a logged entry by its client-assigned `LoggedSet.localId`, never by array index (an index shifts under a concurrent edit/delete), §9.
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/workoutCardMeta.ts` — **active-workout-context redesign (`mezo-8xmf`)**: pure, hook-free view derivations for the `.excard`/v4 set-list table, §2 Active workout — `warmupPctLabel(ex, warmupIdx)` (the last-logged-warmup `B{n} = {pct}% · {kg}` note), `setStatus(ex, {reps,kind})` (the v4 status cell's `'ok'|'below'|'above'`), `exerciseTonnage(sets)`/`topSetDeltaPct(sets, lastWeekWeightKg)`/`avgWorkingRir(sets)` (the v4 table's `Volumen`/`vs múlt hét`/`Átl. RIR` footer cells — the page zips its `LoggedSet[]` `{weight,reps,rir}` with each slot's own `prescribedAt(...).kind` before calling these, since the shared model carries no per-set `kind`), and `sessionProgressSegments(exercises, currentIdx, doneMap)` (the `.wkx-progressbar` header strip); tested in `workoutCardMeta.test.ts`.
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/muscleWeek.ts` — per-muscle weekly aggregation of the meso template (sets/Σrep-range/exercises/frequency) + `muscleRegionGroups` for the Heti zone panel (the former Gym meta-card) grid (`mezo-ly27`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/setBudget.ts` — **the pure budget-group math underneath weekly bands (`mezo-7rdg`, §4 `#### Weekly bands`)**: `setStyle`/`sessionCapWarnings` (`SESSION_MUSCLE_CAP` 8, `mezo-d20.14`, plyo excluded `mezo-0znc`) + `daySessionBreakdown`/`leastLoadedDayFor` (the active-day breakdown + suggest-a-day helper, `mezo-smhn`), still consumed directly by `WeeklyBandsCard`'s callers and `DayBreakdownCard`; `muscleBudgets`/`GROUP_MEV`/`budgetOf` (the original fatigue-scale formula) are **retired from every planning-time card** — `weeklyBands.ts` doesn't call them — and survive only because `weekZone.ts` (below) still calls them directly, a known gap tracked as `mezo-d20.16`. Pure over `MesoDay[]` (its own traversal — does not extend `muscleWeek.ts`). `frontend/src/features/train/logic/weekZone.ts` — **live zone unification (`mezo-oyhy.7`, §4 `#### Weekly bands`), still on the `budgetOf` fatigue scale (follow-up `mezo-d20.16`)**: `weekZoneRows`/`selectPrepRows`/`selectGymRows`/`prepSegments`/`gymSegments`, pure over `MesoDay[]` + `WorkoutDetailResponse[]`, consumed by `WeekZoneCard` (prep) and `ZoneMiniGrid` (Gym); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/warmupSuggest.ts` — `suggestedWarmupSets(day, exId)`, the adaptive warmup-count heuristic (`mezo-dnln`, §4 recommendation-engine subsection); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/offDay.ts` — shared `isOffDay` off-day predicate (ported verbatim from the deleted `MesoDayTabsEditor`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/exerciseDefaults.ts` — `libraryToGymExercise`/`addExerciseWithDefaults` (`mezo-dq60`; since `mezo-d20.14` a **single hypertrophy scheme**, the `goalPreset`-keyed `SCHEMES` table and `planner.ts` are retired) keyed by the picked exercise's `type` alone: plyo always gets `PLYO_SCHEME` + is exempt via `type !== 'plyo'`; `addExerciseWithDefaults` re-runs `suggestedWarmupSets` after insertion to refine the type-seeded `warmupSets`; `frontend/src/features/train/logic/musclePriorities.ts` — `TIER_GROUPS` (the 9 landmark groups), `TIER_LABELS`, `EMPHASIZE_CAP` (2), `tierOf`/`setTier`/`tierTargetOf` (`mezo-3m5m`, §4 `#### Volume progression` + `#### Weekly bands`); `frontend/src/features/train/logic/peakWeekFit.ts` — `peakWeekFit`, the peak-week session-time fit signal, a FE mirror of `WorkoutService.effectiveWorkingSets` (`mezo-3m5m`, spec GD6, §4 `#### Weekly bands`); `frontend/src/features/train/components/PeakFitCard.tsx` — renders `peakWeekFit`'s out-of-band days ("Csúcshét · időbecslés", `mezo-3m5m`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/sportMuscleLoad.ts` — static sport/run-kind → muscle load-level heuristic over the live schedule + run week (`mezo-ly27`; Phase-3 cross-load engine's interim)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/growthForecast.ts` — planned week → estimated athletic/muscle XP + level-up flags; hand-mirrors the backend progression economy (`mezo-ly27`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/MuscleWeekSheet.tsx` — the Heti zone panel (the former Gym meta-card)'s weekly muscle-load detail sheet (three sections over the three logic modules; lazy `useRunning`/`useProgressionProfile`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/challengeOutcome.ts` — pure FE preview of the backend `ChallengeOutcomeEvaluator` (PR/Depth/Volume → hit/miss/inconclusive over session-local logged sets); pre-renders the challenge outcomes on the `closing` summary *before* the finish POST — the server truth lands post-finish (`mezo-cd8s`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/summaryStats.ts` — **`WorkoutSummary` redesign derivations (`mezo-w943`)**: `deriveSummaryStats(exercises, medals)` → done/planned sets+exercises, `volumeT`, `avgRir` (null with 0 logged sets), region pills (`muscleRegion()` aggregation, off-flagged), RECORD/TARGET medal split (`targetGroups` per-exercise counts) and the per-exercise set-chip view (`topSetIndex`/`recordChipIndex` — a RECORD medal's `setIndex` wins, else a weight+reps value match); table-tested, no hooks
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/prepBriefing.ts` — pure view-data adapters for the mission-briefing prep screen (`mezo-bxpg`): `prepStats`/`prepForecast`/`pseudoDayFromPlan` (single-day `growthForecast` wrapper + custom/no-meso adapter), `oneRmByIdentity`/`identityKeyOf` (records-idiom e1RM lookup), `startWeightOf` (first working `prescribedSets` target) — see §2 Active workout
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/morningWindow.ts` — pure wake-anchored morning-training window (`mezo-67rb`): `morningWindow(wake)` = [wake+60′, wake+6h] (Train-only circadian window; **since `mezo-u6jx`** no backend mirror — the habit engine's tick is date-presence), `offendingSlots`/`rescheduledSlots` (`time > end`), `snoozeHash`/`isSnoozed`/`snooze` (content-keyed `mezo-morning-training-snooze`) — §2 Mai
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/MorningTrainingCard.tsx` — the presentational sage `.mtr` nudge card (offending slots → apply/snooze), mounted on `TrainTodayPage` (`mezo-67rb`, §2 Mai)
- `frontend/src/features/train/logic/medalLabels.ts` — the shared Hungarian medal vocabulary (`MEDAL_TYPE_LABEL`, `MEDAL_UNIT_LABEL`, `medalValueLabel`, `formatMedalNumber`), consumed by `MedalToast`/`MedalChip`/`MedalsPage`/`WorkoutSummary` (`mezo-wp6n`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/{MedalToast,MedalChip}.tsx` — **medal collection UI (`mezo-wp6n`), replaces the deleted `PRToast.tsx`**: `MedalToast` fires only for a `RECORD`-tier medal (~4.5s, amber) with the achieving set + the prior value/date it beat; `MedalChip` is the gold row mark for `RECORD` and renders nothing for `TARGET` tier (a `TARGET_HIT` drives no visual of its own since the v4 table redesign — its rep-range status cell already conveys hit/miss, `mezo-8xmf`, §2 Active workout)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/` — sheets/cards (`SetStepper` (Napiv S5 giant stepper — `.stepper`/`.steprow`, `mezo-8141`; ± + tap-to-edit value via `useEditableNumber`, `mezo-o7ds`), `LoadTiles` (Napiv S4 weekly-load tiles over `logic/weeklyLoad.ts`), `PrepHero`/`PrepExerciseCard`/`ChallengesCarousel` (**unreferenced since the prep mosaic, `mezo-d20.3.8` — tests only, §9**; their live successors are `pages/prep/*` + `PrepExerciseTile`), `ProgressionBanner` (the in-workout RIR-aware progression signal — label + delta chip + `Múlt hét → Ma a cél` comparison + rationale, 3 lever tones — replaces `.aistrip` on the `.excard` when a `progression` exists, `mezo-5pfe`, §2 Active workout), `GymScheduleSheet` (standalone weekly gym-time editor → PUT `/gym-schedule`), `ExercisePickerSheet`, `ExerciseRecordSheet`, `VideoDemo` (tap-to-reveal demo player, used in workout/browser/picker — `mezo-52zg`; `videoEmbed()` resolves the stored url to an embed target: YouTube → youtube-nocookie 16:9, Instagram reel/post/tv permalink → `instagram.com/{kind}/{code}/embed` 9:16 portrait, `mezo-4g4i`), `ChallengeCard` (live challenge card, now stacked by `PrepKuldetesekPage` — „tanulom"/tools-hidden/outcome-chip states, `mezo-hbwi`), `FeedbackModal`, `WorkoutSummary` (the explicit-finish + done-day review screen — `closing`/`closed` modes, `mezo-cd8s`; replaced the deleted `WorkoutComplete`; **colorful pill/chip redesign `mezo-w943`** — hero+halo, region pills, 4-cell strip, RECORD/TARGET medal split, per-exercise set-chip map, closing-only note; the `showSetLines` prop is deleted, see §2 Active workout for the full anatomy), `SportLogSheet`, `SportScheduleSheet`, `CrossLoadRow`, `RunLogSheet`, `RunWeekEditor` (two-zone session cards: Menetrend = `WeekdayGrid` + time, Terhelés = load — still on `CompactStepper`/`NumberStep`), `WeekdayGrid` (single-select 7-day picker), `RunSessionCard`, `RunCrossLoadCard`, `WeeklyDayRow` (`Heti`'s `.dayrow` day cards; renders `daySessions` time-ordered items — stacked day-header + per-session blocks with `.metapill` facts since `mezo-lruy`), `TodaySessionCard` (`.todaycard` sport/run/non-today-gym session card — tag + time + title + fact pills + full-width log CTA or `DoneBar`, tone-tinted per modality, `mezo-lruy`; `ctaLabel` omitted ⇒ read-only, `mezo-9bbc`; **since `mezo-jyua` a thin wrapper over `shared/ui/ItemCard`** — the card language is shared with Today, so a change to it is cross-domain), `DoneBar` (the shared "session is done" bar — check + summary + optional detail line, worn by `TodaySessionCard`'s logged state and the gym hero's Kész state, `mezo-9bbc`; **also imported by `shared/ui/ItemCard`** since `mezo-jyua`, so a `shared/ui` file reaches into this feature — an inherited compromise with a follow-up filed to move `DoneBar` into `shared/ui`), `DayStrip` (Mai's `role="tablist"` week navigator — one `.daychip` `role="tab"` per weekday over `dayStripItems`, `mezo-9bbc`; centres the selected chip on mount via `scrollIntoView({ inline: 'center' })` — skipped under reduced motion, mount-only — since 7 chips ≈ 536 px do not fit a 440 px viewport, and its `aria-label` spells out everything the chip shows: `„Hétfő · 18. · 2/2 kész"` / `„… · nincs naplózva"` / `„… · pihenő"`, because the label replaces the chip's content as its accessible name), `MesoEditor`/`MesoEditorHero`/`WeeklyBandsCard`/`DayBreakdownCard`/`ExerciseAccordionRow` (the unified day-tabbed meso day editor, `mezo-7rdg`, §2/§4 — builder `Gyakorlatok` view via `MesoExercises` + the wizard's per-day `ProgramDayView`: `MesoEditor` composes day tabs with session-cap warning dots + `MesoEditorHero` (gradient hero, day/week stats — week-level `warningCount` is session-cap breaches only, the % overage alarm retired with `SetBudgetCard`) + `DayBreakdownCard` (the active-day "Ma · izmonként" per-group bars, `mezo-smhn`, §4 `#### Weekly bands`) + `frontend/src/features/train/components/WeeklyBandsCard.tsx` (`current → ceiling` per tier, no percent text — **replaced `SetBudgetCard` everywhere in `mezo-d20.14`**: this component, the wizard's `Program`-step week band, and `MuscleWeekSheet`'s Gym mirror) + a single-expand `ExerciseAccordionRow` list (🔥/🌿 toggle, 2×2 stepper grid, `Finomhangolás` disclosure, a `↺ javaslat: n` adaptive-warmup chip fed by `logic/warmupSuggest.ts`, `mezo-dnln`) — **supersedes the deleted `MesoDayTabsEditor`** (builder day-tabbed recipe editor) **and `PlannerDaySection`** (the old 5-step planner's per-day exercise-list component, also deleted in `mezo-d20.14`), which themselves had superseded `ExerciseEditRow`/`DayExerciseSection`), `ExerciseRecipeRow` (the older always-open recipe row — **retained only for `CustomWorkoutBuilderPage`**, no longer used by the meso builder/wizard), `ZoneTrack` (the shared zone-bar primitive, `mezo-oyhy.7`, §4 `#### Weekly bands` — track + optimal-zone underlay + `solid`/`today`/`ghost`/`overflow` segments; **no longer used by any planning-time card since `SetBudgetCard`'s deletion** — its only remaining consumers are `WeekZoneCard`/`ZoneMiniGrid` below), `WeekZoneCard` (live zone context, `mezo-oyhy.7`; now rendered by `PrepHetiZonaPage`), `ZoneMiniGrid` (the live group grid, superseding the mezo-ly27 pill grid, `mezo-oyhy.7`; moved from the Gym meta card onto `Heti` with the fold), `VolumeBand`/`DerivationSteps` (the `MesoMusclePage` band + provenance-derivation cards, mesocycle pages v2 Task 4 — successors to the deleted `MesoOverview`/`MesoVolume`; **`VolumeBar.tsx` and its `FinalStat` cell are now DELETED too**, along with `PhaseCurveBars`/`MetaStat`, whose last importer went with `MesoOverviewPage` — `DerivationSteps` owns the whole 01…04 provenance body), `MesoExercises` (seeds local day-state from `meso.days`, hosts `MesoEditor`, fires the background `saveDayExercises` PUT; day-scoped via its `day?` prop since mesocycle pages v2 Task 3 — its old in-cycle `Fókusz`/`MusclePriorityPicker` `<details>` and `PUT .../muscle-priorities` call are gone), `LastWeekStat` (orphaned dead code since Napiv S5, no imports — §9), …). **`GymDaySheet`/`GymExRow` are deleted (`mezo-bxpg` direct-start flow)** — superseded by `GymPage`/`TrainTodayPage`'s own tap-handler resolution (§2).
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/CatalogExerciseSheet.tsx` — user-exercise authoring form (create/edit/delete; name/muscle/type/stim/fatigue/video), opened from the `Gyakorlatok` page `+ Új gyakorlat` chip (`mezo-52zg`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/VideoUrlSheet.tsx` — one-field demo-video URL editor (attach/replace/remove) for ANY catalog row incl. built-in ones, opened from the per-row `▶ Videó` action → `setExerciseVideo` (ownership-free `/video` endpoint, `mezo-bnsk`). The contract `pattern` on `videoUrl` accepts a YouTube watch/short url OR an Instagram `reel`/`reels`/`p`/`tv` permalink (profile-scoped form and `?igsh=` tracking query included); anything else is a 400 surfaced as the generic „Mentés sikertelen" toast (`mezo-4g4i`)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/{ExerciseActionSheet,ExerciseOverviewSheet}.tsx` — the active-workout `⋯` action menu (5 rows incl. **Edzés befejezése…**) + the jump-to overview list (every exercise + live state, `mezo-cd8s` free navigation)
- `frontend/src/features/train/sheets/SetEditSheet.tsx` — edit-or-delete-one-set sheet (`mezo-l3on`), opened by tapping any row of the active-workout v4 set-list table (§2 above, `mezo-8xmf`): reuses `SetStepper` + the RIR/Side pill rows; `mode: 'logged' | 'pending'` (a not-yet-logged slot shows its target read-only, delete-only); the delete action is floored at one slot per exercise (`canDelete`, disabled + explanatory copy at the floor)

**API contract**
- `api/feature/train/train.yml` — the FE↔BE source of truth for Train

**Backend** (`backend/src/main/java/io/mrkuhne/mezo/feature/train/`)
- `controller/TrainController.java` — implements generated `TrainApi`; the meso-template CRUD/start/rerun endpoints delegate straight to `MesoTemplateService` (`mezo-meyc.1`)
- `service/{TrainService,WorkoutService,SportService,GymScheduleService,ExerciseCatalogService,ExerciseRecordService,RunningService}.java` — `ExerciseCatalogService` now has the catalog **write** methods `create`/`update`/`delete`/`setVideo`/`setImages` (owner-scoped except the two media writers; master row → 409 `CATALOG_MASTER_READONLY`; `uniqueSlug` slug-gen) alongside `list` (`mezo-52zg`, `mezo-8xdl.1`). **`TrainService.stampRun`** (package-visible; `mezo-meyc.1`) is the extracted "materialize a run from a plan document" method every start funnels through — its `StampSource` record decouples the stitching from where the plan came from (today: `MesoTemplateEntity` via `MesoTemplateService.start`); `TrainService`'s own direct-create method is deleted along with the endpoint. `TrainService.closeMesocycle` (`mezo-meyc.2`, `:256`) now freezes the report in the close transaction, `hasReport` batching (`:110`).
- `service/MesocycleReportService.java` (`mezo-meyc.2`) — the deterministic report computer + reader (`computeAndStore`/`getReport`/`regenerate`); `mapper/MesoReportMapper.java` maps between the persisted jsonb records and the contract DTOs (report ⇄ `MesoReportJson`, arc ⇄ `MesocycleVolumeArcResponse`). `VolumeArcService.arc(createdBy, mesoId, effectiveCurrentWeek)` (package-visible 3-arg overload, `:65`) is the report-freeze entry point; the public 2-arg overload is unchanged. **`MesoReviewGate.java` + `MesocycleClosed.java`** (both `feature/train/`, `mezo-meyc.3`) are the AI half's two seams — the switch marker bean `getReport` reads for `aiEvalEnabled`, and the record the close/regenerate transactions publish for the companion's AFTER_COMMIT generator.
- `service/MesoTemplateService.java` — the template/run split's write half (`mezo-meyc.1`, ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md)): template CRUD (full-replace `update`), `start` (hands the plan to `TrainService.stampRun`), `rerun` (materializes+links a template for a legacy run on first call, plain lookup after); all finders owner-scoped, missing/foreign → `TRAIN_MESO_TEMPLATE_NOT_FOUND` 404
- `service/CatalogMediaResolver.java` — the ONE batched `catalog_id → CatalogMedia(videoUrl, imageStartUrl, imageEndUrl)` resolve shared by `WorkoutService.getToday` and `TrainService.toDay` (renamed from `CatalogVideoResolver` in `mezo-8xdl.1`)
- `service/{MedalEvaluator,MedalService}.java` — the medal-collection engine (`mezo-wp6n`, §2 `Medálok` + §4): the pure rule surface + the replay/read service, derived-not-materialized (ADR 0016)
- `service/{SetRecommendationService,Prescription}.java` — hypertrophy-drive double-progression engine + its `(sets, rationale, progression)` result record (`mezo-dhdr` + `mezo-5pfe`)
- `service/ProgressionDecider.java` — pure, Spring-free RIR-aware + deload-aware double-progression decider (`mezo-5pfe`, §4 engine subsection); the only caller is `SetRecommendationService`
- `service/{VolumeProgressionService,VolumeDecider,MuscleGroup,MesoWeeks,VolumeArcService}.java` — Progressive Overload Plan 2 (`mezo-hi9m`, §4 `#### Volume progression`): the weekly per-muscle set-rollover engine (`VolumeProgressionService`), its pure decider (`VolumeDecider`), the 21-zone→coarse-group collapse (`MuscleGroup`), shared `startDate`-anchored week math (`MesoWeeks`, package-visible), and the read-only volume-arc endpoint's service (`VolumeArcService`). **`service/PriorityTier.java`** (`mezo-3m5m`, §4 `#### Volume progression`) — the Emphasize/Grow/Maintain enum, its sparse-map resolver (`of`) and `ceiling`, consumed by both `VolumeProgressionService` (the rollover) and `VolumeArcService` (the planned scaffold); `db/changelog/1.0.0/script/202608251200_mezo-3m5m_muscle_priorities.sql` — the `mesocycle`/`meso_template` `muscle_priorities jsonb` columns, no backfill.
- `config/HypertrophyProperties.java` — `mezo.hypertrophy.*` tuning (plate step, per-type increments, the count-keyed `warmupLadders`, `mezo-dnln`); `HypertrophyDriveGate.java` — `@ConditionalOnProperty` switch bean (`mezo.feature.hypertrophy-drive.enabled`)
- `config/VolumeProperties.java` — `mezo.volume.*` tuning (`step`/`deloadFraction`/`grindRirGap`); `VolumeProgressionGate.java` — `@ConditionalOnProperty` switch bean (`mezo.feature.volume-progression.enabled`, default true), mirrors `HypertrophyDriveGate`
- `service/{MesoPlanSkeleton,MesoPlanFiller,MesoPlanMerger,MesoPlanLlm,MesoPlanGeneratorService}.java` — the plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign, §4 `#### Plan generator`): the pure split/set-frame builder (`MesoPlanSkeleton`), the pure deterministic exercise fill (`MesoPlanFiller`), the pure LLM-pick validator/merger (`MesoPlanMerger`, returns `MergeResult{days, appliedPicks}`), the train-owned port interface (`MesoPlanLlm`, Gemini half in `feature/companion/llm/MesoPlanLlmAdapter.java`), and the `@Service` orchestrator behind `POST /api/train/meso-plans/generate` (`MesoPlanGeneratorService` — deliberately NOT `@Transactional`, so the slow-tier LLM round-trip never holds a DB connection open). `config/MesoPlanProperties.java` — `mezo.meso-plan.*` tuning (`@Validated` record: `maxExercisesPerGroupPerDay`/compound+isolation rep ranges/`targetRir`/compound+isolation warmup counts).
- `entity/*` — `MesocycleEntity` (now `templateId`/`closedAt`), `WorkoutSessionEntity`, `ExerciseEntity` (recipe fields `warmupSets`/`workingSets`/`repMin`/`repMax`/`targetRir`/`anchorWeightKg`), `ExerciseSetEntity` (`kind`), `ExerciseFeedbackEntity`, `MuscleGroupVolumeLogEntity`, `ProvenanceEnvelope`, `VolumeRecomputeJson`, `SportSessionEntity`, `SportScheduleSlotEntity`, `GymScheduleSlotEntity`, `ExerciseCatalogEntity`, `RunningBlockEntity`, `RunningBlockStructure`, `RunSessionLogEntity`. **`MesoTemplateEntity`** (`mezo-meyc.1`) — the plan document, `days`/`volumePerMuscle` typed jsonb; `entity/json/{MesoDayJson,GymExerciseJson,VolumeBaselineJson}.java` are its record shapes. **`MesocycleReportEntity`** (`mezo-meyc.2`) — the frozen report row, `report`/`context` typed jsonb; `entity/json/{MesoReportJson,MesoContextJson}.java` are its record shapes (the latter is filled in by the companion's `MesoContextAssembler` since `mezo-meyc.3`, but stays **train-owned** — the companion computes it, train persists and freezes it).
- `repository/*` — notably `ExerciseRepository.findIdentityRowsIncludingDeleted`; `repository/MesoTemplateRepository.java` (`findByCreatedByAndDeletedFalseOrderByCreatedAtAsc`, `findByIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`); `repository/MesocycleRepository.java:25` — `countByTemplateIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`, the library's `runCount`. **`mezo-meyc.2`:** `repository/MesocycleReportRepository.java` (`findByMesocycleIdAndCreatedByAndDeletedFalse`, the `hasReport` batch existence query); `WorkoutSessionRepository.findCompletedMesoInstancesInWindow`; `ExerciseSetRepository.findWorkingSetsInSessions`
- `mapper/{TrainMapper,RunningMapper}.java`
- `{TrainSeedData,RunningSeedData,ExerciseCatalogLoader}.java` — seed + master content loader
- `backend/src/main/resources/content/exercise-catalog.json` — catalog master data, **161 rows** (incl. the 2 plyo entries `db-jump-squat` / `single-leg-plate-hop` and the 22-row `kb-*` kettlebell block, `mezo-18g3`); 124 rows carry `imageStartUrl`/`imageEndUrl` (`mezo-8xdl.2`)
- `scripts/data/exercise-image-map.json` + `scripts/import-exercise-images.mjs` — the hand-curated slug → free-exercise-db map and its by-hand importer
- `frontend/public/exercises/` — 248 vendored demo stills (public domain; precache-excluded, `CacheFirst` at runtime)
- `frontend/src/features/train/components/ExerciseImage.tsx` — the two-frame demo still (`hero` crossfade / `thumb`), `.exdemo*` classes in `styles/prototype.css`
- Migrations: `backend/src/main/resources/db/changelog/1.0.0/script/{202606111400_mezo-n5q_create_train, 202606120900_mezo-tod_t2_workout_execution, 202606121000_mezo-0ae_t3_sport_schedule, 202606121400_mezo-7ot_exercise_catalog, 202606141200_mezo-b4n_create_running_block, 202606141210_mezo-b4n_create_run_session_log, 202606151500_mezo-auk_create_gym_schedule_slot, 202607072000_mezo-dhdr_prescribed_sets, 202607082000_mezo-52zg_catalog_write, 202607141200_mezo-05v6_sport_schedule_slot_sport, 202607301900_mezo-wp6n_exercise_set_target, 202608161200_mezo-meyc.1_create_meso_template, 202608161210_mezo-meyc.1_mesocycle_template_link, 202608161600_mezo-meyc.2_create_mesocycle_report}.sql`

- FE: `frontend/src/features/train/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}` (incl. `logic/restTimer.test.ts`, `logic/useRestTimer.test.ts` + `components/RestTimerBar.test.tsx` (`mezo-xt65`), `components/SetStepper.test.tsx`, Napiv S5; `components/ProgressionBanner.test.tsx`, `components/PrepHero.test.tsx`, `components/PrepExerciseCard.test.tsx` (`mezo-5pfe`)), `frontend/src/data/train/trainHooks.test.tsx`, `train.emptyStates.test.tsx`, `train.nav.test.tsx`, plus the `hideChrome` case in `frontend/src/app/navigation.test.tsx` (Napiv S5); `components/VolumeArcChart.test.tsx`; **mesocycle pages v2 (`mezo-d20.15`, deletes `pages/MesoOverviewPage.test.tsx` + the `DayDetailSheet`/`MesoOverview` tests):** `logic/mesoBands.test.ts`, `logic/mesoWeek.test.ts`, `pages/MesoDayPage.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoWeekPage.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoMusclePage.test.tsx`; `data/train/mesoTemplateHooks.test.ts`, `pages/MesoTemplateEditorPage.test.tsx`, `sheets/MesoStartSheet.test.tsx`, `logic/mesoDays.test.ts` (template/run split, `mezo-meyc.1`); `data/train/mesoReportHooks.test.tsx`, `pages/MesoReportPage.test.tsx`, `sheets/MesoCloseSheet.test.tsx` (run report + close flow, `mezo-meyc.2`) + the `test/msw/handlers.ts` fixtures for the report/close/regenerate endpoints; `logic/mesoCompare.test.ts` (exact-value helper tests incl. the null-volume, week-padding, no-overlap and `betterSide` tie/one-sided-null/regression edges), `pages/MesoComparePage.test.tsx` (the two mock fixture reports side by side + the missing-param / self-comparison / one-side-`notFound` states), `components/ArchivedMesoCard.test.tsx` (`riport` vs `nincs riport`, `closedAt`-vs-`endDate` eyebrow, selection-mode toggle) and `pages/MesocycleLibraryPage.test.tsx`'s third-tap-refused + toggle-pressed-state cases (compare view, `mezo-meyc.4`); **`mezo-tlwa`:** `logic/runToTemplate.test.ts` (field-for-field mapping, rest-day passthrough, `''`→`null`, empty days, baseline collapse + the skip/null fallbacks), `pages/MesoTemplatesPage.test.tsx` (the four card actions, duplicate → `(másolat)` + editor + `0× futtatva`, the two-tap delete, real-mode skeleton + empty state), plus the runs-only guard in `pages/MesocycleLibraryPage.test.tsx` (no template cards / no dashed CTA, the `Sablonok · n` hub-mosaic tile → `/train/templates`, `Sablonná` → the new editor), `ArchivedMesoCard.test.tsx`'s `Sablonná` + both-actions-hidden cases, `MesoReportPage.test.tsx`'s save-as-template case, and `train.nav.test.tsx`'s `Sablonok` navigation case
- BE: `*IT.java` (service + contract) incl. `WorkoutTodayProgressionIT` + the pure `ProgressionDeciderTest` (`mezo-5pfe`) + `support/populator/{TrainPopulator,RunningPopulator}.java`; Progressive Overload Plan 2 (`mezo-hi9m`): the pure `VolumeDeciderTest`, `VolumeProgressionServiceIT`, `VolumePropertiesIT`, `VolumeEffectiveSetsIT` + `VolumeEffectiveSetsSwitchOffIT` (the effective-set distribution, on/off), `VolumeArcContractIT`; **`MesoTemplateIT`** (`mezo-meyc.1`) — template CRUD/ownership, stamp correctness (days/exercises/volume rows copied), single-active invariant, planned start, legacy rerun materialization, the jsonb round-trip of `meso_template`'s `Map<String, record>` `volumePerMuscle` column — + `support/populator/MesoTemplatePopulator.java`; **`MesocycleCloseReportIT`** (`mezo-meyc.2`, 9 tests, mutation-checked) — report math (adherence/strength/records) on populated data, idempotent close, self-eval capture + fill-on-reclose, legacy backfill via regenerate, the `TRAIN_MESO_REPORT_NOT_FOUND`/`TRAIN_MESO_NOT_CLOSED` error paths — over the extended `TrainPopulator`; **`MesocycleCloseReportIT` grew the `MesocycleClosed` publish/no-publish matrix** (`mezo-meyc.3`, `@RecordApplicationEvents`: real close publishes, an idempotent re-close and the fill-if-null self-eval branch do NOT, every accepted regenerate does) and runs with **`mezo.feature.companion.enabled=false`** so the companion's async review listener cannot race its `context`-null / status-`pending` assertions; **`MesoReviewSwitchOffIT`** (`mezo-meyc.3`, own `@TestPropertySource` context) covers `aiEvalEnabled: false` + "context written, AI half left pending". The generator itself is tested from the companion side — `feature/companion/MesoReviewGeneratorIT` ([`companion.md`](companion.md) §10). **Plan generator (mesocycle wizard redesign):** pure `MesoPlanSkeletonTest`/`MesoPlanFillerTest`/`MesoPlanMergerTest` (`service/`), `MesoPlanPropertiesBindingIT` (`config/`), `MesoStartTierSeedIT`, `MesoPlanGenerateContractIT`, `MesoPlanGenerateAiIT` (`companion-fake` profile — exercises the `MesoPlanLlmAdapter` end-to-end through the port, there is no adapter-only unit test) — see §4 `#### Plan generator` and §8 above for detail

**Related docs** — specs/plans in `docs/superpowers/specs|plans/` (dated `2026-06-10`…`2026-06-14`, plus the `2026-07-25` Progressive Overload design spec + the Plan 1 (`2026-07-25-progressive-overload-p1-intensity-signal.md`, `mezo-5pfe`) and Plan 2 (`2026-07-25-progressive-overload-p2-volume-overview.md`, `mezo-hi9m`) implementation plans, and the `2026-08-01-set-budget-unified-editor-design.md` set-budget + unified-editor design (`mezo-7rdg`, §2/§4 above)); house standards in `docs/references/*.md`. **Mesocycle history — epic COMPLETE** (template/run split S1 + deterministic close report S2 + cross-domain context/AI evaluation S3 + two-run compare view S4, `mezo-meyc`, all four slices shipped): design spec [`2026-08-16-mesocycle-history-design.md`](../superpowers/specs/2026-08-16-mesocycle-history-design.md), ADR [0027](../decisions/0027-mesocycle-template-run-history.md).
